(4) The court may release from service of punishment a person who has committed a criminal violation or a less serious crime due to alcohol, narcotic, psychotropic or toxic substance addiction, if that person has agreed to undergo treatment for alcohol, narcotic, psychotropic or toxic substance addiction. The punishment shall be enforced if the person has not commenced treatment within the time specified by the court or has subsequently avoided treatment.

(5) If a person who has been convicted of a criminal offence or for whom a punishment has been imposed by a prosecutor's penal order, after the pronouncement of the judgment or after the prosecutor has issued the penal order, has contracted a mental illness that has deprived them of the ability to understand their actions or to control them, the court shall release that person from service of punishment. Compulsory measures of a medical nature may be imposed on them in accordance with the conditions provided for in this law.

(6) If a person who has been convicted of a criminal offence or for whom a punishment has been imposed by a prosecutor's penal order suffers from another serious, incurable illness, the court may release that person from service of punishment.

(With amendments made by the laws of 27.05.2004., 13.12.2007. and 13.12.2012., which came into force on 01.04.2013.)

### 60.pants. Reduction of Punishment in Exceptional Cases

If the convicted person has helped to uncover a crime committed by another person that is equally serious, more serious or more dangerous than the criminal offence committed by them, and that other person has been brought to criminal liability for that crime, the court may reduce the punishment imposed by the judgment, and may replace the imposed life imprisonment with deprivation of liberty for twenty years.

(In the version of the law of 07.04.2022., which came into force on 04.05.2022.)

### 61.pants. Conditional Early Release from Punishment

(1) A person sentenced to imprisonment, excluding short-term imprisonment, may be conditionally released early from serving the principal punishment if there is reason to believe that after release they will be able to integrate into society without committing a criminal offense.

(2) Taking into account the personality and conduct of the convicted person during the serving of the sentence, conditional early release from punishment may be determined if:

- 1) the convicted person has achieved a certain result of resocialization;
- 2) the convicted person has, according to their means, voluntarily compensated for the harm caused by the criminal offense;
- 3) the convicted person has opportunities after release to obtain means of subsistence by lawful means;
- 4) the time specified in the law regulating the execution of criminal sentences has elapsed after the imposition of a penalty for violation of the sentence serving regime, and there are no valid penalties for administrative violations committed during the execution of the imprisonment sentence;
- 5) the convicted person is addressing and is ready to continue addressing their psychological problems that caused or could cause the commission of a criminal offense;
- 6) the convicted person has agreed to receive treatment for alcohol, narcotic, psychotropic or toxic substance addiction if the criminal offense was committed due to alcohol, narcotic, psychotropic or toxic substance addiction.
  - (21) When conditionally releasing early from punishment, electronic monitoring may be applied to the convicted person, observing the following conditions:
- 1) the convicted person consents to electronic monitoring;
- 2) the implementation of electronic monitoring is possible at the convicted person's place of residence;
- 3) the application of electronic monitoring will promote the convicted person's integration into society.
  - (3) Conditional early release from punishment may be initiated when the convicted person has actually served:
- 1) not less than half of the imposed sentence for a less serious crime committed;
- 2) not less than two thirds of the imposed sentence if it was adjudged for a serious crime, as well as if the convicted person is a person who has previously been sentenced to imprisonment for an intentional crime and the criminal liability for this crime has not been removed or expunged;
- 3) not less than three quarters of the imposed sentence if it was adjudged for a particularly serious crime, as well as if the convicted person is a person who has previously been conditionally released early from punishment and has committed a new intentional crime during the unserved part of the sentence;
- 4) twenty-five years of the imprisonment sentence if the convicted person is a person who has been sentenced to life imprisonment.
  - (31) If, in accordance with what is specified in paragraph (21) of this Article, the application of electronic monitoring is possible, conditional early release from punishment with the determination of electronic monitoring may be initiated when the convicted person has actually served:
- 1) not less than one third of the imposed sentence for a less serious crime;
- 2) not less than half of the imposed sentence if it was adjudged for a serious crime, as well as if the convicted person is a person who has previously been sentenced to imprisonment for an intentional crime and the criminal liability for this crime has not been removed or expunged;
- 3) not less than two thirds of the imposed sentence if it was adjudged for a particularly serious crime, as well as if the convicted person is a person who has previously been conditionally released early from punishment and has committed a new intentional crime during the unserved part of the sentence;
- 4) not less than twenty-four years of the imprisonment sentence if the convicted person is a person who has been sentenced to life imprisonment.
  - (4) The person conditionally released early during the unserved part of the sentence fulfills the obligations provided for in the law regulating the execution of criminal sentences and determined by the sentence execution institution. If the person conditionally released early without justified reason fails to fulfill the mentioned obligations, the court, based on the submission of the sentence execution institution, may adopt a decision on the execution of the unserved part of the sentence.
  - (41) If the person conditionally released early, to whom electronic monitoring has been applied, without justified reason fails to fulfill the obligations related to electronic monitoring specified in the law regulating the execution of criminal sentences, revokes their consent to electronic monitoring, or the circumstances in which they live no longer allow the implementation of electronic monitoring, the court, based on the submission of the sentence execution institution, adopts a decision on the execution of the unserved part of the sentence.
  - (42) If the person conditionally released early, to whom electronic monitoring has been applied, has exemplarily fulfilled the obligations provided for in the law regulating the execution of criminal sentences and determined by the sentence execution institution, and the time limit specified in paragraph three of Article 61.panta of this law has occurred, in accordance with which conditional early release from punishment without the determination of electronic monitoring is possible, the court, based on the submission of the sentence execution institution, may adopt a decision on the cancellation of electronic monitoring. If the court adopts a decision to cancel electronic monitoring, the convicted person is supervised in accordance with the supervision regulations for convicted persons conditionally released early from serving the sentence provided for in the regulatory acts regulating the execution of criminal sentences.
  - (43) A person sentenced to life who has been conditionally released early from serving the sentence is, after release, under the supervision of the sentence execution institution for life and fulfills the obligations provided for in the law regulating the execution of criminal sentences and determined by the sentence execution institution. If a person sentenced to life who has been conditionally released early from serving the sentence without justified reason fails to fulfill the obligations provided for in the law regulating the execution of criminal sentences and determined by the sentence execution institution, the court, based on the submission of the sentence execution institution, may adopt a decision on the replacement of the early supervision period with imprisonment for life (life imprisonment).
  - (44) If a person sentenced to life who has been conditionally released early from serving the sentence and to whom electronic monitoring has been applied, without justified reason fails to fulfill the obligations related to electronic monitoring specified in the law regulating the execution of criminal sentences, revokes their consent to electronic monitoring, or if the circumstances in which they live no longer allow the implementation of electronic monitoring, the court, based on the submission of the sentence execution institution, adopts a decision on the replacement of the early supervision period with imprisonment for life (life imprisonment).
  - (5) If a person who had been granted conditional early release commits a new criminal offense during the unserved part of the sentence, the court determines the punishment in accordance with the provisions specified in Articles 51. and 52. of this law.
  - (6) Conditional early release from punishment is not applied if it was adjudged to an adult person for a particularly serious crime committed against a person who has not reached the age of sixteen and is related to sexual violence. sexual violence.

(With amendments made by laws of 27.05.2004., 21.06.2007., 30.10.2008., 08.07.2011., 01.12.2011., 13.12.2012., 15.05.2014., 16.10.2014., 11.06.2020. and 17.12.2020., which enter into force on 01.01.2022.)

61.1 pants. Electronic Monitoring

(1) Electronic monitoring is intensive control measures determined by the court to restrict the free movement of the convicted person. To implement electronic monitoring, an electronic device is attached to the convicted person's body, which allows controlling their presence at a specified place and time.

(2) The court determines electronic monitoring for a period from one month to twelve months.

(3) The electronic monitoring period begins on the day when the electronic device is attached to the convicted person's body. Upon expiry of the electronic monitoring period determined by the court, the convicted person is supervised in accordance with the supervision regulations for convicted persons conditionally released early from serving the sentence provided for in the regulatory acts regulating the execution of criminal sentences, if the convicted person's unserved sentence time has not yet ended. (In the wording of the law of 16.10.2014., which enters into force on 01.02.2015. The Article enters into force on 01.07.2015. See transitional provision 19.punktu)


This excerpt sits under: Izdevējs: Saeima Publicēts: > VI nodaļa

### 62.pants. Limitation Period for Execution of a Conviction Judgment

(1) A conviction judgment and prosecutor's order of punishment are not enforceable if from the day it entered into legal force, its execution has not been commenced within the following periods:

- 1) two years — if short-term deprivation of liberty, community service or fine has been imposed;
- 11) two years after serving the deprivation of liberty sentence — if the sentence — community service — is enforceable independently in cases provided for in Section 52, Part 2.1 of this Law;
- 2) (deleted by the law of 06.06.2019.);
- 21) three years — if probation supervision has been imposed;
- 3) five years — if deprivation of liberty for a period not exceeding five years has been imposed;
- 4) ten years — if deprivation of liberty for a period not exceeding ten years has been imposed;
- 5) fifteen years — if a more severe punishment than deprivation of liberty for ten years has been imposed.
  - (2) The limitation period is interrupted if the convicted person evades serving the sentence or before the end of the limitation period commits a new criminal offense for which the court has imposed deprivation of liberty for a period of not less than one year. If a new criminal offense is committed, the limitation period starts to run from the moment of its commission, but if the convicted person has evaded serving the sentence — from the moment when he/she arrives to serve the sentence, or from the moment when the convicted person who has been hiding is detained. However, the conviction judgment is not enforceable if fifteen years have passed from the time it entered into force and the limitation period has not been interrupted by a new criminal offense. The question of whether the convicted person has evaded serving the sentence shall be decided by the court.
  - (3) The question of the limitation period for a person sentenced to life imprisonment shall be decided by the court.

(With amendments made by the laws of 12.02.2004., 13.12.2007., 01.12.2011., 13.12.2012., 14.03.2013., 25.09.2014., 29.10.2015., 06.06.2019. and 17.12.2020., which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

### 63.pants. Expungement and Removal of Criminal Record

(1) A criminal record is the legal consequence of conviction or imposition of punishment on a person who has committed a criminal offense, which is in force during the period of serving the sentence determined in the court judgment or prosecutor's order of punishment, as well as thereafter until the expungement or removal of the criminal record in the manner prescribed by law.

(2) A person is considered convicted from the moment the conviction judgment enters into force or from the day the prosecutor's order of punishment enters into force.

(3) The following shall be recognized as not having a criminal record:

- 1) persons who have been released from punishment by court judgment or acquitted;
- 2) persons for whom the limitation period for execution of a conviction judgment provided for by law has occurred;
- 3) conditionally convicted persons — one year after the end of the probation period, but in the case of application of additional punishment
  - one year after the end of the period of serving the additional punishment. If the imposed sentence is executed for a conditionally convicted person, the term of expungement of the criminal record is calculated taking into account the actually served sentence;
- 4) after one year — persons who have served short-term deprivation of liberty, community service or fine;
- 5) after two years — persons who have served a sentence of deprivation of liberty not exceeding three years, or probation supervision;
- 6) after five years — persons who have served a sentence of deprivation of liberty exceeding three years but not exceeding five years;
- 7) after eight years — persons who have served a sentence of deprivation of liberty exceeding five years but not exceeding ten years;
- 8) after ten years — persons who have served a sentence of deprivation of liberty exceeding ten years.
  - (4) The term of expungement of the criminal record is counted from the day the person has fully served the principal and additional punishment.

The execution of the additional punishment — confiscation of property — does not affect the calculation of the term of expungement of the criminal record.

(5) If a person has been released from punishment before the term in the manner prescribed by law, the term of expungement of the criminal record is counted from the day when he/she was released from serving the sentence, taking into account the time of actual serving of the sentence.

(6) (Deleted by the law of 13.12.2012.)

(7) If a person convicted with deprivation of liberty has proved his/her reformation by exemplary behavior and honest attitude toward work after serving the sentence, the court may remove his/her criminal record before the expiration of the terms specified in this Section.

(8) A criminal record may be removed by way of pardon or amnesty.

(9) Expungement and removal of a criminal record annuls all criminal law consequences of the committed criminal offense, except those provided for by law if a new criminal offense is committed before the expungement or removal of the criminal record.

(With amendments made by the laws of 17.10.2002., 12.02.2004., 28.09.2005., 08.11.2007., 21.10.2010., 13.12.2012., 15.05.2014., 25.09.2014., 10.03.2016., 08.06.2017. and 17.12.2020., which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

## Chapter VII

Peculiarities of Criminal Liability of a Minor

(Chapter title amended by the law of 17.12.2020., which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

### 64.pants. Liability of a Minor

(1) The provisions of this Chapter apply to a person who has not reached the age of eighteen before committing a criminal offense.

(2) Other provisions of the General Part of this Law apply to minors unless otherwise provided in this Chapter.

(3) Taking into account the objective of punishment provided for in the second part of Section 35 of this Law, with respect to a minor, his/her resocialization is primary.

(As worded by the law of 17.12.2020., which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

### 65.pants. Types of Punishments for a Minor

(1) One of the following principal punishments may be applied to a minor:

- 1) deprivation of liberty;
- 2) probation supervision;
- 3) community service;
- 4) fine.
  - (2) The following additional punishments may be applied to a minor without a principal punishment:
- 1) probation supervision;
- 2) restriction of rights;
- 3) expulsion from the Republic of Latvia.
  - (3) A prosecutor, when drawing up an order of punishment, may apply probation supervision, community service or fine, as well as additional punishment — restriction of rights.

(As worded by the law of 17.12.2020., which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

### 66.pants. Application of Educational Coercive Measures to Minors

(1) Taking into account the special circumstances of the commission of the criminal offense and the information obtained about the guilty person's personality that mitigates their liability, the court may release the minor from the imposed punishment by applying the educational coercive measures provided for by law.

(2) The punishment shall be executed if the minor who has been released from it fails to fulfill the obligations imposed by the court during the period of the imposed punishment.

(Amended by the Law of 31.10.2002, which entered into force on 01.01.2003.)

66.1 pants. Application of Deprivation of Liberty

(1) Deprivation of liberty is the placement of a minor in an educational institution and includes a set of social behavior correction and social rehabilitation measures and behavioral rules appropriate to the minor's age, psychological characteristics and level of development, as provided for in the law regulating the execution of criminal punishments.

(2) The period of deprivation of liberty may not exceed ten years — for particularly serious crimes; five years — for serious crimes involving violence or the threat of violence or causing serious consequences; two years — for other serious crimes.

(3) Deprivation of liberty shall not be applied for criminal violations and less serious crimes.

(4) If a person has committed a criminal offense before reaching the age of eighteen for which the minimum limit of the applicable punishment of deprivation of liberty is specified in the sanction of the relevant article of the Special Part of this Law, the court may impose a punishment that is lower than this minimum limit, even in cases where it has found that the criminal offense was committed under aggravating circumstances.

(5) Deprivation of liberty is always applied to a minor together with an additional punishment — probation supervision, even in cases where this additional punishment is not provided for in the sanction of the relevant article of the Special Part of this Law.

(Wording of the Law of 17.12.2020, which entered into force on 01.01.2022.)

66.2 pants. Application of Probation Supervision

(1) In accordance with the harm of the criminal offense, the court may impose probation supervision as the main punishment on a minor for a period of three to six years also for the commission of a serious crime for which this Law provides for deprivation of liberty for a period longer than five years, and for the commission of a particularly serious crime.

(2) The court, based on a submission from the punishment execution institution, may decide that a minor who has been assigned probation supervision as a punishment by a court judgment or prosecutor's order regarding punishment shall be placed in a social correction educational institution for a period of up to three years, but not less than one year, taking into account the unfavorable social environment in which the minor is located, a possible threat to the minor's health or life, or other reasons that hinder the execution of probation supervision.

(3) If a minor who has been assigned probation supervision as a punishment by a court judgment or prosecutor's order regarding punishment, or whose community service has been replaced with probation supervision in the manner specified in this Law, fails to fulfill the obligations provided for in the law regulating the execution of criminal punishments or established by the punishment execution institution without justifiable reason, the court, based on a submission from the punishment execution institution, may replace the unserved period of punishment with deprivation of liberty, counting two days of probation supervision as one day of deprivation of liberty.

(Wording of the Law of 17.12.2020, which entered into force on 01.01.2022.)

66.3 pants. Replacement of Community Service

If a minor who has been sentenced to community service, or a minor for whom community service has been specified by a prosecutor's order regarding punishment, fails to perform community service without justifiable reason, the court, based on a submission from the punishment execution institution, shall replace the unserved punishment with probation supervision for one year.

(Wording of the Law of 17.12.2020, which entered into force on 01.01.2022.)

66.4 pants. Application of Fine

(1) A fine may be applied only to those minors who have their own income. A fine for minors shall be applied in the amount of one to fifty minimum monthly wages established in the Republic of Latvia.

(2) A fine not paid by a minor within the specified time shall be replaced with community service, counting one minimum monthly wage as forty hours of community service, but not more than two hundred and eighty hours.

(Wording of the Law of 17.12.2020 as amended by the Law of 07.04.2022, which entered into force on 04.05.2022.)

66.5 pants. Other Issues Related to the Imposition of Punishment

(1) For a person who has committed a criminal offense before reaching the age of eighteen, the punishment for several criminal offenses or under several judgments shall be determined in accordance with the provisions of Articles 50 and 51 of this Law, taking into account that the total period of deprivation of liberty for several criminal offenses may not exceed twelve years and six months, and under several judgments — fifteen years.

(2) Conditional early release from punishment may be initiated if the person has served not less than half of the imposed period of deprivation of liberty.

(3) Conditional early release from punishment with the establishment of electronic monitoring may be initiated if the person has served not less than one third of the imposed period of deprivation of liberty.

(4) A person who has committed a criminal violation or a less serious crime before reaching the age of eighteen shall be deemed not convicted after serving the punishment.

(Wording of the Law of 17.12.2020, which entered into force on 01.01.2022.)

### 67.pants. Conditional Sentencing of a Minor

(Deleted by the Law of 21.05.2009, which entered into force on 01.07.2009.)

## VIII nodaļa

Coercive Measures of a Medical Nature

### 68.pants. Coercive Measures of a Medical Nature

(1) The following coercive measures of a medical nature may be imposed on persons who have committed acts provided for in this Law but suffer from mental disorders and have been recognized as not accountable or having limited accountability:

- 1) outpatient treatment in a medical institution;
- 2) treatment in a general-type psychiatric hospital (department);
- 3) treatment in a specialized psychiatric hospital (department) with security.
  - (2) If the person referred to in paragraph one of this Article is not dangerous to society due to the nature of the act committed and their mental condition, the court may place them in the care of relatives or other persons who provide care for patients.
  - (3) Persons referred to in paragraph one of this Article who have been recognized as having limited accountability may also be assigned treatment in places of deprivation of liberty appropriate for this purpose.

(Amended by the Law of 15.05.2014, which entered into force on 14.06.2014.)

### 69.pants. Determination of Compulsory Measures of a Medical Nature for Persons in a State of Insanity

(1) The court may impose the compulsory measures of a medical nature provided for in this Law on persons who have committed the offences provided for in this Law while in a state of insanity, or who, after committing the offence or after the pronouncement of the judgment, have fallen ill with a mental illness that has deprived them of the ability to understand their actions or control them, if such persons are dangerous to society due to the nature of the offence committed and their mental condition.

(2) Compulsory treatment and the type of medical institution shall be determined by the court depending on the mental illness with which the person in question has fallen ill and the nature of the offence committed by that person. When determining treatment in a psychiatric hospital (unit), the type thereof shall be chosen by the medical institution.

(3) A person who, after committing a criminal offence or after the pronouncement of a judgment, has fallen ill with a mental illness that has deprived them of the ability to understand their actions or control them, may be sentenced by the court after recovery, if the limitation period has not expired or there is no other basis for their release from criminal liability and punishment.

(4) The court shall terminate the application of a compulsory measure of a medical nature or modify it to a less restrictive measure if the person in question has recovered or their health condition has improved, or it is established that the health condition of that person has otherwise changed to such an extent that they are no longer dangerous to society.

(41) The court may modify a compulsory measure of a medical nature to a more restrictive measure if the person fails to comply with the compulsory measure of a medical nature imposed on them.

(5) If such a person is sentenced after recovery, the time during which compulsory measures of a medical nature have been applied shall be counted towards the term of the sentence.

(With amendments made by the Laws of 15.05.2014 and 25.09.2014, which enter into force on 29.10.2014.)

69.1 pants. Determination of a Compulsory Measure of a Medical Nature Following Several Decisions

If a person for whom a compulsory measure of a medical nature has been determined commits a new criminal offence after such determination, for which a compulsory measure of a medical nature is determined, the court shall add the previously determined compulsory measure of a medical nature to the compulsory measure of a medical nature determined in the new decision, incorporating the less restrictive compulsory measure of a medical nature into the more restrictive measure.

(Wording of the Law of 15.05.2014, which enters into force on 14.06.2014.)

### 70.pants. Determination of Compulsory Measures of a Medical Nature for Persons in a State of Limited

Accountability

Compulsory measures of a medical nature may also be determined for persons who have committed criminal offences while in a state of limited accountability. If such a person is convicted with deprivation of liberty, treatment shall take place in places of deprivation of liberty appropriate for them. If such a person is convicted without deprivation of liberty, the court shall impose on them an obligation to receive treatment in a psychiatric medical institution at their place of residence.

## Chapter VIII

Coercive measures applicable to legal entities

(Chapter in the wording of the law of 05.05.2005., which comes into force 01.10.2005.)

Article 70.1. Grounds for application of coercive measure to a legal entity

For a criminal offence provided for in the Special Part of this Law, a coercive measure may be applied to a private law legal entity, including a state or local government capital company, as well as a general partnership, by a court or, in cases provided for by law, by a prosecutor, if the offence has been committed in the interests of the legal entity, for the benefit of this entity or as a result of its inadequate supervision or control, by a natural person acting individually or as a member of a collegial institution of the respective legal entity:

- 1) on the basis of the right to represent the legal entity or act on its behalf;
- 2) on the basis of the right to take decisions in the name of the legal entity;
- 3) exercising control within the framework of the legal entity.

(In the wording of the law of 14.03.2013., which comes into force 01.04.2013.)

Article 70.2. Types of coercive measures applicable to legal entities

(1) The following coercive measures may be imposed on a legal entity:

- 1) liquidation;
- 2) restriction of rights;
- 3) confiscation of property;
- 4) pecuniary exaction.
  - (2) One or more of the coercive measures provided for in paragraph one of this Article may be applied to a legal entity. When applying liquidation, other coercive measures are not imposed.
  - (3) The procedure for execution of coercive measures shall be determined in accordance with law.
  - (4) For a criminal violation, less serious crime or serious crime provided for in the Special Part of this Law, for which imprisonment of up to five years is prescribed, a prosecutor may impose on a legal entity, by drawing up a prescription on coercive measure, pecuniary exaction or restriction of rights as a coercive measure.

(In the wording of the law of 14.03.2013. with amendments made by the law of 10.03.2016., which comes into force 07.04.2016.)

Article 70.3. Liquidation

(1) Liquidation is the forced termination of the activities of a legal entity.

(2) A legal entity shall be liquidated only in those cases where the legal entity has been specially established for the commission of a criminal offence or where a serious or particularly serious crime has been committed.

(3) Upon liquidation of a legal entity, all property owned by it shall be alienated without compensation into state ownership.

(With amendments made by the law of 14.03.2013., which comes into force 01.04.2013.)

Article 70.4. Restriction of rights

(1) Restriction of rights is the withdrawal of certain rights or permits or the imposition of a prohibition that does not allow the legal entity to use certain rights, receive state support or assistance, participate in state or local government procurement procedures, carry out a certain type of activity for a period from one year to ten years.

(2) (Deleted by the law of 10.03.2016.)

(In the wording of the law of 14.03.2013. with amendments made by the law of 10.03.2016., which comes into force 07.04.2016.)

Article 70.5. Confiscation of property

(1) Confiscation of property is the forced alienation without compensation into state ownership of property owned by a legal entity.

(2) The court, when imposing confiscation of property, specifically indicates which property is to be confiscated.

(3) (Deleted by the law of 14.03.2013.)

(4) Property owned by the legal entity that has been transferred to another person may also be confiscated.

(With amendments made by the laws of 13.12.2012. and 14.03.2013., which comes into force 01.04.2013.)

Article 70.6. Pecuniary exaction

(1) Pecuniary exaction is a sum of money that the court or prosecutor imposes to be paid in favour of the state within 30 days in the amount specified in this Article.

(11) Pecuniary exaction, according to the harmfulness of the criminal offence and the financial condition of the legal entity, shall be imposed:

- 1) for a criminal violation — in the amount of five to ten thousand minimum monthly wages established in the Republic of Latvia;
- 2) for a less serious crime — in the amount of ten to fifty thousand minimum monthly wages established in the Republic of Latvia;
- 3) for a serious crime — in the amount of twenty to seventy-five thousand minimum monthly wages established in the Republic of Latvia;
- 4) for a particularly serious crime — in the amount of thirty to one hundred thousand minimum monthly wages established in the Republic of Latvia.
  - (12) At the time of making the decision, the amount of pecuniary exaction shall be indicated in the decision in the monetary units of the Republic of Latvia.
  - (2) Pecuniary exaction imposed on a legal entity shall be paid from the funds of this entity.
  - (3) Payment of the pecuniary exaction may be divided into instalments or postponed by the court or prosecutor respectively for a period not exceeding one year from the day the decision or prescription on coercive measure has entered into legal force.
  - (4) If the pecuniary exaction is not paid, the coercive measure shall be executed by compulsory procedure.

(In the wording of the law of 14.03.2013. with amendments made by the laws of 29.10.2015. and 07.04.2022., which comes into force

Article 70.7. Compensation for harm

(Deleted by the law of 14.03.2013., which comes into force 01.04.2013.)

Article 70.8. Conditions for application of coercive measures to a legal entity

(1) When determining the type of coercive measure, the nature of the criminal offence, the harm caused and whether a coercive measure has been previously applied to the legal entity shall be taken into account.

(2) When determining the extent of a coercive measure, the following conditions shall be observed:

- 1) the actual conduct of the legal entity;
- 2) the nature and consequences of the activities of the legal entity;
- 3) measures taken by the legal entity to prevent the commission of a criminal offence;
- 4) the size, type of occupation and financial condition of the legal entity;
- 5) measures taken by the legal entity to compensate for the loss caused or prevent the harm caused;
- 6) whether the legal entity has reconciled with the victim.
  - (3) If several coercive measures have been applied to a legal entity in accordance with paragraph two of Article 70.2 of this Law, then each of them shall be executed independently.
  - (4) In a criminal case concerning several independent criminal offences, the court, when rendering a judgment, or the prosecutor, when drawing up a prescription on coercive measure, shall impose on the legal entity a coercive measure separately for each criminal offence. In such case, the final set of coercive measures to be applied shall be determined according to the aggregate of criminal offences in the following order:
- 1) by subsuming the lighter pecuniary exaction into the more severe one or by adding them fully or partially;
- 2) by fully adding the restrictions of rights;
- 3) by fully adding the confiscations of property.
  - (5) If, after the judgment or prosecutor's prescription on coercive measure has entered into force, but before the complete execution of the coercive measure, the legal entity is involved in a new criminal proceeding on application of a coercive measure, the court shall add to the coercive measure imposed in the new judgment the coercive measure that has not been executed under the previous judgment or prosecutor's prescription on coercive measure, in the following order:
- 1) by fully or partially adding the pecuniary exactions;
- 2) by fully adding the restrictions of rights;
- 3) by fully adding the confiscations of property.
  - (6) If liquidation has been applied, then in the cases referred to in paragraphs four and five of this Article, other coercive measures shall be subsumed into liquidation.
  - (7) In the cases referred to in paragraphs four and five of this Article, the total amount or time of the coercive measure may exceed the maximum amount or time of the coercive measure provided for the most serious of the criminal offences committed, but not by more than half of the maximum amount or time of the coercive measure provided for the most serious of the criminal offences committed.

(In the wording of the law of 14.03.2013. with amendments made by the laws of 29.10.2015., 11.06.2020. and 07.04.2022., which comes into force 04.05.2022.)

Article 70.9. Calculation of limitation period

(1) In proceedings on application of coercive measure, the limitation period shall be calculated from the day the criminal offence was committed until the moment when the legal entity is notified of the initiation of proceedings on application of coercive measure, observing the time limits specified in points 2-6 of paragraph one of Article 56 and in Article 57 of this Law.

(2) A judgment or prosecutor's prescription on application of coercive measure shall not be enforceable if five years have passed from the day it entered into legal force.

(In the wording of the law of 15.05.2014., which comes into force 14.06.2014.)

## Chapter VIII

Special Confiscation of Property

(Chapter in the wording of the law of 22.06.2017, which enters into force on 01.08.2017. See point 21 of transitional provisions)

Article 70.10. Concept of Special Confiscation of Property

Special confiscation of property is the compulsory alienation without compensation into state ownership of criminally obtained property or an object used for committing a criminal offense, or property related to a criminal offense. Special confiscation of property is not a criminal punishment.

(In the wording of the law of 22.06.2017, which enters into force on 01.08.2017.)

Article 70.11. Confiscation of Criminally Obtained Property

(1) Criminally obtained property is any economic benefit that has come into the ownership or possession of a person directly or indirectly as a result of committing a criminal offense.

(11) Indirectly criminally obtained property is any economic benefit that has come into the ownership or possession of a person directly as a result of further use of criminally obtained property, including as a result of reinvestment or conversion, or funds that a person has obtained from the sale of such property, as well as the fruits and profits obtained.

(2) If the value of property is not commensurate with a person's lawful income and the person does not prove that the property was obtained lawfully, property belonging to a person who may also be recognized as criminally obtained property:

- 1) has committed a crime that by its nature is directed at obtaining material or other benefit, regardless of whether material or other benefit has been obtained as a result of the crime;
- 2) is a member of an organized group;
- 3) has committed a crime related to terrorism.
  - (3) Property that is in the possession of another such person who maintains permanent family, economic or other property relations with the person referred to in paragraph two of this Article may also be recognized as criminally obtained property, if the value of the property in the possession of that person is not commensurate with its lawful income and if the person does not prove that the property was obtained lawfully.
  - (4) Criminally obtained property shall be confiscated if it is not to be returned to the owner or lawful possessor.

(In the wording of the law of 22.06.2017, with amendments made by the law of 11.06.2020 and 07.04.2022, which enters into force

Article 70.12. Confiscation of Objects Used for Committing a Criminal Offense

(1) An object used for committing a criminal offense is tools and means that were intended or used for committing a criminal offense.

(2) Objects used for committing a criminal offense shall be confiscated.

(In the wording of the law of 22.06.2017, which enters into force on 01.08.2017.)

Article 70.13. Confiscation of Property Related to a Criminal Offense

(1) Property related to a criminal offense is items prohibited from circulation or items whose origin or ownership in the particular criminal case has not been established, or property belonging to a person who has committed criminal offenses that, in connection with the criminal offense committed, is not to be left in the person's ownership.

(2) Property related to a criminal offense shall be confiscated.

(3) Animals may be confiscated if, in connection with the criminal offense committed, they are not to be left in the ownership of a person who has committed criminal offenses.

(4) A vehicle belonging to a person who has committed criminal offenses may be subject to confiscation if a criminal offense against traffic safety has been committed.

(5) A vehicle belonging to a person who has committed criminal offenses, with which it has committed the criminal offense provided for in Article 262 or 262.1 of this Law, shall be subject to confiscation.

(In the wording of the law of 22.06.2017, with amendments made by the law of 27.10.2022, which enters into force on 25.11.2022.)

Article 70.14. Replacement of Property to be Confiscated

(1) If an object used for committing a criminal offense belongs to another person, the value of the object used for committing a criminal offense may be recovered.

(11) If the criminal offense provided for in Article 262 or 262.1 of this Law has been committed with a vehicle belonging to another person, the full or partial value of this vehicle shall be recovered from the person who has committed the criminal offenses.

(2) If criminally obtained property has been alienated, destroyed, concealed or disguised and it cannot be confiscated, the value of the property to be confiscated may be recovered.

(3) If confiscation of criminally obtained property has been applied to a person, the property to be confiscated may be replaced with financial means in the value of this property. Property that has historical, artistic or scientific value may not be replaced.

(4) If it is not possible to confiscate criminally obtained property because it has been alienated, destroyed, concealed or disguised, and the person who has committed the criminal offenses has no other property on which to enforce recovery, the following may be confiscated:

- 1) property that a person, after the commencement of a criminal offense, has alienated to another person free of charge or for a value that is significantly lower or significantly higher than the market value;
- 2) joint property of the person who has committed a criminal offense and his or her spouse, unless separation of spouses' property has been established at least one year before the commencement of the criminal offense;
- 3) property belonging to another person with whom the person who has committed a criminal offense has a joint (undivided) household, if this property was obtained after the commencement of the criminal offense.

(In the wording of the law of 22.06.2017, with amendments made by the law of 27.10.2022, which enters into force on 25.11.2022.)

SPECIAL PART

## Chapter IX

Crimes Against Humanity, Peace, War Crimes, Genocide

### Article 71. Genocide

For genocide, that is, for intentional actions aimed at completely or partially destroying any national, ethnic, racial or religious group of people as such, by killing members of such a group, causing them life-threatening or health-threatening bodily injuries or bringing them to mental illness, intentionally creating living conditions for them that completely or partially physically destroy these people, using means aimed at preventing the birth of children in such a group, or transferring children by force from one group of people to another, — shall be punished by life imprisonment or deprivation of liberty for a term of three to twenty years.

(With amendments made by the law of 21.05.2009, which enters into force on 01.07.2009.)

(With amendments made by the law of 21.05.2009, which enters into force on 01.07.2009.)

Article 71.1. Incitement to Genocide

For public incitement to genocide — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to eight years.

(In the wording of the law of 28.04.2005, which enters into force on 01.06.2005.)

Article 71.2. Crimes Against Humanity

For a crime against humanity, that is, for an act committed as part of a widespread or systematic attack against civilians and manifested as murder, extermination, enslavement, deportation or forced displacement, unlawful deprivation or restriction of liberty, torture, rape, involvement of a person in sexual slavery, forcing to engage in prostitution, forced insemination or sterilization or other sexual violence of a similar degree of gravity, apartheid, persecution of any group of people or association based on political, racial, national, ethnic, cultural, religious or gender affiliation or other reasons recognized as inadmissible in international law, in connection with any act specified in this Article, or genocide, or a war crime, or other acts provided for in international law binding on the Republic of Latvia that cause serious physical or mental suffering, — shall be punished by life imprisonment or deprivation of liberty for a term of three to twenty years.

(In the wording of the law of 21.05.2009, which enters into force on 01.07.2009.)

### Article 72. Crimes Against Peace

For crimes committed against peace, that is, for planning, preparing, initiating, participating in aggression, for waging aggressive war in violation of international treaties binding on the Republic of Latvia, for participating in a conspiracy with the intent to commit the crimes specified in this Article — shall be punished by life imprisonment or deprivation of liberty for a term of three to twenty years.

(With amendments made by the law of 16.10.2014, which enters into force on 01.02.2015.)

### 73.pants.

Production, Storage, Transportation, Use and Proliferation of Weapons of Mass Destruction

For the production, storage, transportation, use or proliferation of nuclear, chemical, biological, bacteriological, toxic or other weapons of mass destruction — the applicable sentence is life imprisonment or deprivation of liberty for a term of three to twenty years.

(With amendments made by the Law of 19.11.2009, which entered into force on 23.12.2009.)

73.1 pants. Financing of Production, Storage, Transportation, Use and Proliferation of Weapons of Mass Destruction

(1) For the financing of production, storage, transportation, use or proliferation of weapons of mass destruction, that is, the direct or indirect collection or transfer of funds or other property obtained in any manner with the purpose of using them or knowing that they will be used, in whole or in part, to finance the production, storage, transportation, use or proliferation of weapons of mass destruction, — the applicable sentence is life imprisonment or deprivation of liberty for a term of eight to twenty years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property.

(2) For the financing of production, storage, transportation, use or proliferation of weapons of mass destruction, if committed on a large scale, — the applicable sentence is life imprisonment or deprivation of liberty for a term of fifteen to twenty years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property.

(In the wording of the Law of 10.03.2016 with amendments made by the Law of 26.04.2018, which entered into force on 23.05.2018.)

### 74.pants. War Crimes

For war crimes, that is, for violation of the rules of warfare or international humanitarian law prohibited by international law binding on the Republic of Latvia, including the murder, torture or inhumane treatment of persons protected by humanitarian law, the taking of hostages, unlawful deportation, displacement, restriction of liberty, unjustified destruction of cities or other objects, or other prohibited acts — the applicable sentence is life imprisonment or deprivation of liberty for a term of three to twenty years.

(With amendments made by the Law of 21.05.2009, which entered into force on 01.07.2009.)

74.1 pants. Justification of Genocide, Crime Against Humanity, Crime Against Peace and War Crime

(1) For the public glorification of genocide, crime against humanity, crime against peace or war crime, or the public glorification, denial, justification or gross trivialisation of committed genocide, crime against humanity, crime against peace or war crime, including genocide, crime against humanity, crime against peace or war crime committed by the USSR or Nazi Germany against the Republic of Latvia and its inhabitants, — the applicable sentence is deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years, or short-term deprivation of liberty, or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.

(2) For the same acts, if committed by a group of persons or by a State official, or a responsible employee of an enterprise (company) or organisation, or if committed using an automated data processing system, — the applicable sentence is deprivation of liberty for a term of up to six years and probation supervision for a term of up to three years or without it.

(In the wording of the Law of 21.05.2009 with amendments made by the Laws of 13.12.2012, 15.05.2014, 17.12.2020 and 14.12.2023, which entered into force on 30.12.2023.)

### 75.pants. Violence Against the Population in a Zone of Hostilities

For unlawful violence against the population in a zone of hostilities, as well as for the unlawful violent seizure or destruction of their property — the applicable sentence is deprivation of liberty for a term of three to fifteen years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property, and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years or without it.

(With amendments made by the Law of 14.12.2023, which entered into force on 30.12.2023.)

### 76.pants. Marauding

For the appropriation of property of the fallen or wounded on the battlefield (marauding) — the applicable sentence is deprivation of liberty for a term of up to eight years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property, and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years or without it.

(With amendments made by the Law of 14.12.2023, which entered into force on 30.12.2023.)

### 77.pants. Incitement to Aggressive War

For public incitement to aggressive war or the provocation of a military conflict — the applicable sentence is deprivation of liberty for a term of up to eight years.

77.1 pants. Unlawful Participation in an Armed Conflict

For unlawful participation in an armed conflict, that is, for active participation in an armed conflict taking place outside the territory of the Republic of Latvia, which is directed against the territorial integrity or political independence of a State or is otherwise contrary to international law binding on the Republic of Latvia, without complying with regulatory enactments or international agreements binding on the Republic of Latvia, — the applicable sentence is deprivation of liberty for a term of up to ten years and probation supervision for a term of up to three years.

(In the wording of the Law of 12.02.2015, which entered into force on 19.02.2015.)

77.2 pants. Financing of an Armed Conflict

For the direct or indirect collection or transfer of funds or other property obtained in any manner to a party involved in an armed conflict taking place outside the territory of the Republic of Latvia, the activities of which are directed against the territorial integrity or political independence of a State or are otherwise contrary to international law binding on the Republic of Latvia, as well as for the recruitment, training or dispatch of a person for unlawful participation in an armed conflict taking place outside the territory of the Republic of Latvia, — the applicable sentence is deprivation of liberty for a term of up to ten years and probation supervision for a term of up to three years.

(In the wording of the Law of 12.02.2015, which entered into force on 19.02.2015.)

77.3 pants. Recruitment, Training and Dispatch for an Armed Conflict

For the recruitment, training or dispatch of a person for unlawful participation in an armed conflict taking place outside the territory of the Republic of Latvia, — the applicable sentence is deprivation of liberty for a term of up to eight years and probation supervision for a term of up to three years.

(In the wording of the Law of 12.02.2015, which entered into force on 19.02.2015.)

77.4 pants. Assistance to a Foreign State in Action Directed Against Another State

For action with the intent to assist a person, foreign state or foreign organisation in undermining or threatening the territorial integrity or political independence of another democratic state, or otherwise acting against it contrary to international law binding on the Republic of Latvia, without complying with regulatory enactments or international agreements binding on the Republic of Latvia, — the applicable sentence is deprivation of liberty for a term of up to eight years and probation supervision for a term of up to three years.

(In the wording of the Law of 14.12.2023, which entered into force on 30.12.2023.)

### 78.pants. Incitement of National, Ethnic and Racial Hatred

(1) For action aimed at inciting national, ethnic, racial or religious hatred or discord, — the applicable sentence is deprivation of liberty for a term of up to three years, or short-term deprivation of liberty, or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.

(2) For the same acts, if committed by a group of persons or by a State official, or a responsible employee of an enterprise (company) or organisation, or if committed using an automated data processing system, — the applicable sentence is deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years, or short-term deprivation of liberty, or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.

(3) For the acts provided for in paragraph one of this Article, if they involve violence or threats, or if committed by an organised group, — the applicable sentence is deprivation of liberty for a term of up to ten years and probation supervision for a term of up to three years or without it.

(In the wording of the Law of 25.09.2014 with amendments made by the Law of 17.12.2020, which entered into force on 01.01.2022.)

### 79.pants. Destruction of Cultural and National Heritage

For deliberate destruction of values that are a component of cultural and national heritage, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to twelve years.

(With amendments made by the law of 13.12.2012, which enters into force on 01.04.2013.)

## Chapter IX

Offences Related to Terrorism

(Chapter as worded by the law of 26.04.2018, which enters into force on 23.05.2018.)

79.1 pants. Terrorism

(1) For bombing, arson, use of nuclear, chemical, biological, bacteriological, toxic or other weapons of mass destruction, mass poisoning, spread of epidemics or epizootics, kidnapping of a person, seizure of hostages, seizure of air, land or water transport, or other acts, if they are carried out with the intent to intimidate the population or to compel a state, its institutions or an international organisation to perform any act or to refrain from it, or to harm the interests of the state, its population or an international organisation (terrorism), — shall be punished by life imprisonment or by deprivation of liberty for a term from eight to twenty years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property, and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years.

(2) For destruction or damage of physical objects, automated data processing systems, electronic networks, as well as other objects located in the territory of the state or on the continental shelf, the purpose of which is to ensure state security, if such acts are carried out with the intent provided for in paragraph one of this Article, — shall be punished by life imprisonment or by deprivation of liberty for a term from ten to twenty years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property, and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years.

(As worded by the law of 26.04.2018, which enters into force on 23.05.2018.)

79.2 pants. Financing of Terrorism

(1) For financing of terrorism — shall be punished by life imprisonment or by deprivation of liberty for a term from eight to twenty years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property, and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years or without it.

(2) For financing of terrorism, if it has been committed on a large scale, — shall be punished by life imprisonment or by deprivation of liberty for a term from ten to twenty years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property, and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years or without it.

(As worded by the law of 26.04.2018, which enters into force on 23.05.2018.)

79.3 pants. Terrorist Group

(1) For organising a terrorist group, that is, a group of persons by prior agreement, with the intent to commit one or more offences related to terrorism, as well as for involvement in such a group — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term from eight to seventeen years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property, and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years.

(2) For leading a terrorist group or participating in offences referred to in paragraph one of this Article committed by such a group — shall be punished by life imprisonment or deprivation of liberty for a term from twelve to twenty years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property, and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years.

(As worded by the law of 26.04.2018, which enters into force on 23.05.2018.)

79.4 pants. Recruitment, Training and Receiving Training for Terrorism

(1) For obtaining or receiving instructions, knowledge or practical skills with the intent to commit or facilitate terrorism (receiving training for terrorism) — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to seven years and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years.

(2) For recruiting a person for terrorism or for involvement in a terrorist group or for training for terrorism — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to ten years and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years.

(As worded by the law of 26.04.2018, which enters into force on 23.05.2018.)

79.5 pants. Travelling for the Purpose of Terrorism

For travelling with the intent to commit or facilitate terrorism, to join a terrorist group or to train or receive training for terrorism — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to eight years and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years.

(As worded by the law of 26.04.2018, which enters into force on 23.05.2018.)

79.6 pants. Justification of Terrorism, Incitement to Terrorism and Threats of Terrorism

(1) For public glorification or justification of terrorism or for public incitement to terrorism, or for distribution of material with content glorifying, justifying or inciting terrorism — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2) For threats to commit terrorism, if there are grounds to believe that it may be carried out, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to eight years and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years.

(As worded by the law of 26.04.2018, with amendments made by the law of 17.12.2020, which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

## Chapter X

Offences Against the State

### 80.pants. Acts Directed Against the Republic of Latvia

(1) For acts directed against the state independence, sovereignty, territorial integrity, state power or state structure of the Republic of Latvia in a manner not provided for in the Constitution of the Republic of Latvia, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to eight years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property, and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years.

(2) For the same acts, if committed using violence, or if committed by an organised group, or if committed by a state official or a responsible employee of an enterprise (company) or organisation, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term from five to fifteen years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property, and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years.

(As worded by the law of 21.04.2016, with amendments made by the law of 14.12.2023, which enters into force on 30.12.2023.)

80.1 pants. Association in an Organised Group with the Aim of Carrying Out Acts Directed Against the Republic of Latvia

(1) For association of more than two persons in an organised group with the intent to act against the state independence, sovereignty, territorial integrity, state power or state structure of the Republic of Latvia in a manner not provided for in the Constitution of the Republic of Latvia — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to eight years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property, and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years.

(2) For the same acts, if committed by a state official or a responsible employee of an enterprise (company) or organisation, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term from two to ten years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property, and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years.

(As worded by the law of 21.04.2016, with amendments made by the law of 17.12.2020 and 14.12.2023, which enters into force

### 81.pants. Call Directed against the Republic of Latvia

(1) For a public call to act against the state independence, sovereignty, territorial integrity, state power or state structure of the Republic of Latvia in a manner not provided for in the Constitution of the Republic of Latvia or for distribution of material containing such a call — punishable by deprivation of liberty for a term up to five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision.

(2) For the same action, if committed by a group of persons or a state official, or a responsible employee of an enterprise (company) or organization, — punishable by deprivation of liberty for a term up to six years and by probation supervision for a term up to three years. (Version of law of 21.04.2016 with amendments made by laws of 17.12.2020 and 14.12.2023, which comes into force Section 81.1 Assistance to a Foreign State in Activities Directed against the Republic of Latvia

(1) For action with the intent to assist a foreigner, foreign state or foreign organization in acting against the state independence, sovereignty, territorial integrity, state power, state structure or state security of the Republic of Latvia — punishable by deprivation of liberty for a term up to five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property.

(2) For the same action, if committed by a group of persons or a state official, or a responsible employee of an enterprise (company) or organization, — punishable by deprivation of liberty for a term from three to twenty years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property, and by probation supervision for a term up to three years.

(Version of law of 21.04.2016 with amendments made by laws of 17.12.2020 and 14.12.2023, which comes into force

### 82.pants. Call to Liquidate the State Independence of the Republic of Latvia

(Deleted by law of 21.04.2016, which comes into force 11.05.2016.)

### 83.pants. Call to Destroy the Territorial Integrity of the Republic of Latvia

(Deleted by law of 21.04.2016, which comes into force 11.05.2016.)

### 84.pants. Violation of Sanctions Imposed by International Organizations and the Republic of Latvia

(1) For violation of sanctions imposed by the United Nations, the European Union and other international organizations or for violation of national sanctions imposed by the Republic of Latvia — punishable by deprivation of liberty for a term up to four years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2) For the same actions, if they have caused substantial harm, — punishable by deprivation of liberty for a term up to five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(3) For the criminal offense provided for in paragraph two of this Section, if committed by a group of persons pursuant to a prior agreement or if committed by a state official or a responsible employee of an enterprise (company) or organization, — punishable by deprivation of liberty for a term up to eight years.

(With amendments made by laws of 01.06.2000, 13.12.2012, 29.10.2015, 28.01.2016, 06.06.2019, 17.12.2020 and 14.12.2023, which comes into force 30.12.2023.)

### 85.pants. Espionage

(1) For unlawful collection of non-disclosable information with the intent to transfer it or for its transfer to a foreign state or foreign organization directly or through an intermediary or for unlawful collection or transfer of other information to a foreign intelligence service on its assignment directly or through an intermediary — punishable by deprivation of liberty for a term from one to ten years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property, and by probation supervision for a term up to three years.

(2) For unlawful collection or transfer of state secret to a foreign state or foreign organization directly or through an intermediary — punishable by deprivation of liberty for a term from three to twenty years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property, and by probation supervision for a term up to three years.

(Version of law of 21.04.2016 with amendments made by law of 14.12.2023, which comes into force 30.12.2023.)

85.1 pants. Organization and Management of Espionage

For organization or management of espionage — punishable by life imprisonment or by deprivation of liberty for a term from eight to twenty years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property, and by probation supervision for a term up to three years.

(Version of law of 14.12.2023, which comes into force 30.12.2023.)

### 86.pants. Threat to the Life and Health of the President of the State of the Republic of Latvia, a Member of the Saeima, a Member of the Cabinet of Ministers and Other

State Official

For an attack on the President of the State of the Republic of Latvia, a Member of the Saeima, a Member of the Cabinet of Ministers or other state official elected, appointed or confirmed by the Saeima of the Republic of Latvia in connection with their state activities in the interests of the Republic of Latvia, if the attack is associated with a threat to the life or health of such person, — punishable by deprivation of liberty for a term from three to fifteen years and by probation supervision for a term up to three years or without it.

(With amendments made by laws of 13.12.2012 and 14.12.2023, which comes into force 30.12.2023.)

### 87.pants. Threat to the Life and Health of Foreign Representatives

(1) For an attack on the head of a foreign state or its government or other foreign representative who has officially arrived in the Republic of Latvia, if the attack is associated with a threat to the life or health of such person, — punishable by deprivation of liberty for a term from three to fifteen years and by probation supervision for a term up to three years or without it.

(2) For the same actions, if they have caused serious consequences for the Republic of Latvia, — punishable by deprivation of liberty for a term from five to twenty years and by probation supervision for a term up to three years or without it.

(With amendments made by laws of 13.12.2012 and 14.12.2023, which comes into force 30.12.2023.)

### 88.pants. Terrorism

(Deleted by law of 26.04.2018, which comes into force 23.05.2018. See transitional provision 22)

88.1 pants. Financing of Terrorism

(Deleted by law of 26.04.2018, which comes into force 23.05.2018. See transitional provision 22)

88.2 pants. Call for Terrorism and Terrorist Threats

(Deleted by law of 26.04.2018, which comes into force 23.05.2018. See transitional provision 22)

88.3 pants. Recruitment and Training of Persons for Committing Terrorist Acts

(Deleted by law of 26.04.2018, which comes into force 23.05.2018. See transitional provision 22)

### 89.pants. Sabotage

For actions or inactions directed at undermining the financial system, industry, transport, agriculture, trade or other sectors of the national economy, as well as the functioning of institutions or organizations with the intent to harm the Republic of Latvia, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of five to twelve years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property.

(With amendments made by the laws of 11.12.2003. and 13.12.2012., which enters into force on 01.04.2013.)

89.1 pants. Criminal Organization

(1) For the establishment of a criminal organization (association) composed of at least five persons with the intent to commit particularly serious crimes against humanity or peace, war crimes, to carry out genocide or to commit particularly serious crimes against the state, as well as for involvement in such an organization or in an organized group or other criminal formation forming part thereof — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of eight to seventeen years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property, and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years or without such.

(2) For leading a criminal organization or for participation in crimes provided for in paragraph one of this Article committed by such an organization — shall be punished by life imprisonment or by deprivation of liberty for a term of ten to twenty years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property, and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years.

(In the wording of the law of 25.04.2002. with amendments made by the law of 13.12.2012., which enters into force on 01.04.2013. See transitional provision point 16)

### 90.pants. Hindering the Exercise of Electoral Rights, the Right to Participate in the Initiation of a Law, the Initiation of a Referendum, a Referendum and the Support of a European Citizens' Initiative

(1) For intentionally hindering a person from freely participating in the collection of signatures for the initiation of a law or referendum or for the support of a European Citizens' initiative organized in accordance with the laws of the Republic of Latvia, by using violence, fraud, threats, bribery or other unlawful means, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to one year or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2) For intentionally hindering a person from freely exercising the right to elect deputies and to be elected or to freely participate in a referendum organized in accordance with the laws of the Republic of Latvia, by using violence, fraud, threats, bribery or other unlawful means, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to three years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision.

(In the wording of the law of 13.12.2012. with amendments made by the laws of 17.12.2020. and 14.12.2023., which enters into force

### 91.pants. Dissemination of Intentionally False Information About a Candidate for Deputy

(Deleted by the law of 12.06.2003., which enters into force on 15.07.2003.)

### 92.pants. Falsification of Signature Collection, Election and Referendum Documents, Incorrect Vote Counting and Violation of Secret Ballot

For falsification of signature collection documents for the initiation of a law or referendum or for the support of a European Citizens' initiative or for falsification of election or referendum documents or intentionally incorrect vote counting, as well as for intentional violation of secret ballot, committed by a state official or a member of an election commission, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to four years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine, with deprivation of the right to hold a specific position for a term of up to five years.

(In the wording of the law of 13.12.2012. with amendments made by the law of 17.12.2020., which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

### 93.pants. Desecration of State Symbols

For tearing down, tearing up, breaking, destroying or otherwise desecrating the coat of arms of the State of Latvia or the flag of the State of Latvia, as well as for public desecration of the national anthem of Latvia — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to three years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(With amendments made by the laws of 12.02.2004., 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

### 94. pants. Intentional Disclosure of Confidential, Secret and Top Secret State Secrets

For intentional disclosure of a confidential, secret or top secret state secret, if committed by a person who has been warned against disclosure of confidential, secret and top secret state secrets, and if this offense does not have the characteristics of espionage, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine, with deprivation of the right to engage in a specific occupation or the right to hold a specific position for a term of up to five years.

(In the wording of the law of 21.10.2010. with amendments made by the laws of 13.12.2012., 17.12.2020. and 20.04.2023., which enters into force on 01.05.2023.)

### 95. pants. Negligent Disclosure of Confidential, Secret and Top Secret State Secrets

For negligent disclosure of a confidential, secret or top secret state secret, if committed by a person who has been warned against disclosure of confidential, secret and top secret state secrets, or for the loss of a confidential, secret or top secret state secret object, if committed by a person to whom this state secret object was entrusted, and if substantial harm has been caused thereby, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to two years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(In the wording of the law of 21.10.2010. with amendments made by the laws of 13.12.2012., 17.12.2020. and 20.04.2023., which enters into force on 01.05.2023.)

95.1 pants. Service Abroad

For violation of the prohibition established in regulatory enactments to serve in the armed forces, internal military forces, military organization, intelligence or security service, police (militia) or judicial institution service of a foreign state or other subject of international law or established in its territory — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to four years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(In the wording of the law of 15.12.2016. with amendments made by the law of 17.12.2020., which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

95.2 pants. Violation of the Prohibition to Organize and Conduct Military Tactical Task Performance Training and to Participate Therein

For violation of the prohibition established in regulatory enactments to organize or conduct military tactical task performance training, as well as for violation of the prohibition to participate in such training — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by a fine.

(In the wording of the law of 14.12.2023., which enters into force on 30.12.2023.)

## XI nodaļa

Criminal Offenses Against the Natural Environment

### 96.pants. Violation of Rules for the Management and Use of Land, Its Subsoil, Waters and Forests

For violation of the rules for the management or use of land, its subsoil, waters or forests, if substantial harm has been caused thereby, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to three years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(With amendments made by the laws of 21.10.2010., 13.12.2012., 17.12.2020. and 07.04.2022., which enters into force

### 97.pants. Violation of Rules for the Use of Marine Natural Resources

(1) (Deleted by the Law of 21.10.2010.)

(2) For violation of rules for the exploration or use of natural resources in the territorial sea, continental shelf, or exclusive economic zone of the Republic of Latvia, if it has caused substantial harm, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to four years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(As amended by the Laws of 12.02.2004., 21.10.2010., 13.12.2012., 17.12.2020. and 07.04.2022., which entered into force on 04.05.2022.)

### 98.pants. Violation of Safety Rules for the Circulation of Radioactive and Chemical Substances

(1) (Deleted by the Law of 21.10.2010.)

(2) For violation of rules for the production, acquisition, movement, storage, processing, or use of radioactive substances or other sources of ionizing radiation, dangerous biological, bacteriological, toxic, chemical substances or mixtures, or other dangerous substances or materials, preparations, or viruses, if it has caused substantial harm, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to four years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.
