(3) For violation of the rules specified in paragraph two of this Section, if it has caused serious consequences, or for the criminal offense provided for in paragraph two of this Section, if it has been committed by an organized group, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to eight years and by probation supervision for a term of up to three years or without it.

(4) For violation of the rules specified in paragraph two of this Section, if it has caused the death of two or more persons, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to ten years and by probation supervision for a term of up to three years or without it.

(As amended by the Laws of 12.02.2004., 12.10.2006., 13.12.2007., 21.10.2010., 08.07.2011., 13.12.2012., 25.09.2014., 08.06.2017., 17.12.2020. and 07.04.2022., which entered into force on 04.05.2022.)

98.1 pants. Illegal Activities with Ozone-Depleting Substances

(1) For violation of rules for the production, acquisition, disposal, movement, storage, processing, or use of ozone-depleting substances — shall be punished by short-term deprivation of liberty or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2) For the same activities, if they have caused substantial harm, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to four years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(As worded by the Law of 25.09.2014. as amended by the Law of 17.12.2020., which entered into force on 01.01.2022.)

### 99.pants. Violation of Waste Management Rules

(1) (Deleted by the Law of 21.10.2010.)

(2) For violation of waste management rules, if it has caused substantial harm, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to four years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(3) For import of waste into the territory of Latvia or transit transportation through the territory of Latvia, in violation of rules, if it has caused substantial harm, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(4) For the criminal offense provided for in paragraph two or three of this Section, if it has been committed by an organized group, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to seven years and by probation supervision for a term of up to three years. (As amended by the Laws of 12.02.2004., 13.12.2007., 21.10.2010., 08.07.2011., 13.12.2012., 25.09.2014., 08.06.2017., 17.12.2020. and 07.04.2022., which entered into force on 04.05.2022.)

### 100.pants. Unauthorized Disposal of Hazardous Substances in Waters and in the Subsoil

(1) For unauthorized disposal of radioactive substances, including nuclear materials, hazardous chemical substances or products, materials, or waste in waters or in the subsoil — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to four years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2) For disposal of nuclear, chemical, biological, bacteriological, toxic, or other weapons of mass destruction in waters or in the subsoil — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of three to twenty years.

(As worded by the Law of 18.05.2000. as amended by the Laws of 12.02.2004., 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which entered into force on 01.01.2022.)

### 101.pants. Marine Pollution

(1) (Deleted by the Law of 21.10.2010.)

(2) For marine pollution with hazardous or other harmful substances, materials, or waste, if it has caused substantial harm, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to four years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(3) For marine pollution with hazardous or other harmful substances, materials, or waste, if it has caused serious consequences, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to ten years.

(As amended by the Laws of 12.02.2004., 13.12.2007., 21.10.2010., 13.12.2012., 29.10.2015., 17.12.2020. and 07.04.2022., which entered into force on 04.05.2022.)

### 102.pants. Pollution and Littering of Land, Forests, and Waters

(1) (Deleted by the Law of 21.10.2010.)

(2) For pollution of land, forests, or inland waters (surface or underground) with hazardous or other harmful substances, materials, or waste, littering, or other harmful impact on them in any way, if it has caused substantial harm, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to four years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(3) For pollution of land, forests, or inland waters (surface or underground) with hazardous or other harmful substances, materials, or waste, littering, or other harmful impact on them in any way, if it has caused serious consequences, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to ten years.

(As amended by the Laws of 12.02.2004., 13.12.2007., 21.10.2010., 13.12.2012., 17.12.2020. and 07.04.2022., which entered into force on 04.05.2022.)

### 103.pants. Air Pollution

(1) (Deleted by the Law of 21.10.2010.)

(2) For air pollution, littering, physical or other harmful impact on it in any way, exceeding the established standards or violating rules, if it has caused substantial harm, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to four years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(As amended by the Laws of 12.02.2004., 21.10.2010., 13.12.2012., 29.10.2015., 17.12.2020. and 07.04.2022., which entered into force on 04.05.2022.)

### 104.pants. Operation of Facilities Without Treatment Structures

For the operation of industrial, agricultural, municipal or other facilities if they do not have the necessary treatment structures and devices for capturing harmful substances and dust, or if they are in such a condition that they are not fit for operation, and if this has caused significant harm, — shall be punished by short-term deprivation of liberty or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine. (21.10.2010. likuma redakcijā ar grozījumiem, kas izdarīti ar 13.12.2012., 17.12.2020. un 07.04.2022. likumu, kas stājas spēkā 04.05.2022.)

104.1 pants. Illegal Operation of Installations

For illegal operation of an installation specified in a pollution control regulatory enactment, in which environmentally hazardous activities are carried out or hazardous substances or preparations are stored or used, if this has caused significant harm, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to two years or short-term deprivation of liberty, or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.

(25.09.2014. likuma redakcijā ar grozījumiem, kas izdarīti ar 17.12.2020. un 07.04.2022. likumu, kas stājas spēkā

### 105.pants. Failure to Take Measures to Eliminate Environmental Pollution

For failure to take or improper execution of measures necessary to eliminate environmental pollution and prevent other harmful consequences that fall within a person's duties, as well as for failure to report when harmful consequences have occurred, shall be punished by short-term deprivation of liberty or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine. (Ar grozījumiem, kas izdarīti ar 12.02.2004., 21.10.2010., 13.12.2012. un 17.12.2020. likumu, kas stājas spēkā

### 106.pants. Concealment of Data on Environmental Pollution

(1) For failure to report pollution of sea waters or other harmful effects from vehicles or structures at sea, if committed by a person who had a duty to report, — shall be punished by short-term deprivation of liberty or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.

(2) For deliberate concealment or falsification of data on the extent of environmental pollution, if committed by a person who had a duty to provide such data, and if it has resulted in significant harm, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to four years or short-term deprivation of liberty, or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.

(21.10.2010. likuma redakcijā ar grozījumiem, kas izdarīti ar 13.12.2012., 17.12.2020. un 07.04.2022. likumu, kas stājas spēkā 04.05.2022.)

### 107.pants. Forest Arson

(1) For deliberate setting fire to a forest — shall be punished by short-term deprivation of liberty or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.

(2) For the same acts, if they have caused significant harm, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to three years or short-term deprivation of liberty, or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.

(3) For the same acts, if they have caused the death of a person or other serious consequences through the negligence of the guilty party, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to eight years and probation supervision for a term of up to three years or without it.

(13.12.2012. likuma redakcijā ar grozījumiem, kas izdarīti ar 17.12.2020. likumu, kas stājas spēkā 01.01.2022.)

### 108.pants. Destruction and Damage of Forest by Negligence

(1) For destruction or damage of a forest area by negligence, through careless handling of fire or in another manner, if this has caused significant harm, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to two years or short-term deprivation of liberty, or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.

(2) For the same acts, if they have caused the death of a person or other serious consequences, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or short-term deprivation of liberty, or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.

(Ar grozījumiem, kas izdarīti ar 12.02.2004., 21.10.2010., 13.12.2012. un 17.12.2020. likumu, kas stājas spēkā

### 109.pants. Unauthorized Felling and Damage of Trees

(1) For unauthorized felling of trees in another's forest or other land area — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to two years or short-term deprivation of liberty, or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.

(2) For unauthorized felling, destruction or damage of trees in a specially protected nature area, micro-reserve, park, square, alley or environmental and natural resource protection buffer zone — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to three years or short-term deprivation of liberty, or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.

(3) For unauthorized felling, destruction or damage of trees, if these acts have caused significant harm, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or short-term deprivation of liberty, or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.

(4) For the criminal offence provided for in paragraph one, two or three of this Section, if committed by an organised group, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to eight years.

(13.12.2007. likuma redakcijā ar grozījumiem, kas izdarīti ar 21.10.2010., 13.12.2012., 29.10.2015., 08.06.2017. un 17.12.2020. likumu, kas stājas spēkā 01.01.2022.)

### 110.pants. Unauthorized Fishing and Acquisition of Aquatic Animals

(1) (Izslēgta ar 21.10.2010. likumu)

(2) For catching or otherwise acquiring fish or aquatic animals without the relevant permit, during a prohibited period or in prohibited places, or with prohibited gear or methods (unauthorized acquisition), if this has caused significant harm, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to two years or short-term deprivation of liberty, or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.

(21) For the criminal offence provided for in paragraph two of this Section, if committed by a group of persons pursuant to a prior agreement or if committed in a specially protected nature area, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to three years or short-term deprivation of liberty, or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.

(3) For unauthorized acquisition of fish or aquatic animals using electric current, explosive substances, toxic substances or other generally dangerous means or methods, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to four years or short-term deprivation of liberty, or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.

(Ar grozījumiem, kas izdarīti ar 25.04.2002., 21.10.2010., 13.12.2012., 25.09.2014. un 17.12.2020. likumu, kas stājas spēkā 01.01.2022.)

### 111.pants.

Illegal Manufacturing, Acquisition, Storage, Sale, Transportation and Forwarding of Electrofishing Devices

For illegal manufacturing, acquisition, storage, sale, transportation or forwarding of electrofishing devices — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to two years or short-term deprivation of liberty, or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.

(Ar grozījumiem, kas izdarīti ar 12.02.2004., 21.10.2010., 13.12.2012. un 17.12.2020. likumu, kas stājas spēkā

### 112.pants. Illegal Hunting

(1) (Deleted by Law of 21.10.2010.)

(2) For illegal hunting, if committed by a group of persons pursuant to prior agreement or if committed in a specially protected natural area, or if significant harm has been caused thereby, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to two years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(3) For the use of prohibited generally dangerous means, methods, tools or techniques in hunting — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to four years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(In the wording of the Law of 18.12.2003., with amendments made by Laws of 12.02.2004., 21.10.2010., 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

### 113.pants. Carrying out Blasting and Other Work in Violation of Animal Protection Regulations

For carrying out blasting, melioration, timber preparation or other work in violation of animal protection regulations, if such activities have caused significant harm to fish resources, birds or other wild animals, — shall be punished by short-term deprivation of liberty or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine. (With amendments made by Laws of 12.02.2004., 21.10.2010., 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which enters into force on

### 114.pants. Destruction and Damage of Specially Protected Natural Areas

For destruction or damage of specially protected natural areas, if significant harm has been caused thereby, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(In the wording of the Law of 21.10.2010., with amendments made by Laws of 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which enters into force on

### 115.pants. Destruction and Damage of Specially Protected Animals and Plants

For catching, keeping or destroying specially protected animals, or for obtaining, keeping, damaging or destroying specially protected plants, fungi or lichens, or for destruction or damage of habitats of specially protected species or specially protected biotopes, if significant harm has been caused thereby, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(In the wording of the Law of 21.10.2010., with amendments made by Laws of 13.12.2012., 25.09.2014. and 17.12.2020., which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

115.1 pants. Violation of Trade Regulations for Specimens of Endangered Wild Fauna and Flora Species

For violation of trade regulations concerning specimens of endangered wild fauna or flora species, or parts thereof or products, if significant harm has been caused thereby, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to two years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(In the wording of the Law of 21.10.2010., with amendments made by Laws of 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which enters into force on

## XII nodaļa

Killing

### 116.pants. Murder

For intentional unlawful killing of another person (murder) — shall be punished by life imprisonment or by deprivation of liberty for a term from five to twenty years and by probation supervision for a term of up to three years.

(With amendments made by Laws of 08.07.2011., 13.12.2012. and 15.05.2014., which enters into force on 14.06.2014. See Paragraph 11 of Transitional Provisions)

### 117.pants. Murder under Aggravating Circumstances

For murder, if:

- 1) a woman has been killed, the guilty person being aware that she is in a state of pregnancy;
- 2) a person has been killed, the guilty person being aware that the person is in a helpless state;
- 3) it has been committed in a manner dangerous to the lives of several persons;
- 4) it has been committed with particular cruelty;
- 5) desecration of the corpse has been committed thereafter;
- 6) it is connected with robbery;
- 7) it is connected with rape;
- 8) it has been committed for the purpose of concealing another criminal offence or facilitating its commission;
- 9) it has been committed for mercenary purposes;
- 10) it has been committed by a group of persons;
- 11) it has been committed by a person who is placed in a place of short-term detention or imprisonment;
- 12) a minor has been killed,— shall be punished by life imprisonment or by deprivation of liberty for a term from ten to twenty years and by probation supervision for a term of up to three years, with or without confiscation of property.

(With amendments made by Laws of 18.05.2000., 08.07.2011. and 13.12.2012., which enters into force on 01.04.2013. See Paragraph 11 of Transitional Provisions)

### 118.pants. Murder under Particularly Aggravating Circumstances

For murder

1) in connection with the fact that the victim or a relative thereof has performed official or professional duties or participated in preventing or interrupting a criminal or other unlawful act, or given testimony in court or in pre-trial criminal proceedings;

2) if two or more persons have been killed;

3) (deleted by Law of 13.12.2012.);

4) if it has been committed by a person serving life imprisonment;

5) if it has been committed by an organised group, — shall be punished by life imprisonment or by deprivation of liberty for a term from fifteen to twenty years and by probation supervision for a term of up to three years, with or without confiscation of property.

(With amendments made by Laws of 13.12.2007., 08.07.2011., 01.12.2011. and 13.12.2012., which enters into force on 01.04.2013. See Paragraph 11 of Transitional Provisions)

### 119.pants. Murder of a Newborn Child

For murder of her child committed by a mother during childbirth or immediately after childbirth under the influence of the mental and physiological state caused thereby, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service.

(With amendments made by Laws of 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

### 120.pants. Murder Committed in a State of Strong Mental Agitation

For murder committed in a state of sudden strong mental agitation caused by violence or serious insult to honour on the part of the victim, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service.

(With amendments made by Laws of 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

### 121.pants. Murder Committed by Exceeding the Limits of Necessary Defence

For murder committed by exceeding the limits of necessary defence, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to two years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service. supervision, or by community service.

(With amendments made by Laws of 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

### 122.pants. Murder Committed by Exceeding the Conditions of Person's Apprehension

(1) For murder committed by exceeding the conditions of person's apprehension, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to two years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service.

(2) For the same acts, if committed by a public official, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to three years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service.

(With amendments made by Laws of 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

### 123.pants. Homicide by Negligence

(1) For unlawful homicide by negligence — shall be punished with imprisonment for a term of up to three years or with short-term imprisonment, or with probation supervision, or with community service.

(2) For unlawful homicide by negligence, if two or more persons have been killed or if the homicide was committed while handling a firearm or explosives or in another generally dangerous manner, — shall be punished with imprisonment for a term of up to five years or with short-term imprisonment, or with probation supervision, or with community service.

(As amended by the laws of 13.12.2012 and 17.12.2020, which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

### 124.pants. Driving to Suicide

(1) For driving a person to suicide or to an attempt thereof, by treating the victim cruelly or systematically humiliating their personal dignity, if this person was not in material or other dependence on the guilty party, — shall be punished with imprisonment for a term of up to three years or with short-term imprisonment, or with probation supervision, or with community service.

(2) For the same acts in relation to a person who was in material or other dependence on the guilty party, — shall be punished with imprisonment for a term of up to five years or with short-term imprisonment, or with probation supervision, or with community service.

(As amended by the laws of 13.12.2012 and 17.12.2020, which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

## XIII nodaļa

Criminal Offenses Against Personal Health

### 125.pants. Intentional Severe Bodily Injury

(1) For intentional infliction of severe bodily injury — shall be punished with imprisonment for a term of up to seven years and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years or without it.

(2) For the same acts, if:

- 1) they were committed in connection with the victim or their relative having fulfilled their official or professional duties or having participated in the prevention or interruption of a criminal or other unlawful act, or having given testimony in court or in pre-trial criminal proceedings;
- 2) they were committed against two or more persons;
- 3) they were committed in a manner dangerous to the life or health of several persons;
- 4) they had the character of torture or torment;
- 5) they were committed by a group of persons;
- 6) (excluded by the law of 13.12.2012);
- 7) they were committed by a person who is placed in a short-term detention or imprisonment facility;
- 8) they were committed against a person in a helpless state;
- 9) they were committed against a person with whom the perpetrator of the criminal offense is in the first or second degree of kinship, or against a spouse or former spouse, or against a person with whom the perpetrator of the criminal offense is or has been in a permanent intimate relationship, or against a person with whom the perpetrator of the criminal offense has a common (undivided) household, — shall be punished with imprisonment for a term from two to ten years and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years or without it.
  - (3) For the infliction of intentional severe bodily injury, which through the negligence of the guilty party has been the cause of the victim's death, or for the infliction of intentional severe bodily injury, if it was committed by an organized group, — shall be punished with imprisonment for a term from three to fifteen years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property, and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years or without it.
  - (4) (Excluded by the law of 13.12.2012) (As amended by the laws of 13.12.2007, 30.10.2008, 21.05.2009, 08.07.2011, 13.12.2012, 08.06.2017 and 26.04.2018, which enters into force on 23.05.2018.)

### 126.pants. Intentional Moderate Bodily Injury

(1) For intentional infliction of moderate bodily injury — shall be punished with imprisonment for a term of up to three years or with short-term imprisonment, or with probation supervision, or with community service, or with a fine.

(2) For the same acts, if:

- 1) they were committed in connection with the victim or their relative having fulfilled their official or professional duties or having participated in the prevention or interruption of a criminal or other unlawful act, or having given testimony in court or in pre-trial criminal proceedings;
- 2) they had the character of torture or torment;
- 3) they were committed by a group of persons;
- 4) (excluded by the law of 13.12.2012);
- 5) they were committed by a person who is placed in a short-term detention or imprisonment facility;
- 6) they were committed against a person in a helpless state;
- 7) they were committed against a person with whom the perpetrator of the criminal offense is in the first or second degree of kinship, or against a spouse or former spouse, or against a person with whom the perpetrator of the criminal offense is or has been in a permanent intimate relationship, or against a person with whom the perpetrator of the criminal offense has a common (undivided) household, — shall be punished with imprisonment for a term of up to five years or with short-term imprisonment, or with probation supervision, or with community service, or with a fine.

(As amended by the laws of 30.10.2008, 21.05.2009, 13.12.2012, 08.06.2017, 26.04.2018 and 17.12.2020, which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

### 127.pants. Intentional Bodily Injury Inflicted in a State of Strong Mental Agitation

For intentional severe or moderate bodily injury inflicted in a state of sudden strong mental agitation caused by violence or grave insult to honor from the victim, — shall be punished with imprisonment for a term of up to three years or with short-term imprisonment, or with probation supervision, or with community service, or with a fine.

(As amended by the laws of 13.12.2012 and 17.12.2020, which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

### 128.pants. Infliction of Intentional Bodily Injury Exceeding the Limits of Necessary Defense

For the infliction of intentional severe or moderate bodily injury exceeding the limits of necessary defense, if this injury was not inflicted while defending against a threat to life or against rape, — shall be punished with short-term imprisonment or with probation supervision, or with community service, or with a fine.

(As amended by the laws of 13.12.2012 and 17.12.2020, which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

### 129.pants. Infliction of Intentional Bodily Injury Exceeding the Conditions of Person Detention

(1) For the infliction of intentional severe or moderate bodily injury exceeding the conditions of person detention, — shall be punished with short-term imprisonment or with probation supervision, or with community service, or with a fine.

(2) For the same acts, if they were committed by a state official, — shall be punished with imprisonment for a term of up to one year or with short-term imprisonment, or with probation supervision, or with community service, or with a fine.

(As amended by the laws of 12.02.2004, 13.12.2012 and 17.12.2020, which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

### Article 130. Intentional Minor Bodily Injury

(1) (Deleted by the law of 21.10.2010.)

(2) For the intentional infliction of minor bodily injury, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term up to one year or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(3) For the same acts, if:

- 1) they were committed in connection with the fact that the victim or his or her relative has performed his or her official or professional duties or participated in the prevention or interruption of a criminal or other unlawful act, or given testimony in court or in pre-trial criminal proceedings;
- 2) they were of the nature of torture or torment;
- 3) they were committed by a group of persons;
- 4) they were committed by a person placed in a place of short-term detention or imprisonment;
- 5) they were committed against a person in a state of helplessness;
- 6) they were committed against a person with whom the perpetrator of the criminal offence is related in the first or second degree, or against a spouse or former spouse, or against a person with whom the perpetrator of the criminal offence is or has been in a permanent intimate relationship, or against a person with whom the perpetrator of the criminal offence has a common (undivided) household, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term up to three years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(As amended by the laws of 21.10.2010., 13.12.2012., 25.09.2014., 08.06.2017., 26.04.2018. and 17.12.2020., which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

Article 130.1 Torture

For torture, if these acts have not had the consequences provided for in Articles 125, 126 or 130 of this law, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term up to one year or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(As worded by the law of 25.09.2014. as amended by the law of 17.12.2020., which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

### Article 131. Bodily Injury by Negligence

For causing serious or moderate bodily injury by negligence — shall be punished by short-term deprivation of liberty or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(As amended by the laws of 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

### Article 132. Threats to Commit Murder and Inflict Serious Bodily Injury

(1) For threats to commit murder or inflict serious bodily injury, if there has been reason to fear that these threats may be carried out, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term up to one year or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2) For the same acts, if they were committed against a person with whom the perpetrator of the criminal offence is related in the first or second degree, or against a spouse or former spouse, or against a person with whom the perpetrator of the criminal offence is or has been in a permanent intimate relationship, or against a person with whom the perpetrator of the criminal offence has a common (undivided) household, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term up to three years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(As amended by the laws of 13.12.2012., 17.12.2020. and 15.06.2023., which enters into force on 15.07.2023. See transitional provision 25.1)

Article 132.1 Stalking

(1) For repeated or prolonged stalking, surveillance, threatening or unwanted communication with another person, if this person has had reason to fear for the safety of himself or herself or his or her relatives, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term up to one year or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2) For the same acts, if they were committed against a person with whom the perpetrator of the criminal offence is related in the first or second degree, or against a spouse or former spouse, or against a person with whom the perpetrator of the criminal offence is or has been in a permanent intimate relationship, or against a person with whom the perpetrator of the criminal offence has a common (undivided) household, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term up to three years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(As worded by the law of 08.06.2017. as amended by the laws of 17.12.2020. and 15.06.2023., which enters into force on

### Article 133. Infection with Human Immunodeficiency and Hepatitis B and C Virus

For the deliberate infection of a person with the human immunodeficiency virus or hepatitis B or C virus — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term up to five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(As worded by the law of 21.05.2009. as amended by the laws of 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which enters into force on Article 133.1 Infection with a Dangerous Infectious Disease Pathogen

For the deliberate infection of a person with a dangerous infectious disease pathogen, if it has resulted in serious consequences, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term up to five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(As worded by the law of 21.05.2009. as amended by the laws of 13.12.2012., 03.09.2020. and 17.12.2020., which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

### Article 134. Infection with a Sexually Transmitted Disease Pathogen

(1) For the deliberate infection of a person with a sexually transmitted disease pathogen — shall be punished by short-term deprivation of liberty or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2) For the deliberate infection of a minor with a sexually transmitted disease pathogen — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term up to one year or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(As amended by the laws of 12.02.2004., 21.05.2009., 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which enters into force on

### Article 135. Unauthorized Performance of Abortion

(1) For an abortion performed on a pregnant woman by a person who has the right to do so, if the abortion was performed outside a hospital or other medical institution or in a medical institution but without legal grounds, — shall be punished by short-term deprivation of liberty or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine, with deprivation of the right to engage in medical practice for a term up to five years.

(2) For performing an abortion on a pregnant woman in unsanitary conditions or if it was performed by a person who does not have the right to perform an abortion, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term up to two years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine, with deprivation of the right to engage in medical practice for a term up to five years.

(3) (Deleted by the law of 13.12.2012.)

(4) For the unauthorized performance of an abortion against the will of the pregnant woman or if the unauthorized performance of an abortion has caused the death of the pregnant woman

(4) For the unauthorized performance of an abortion against the will of the pregnant woman or if the unauthorized performance of an abortion has caused the death of the pregnant woman or other serious consequences — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term from three to twelve years, with deprivation of the right to engage in medical practice for a term up to ten years.

(As amended by the laws of 12.02.2004., 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

### Article 136. Coercion to Have an Abortion

For coercing a pregnant woman to have an abortion, if as a result the abortion was performed, — shall be punished by short-term deprivation of liberty or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(As amended by the laws of 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

### 137.pants. Unauthorized Medical Treatment

(1) For unauthorized medical treatment, if it has caused harm to the victim's health, — shall be punished by short-term deprivation of liberty or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.

(2) For unauthorized medical treatment, if it has caused the death of the victim or serious bodily injury through the negligence of the guilty person, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to eight years.

(As amended by laws of 12.02.2004., 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which enters into force 01.01.2022.)

### 138.pants. Improper Performance of Professional Duties by Medical Personnel

(1) For failure to perform or negligent performance of professional duties by medical personnel, if this offence through the negligence of the guilty person has caused serious or moderate bodily injury to the victim, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to one year or short-term deprivation of liberty, or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.

(2) For the same offence, if it has caused the victim to be infected with human immunodeficiency virus or hepatitis B or C virus, or has caused the death of the victim, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or short-term deprivation of liberty, or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.

(As amended by laws of 12.02.2004., 21.05.2009., 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which enters into force

### 139.pants. Illegal Removal and Use of Human Tissues and Organs

(1) For illegal removal or use of tissues or organs of a deceased person for medical or any other purposes — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to three years or short-term deprivation of liberty, or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.

(2) For illegal removal or use of tissues or organs of a living person for medical or any other purposes — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or short-term deprivation of liberty, or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.

(3) For the acts specified in paragraph one or two of this Section, if they have been committed with mercenary intent or if they have been committed by a group of persons, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to seven years.

(4) For the acts specified in paragraph one or two of this Section, if they have been committed by a medical person or if they have been committed by an organised group, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to ten years, with deprivation of the right to engage in medical practice for a term of up to five years.

(In the wording of the law of 13.12.2012. as amended by laws of 06.06.2019., 17.12.2020. and 06.07.2021., which enters into force 05.08.2021. The amendment to paragraph one regarding the replacement of the words "piespiedu darbu" with the words "probācijas uzraudzību, vai ar sabiedrisko darbu" enters into force 01.01.2022. The amendment to paragraph two regarding the replacement of the words "vai ar piespiedu darbu" with the words "vai ar probācijas uzraudzību, vai ar sabiedrisko darbu" enters into force 01.01.2022. See transitional provision point 26.)

139.1 pants. Recruitment of Donors and Recipients of Human Tissues and Organs

(1) For the recruitment of a donor or recipient for illegal removal or transplantation of human tissues or organs — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to one year or short-term deprivation of liberty, or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.

(2) For the same acts, if they have been committed with mercenary intent or if they have been committed by a group of persons, or if they have been committed by a medical person, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to two years or short-term deprivation of liberty, or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.

(In the wording of the law of 06.06.2019. as amended by law of 06.07.2021., which enters into force 05.08.2021. Amendments to paragraphs one and two regarding the replacement of the words "vai ar piespiedu darbu" with the words "vai ar probācijas uzraudzību, vai ar sabiedrisko darbu" enter into force 01.01.2022. See transitional provision point 26.)

### 140.pants. Violation of Sanitary-Hygienic and Epidemiological Safety Regulations

For violation of sanitary-hygienic or epidemiological safety regulations, if it has caused an epidemic or other serious consequences, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or short-term deprivation of liberty, or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.

(As amended by laws of 12.02.2004., 13.12.2012., 03.09.2020. and 17.12.2020., which enters into force

### 141.pants. Leaving Without Assistance

(1) For failure to provide necessary and obviously urgent assistance to a person who is in a life-threatening condition, if the guilty person was aware that they could provide it without serious danger to themselves and other persons, and if the failure to provide assistance has caused the death of a person or other serious consequences,— shall be punished by probation supervision or community service or a fine.

(2) For deliberately leaving without assistance a person who is in a condition dangerous to life or health and who has no possibility to preserve themselves due to minority, age, illness or their own incapacity, if the guilty person had the opportunity to provide assistance to the victim and had a duty to care for them or the guilty person themselves placed them in a life-threatening condition, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to two years or short-term deprivation of liberty, or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.

(21) For the acts specified in paragraph two of this Section, if the failure to provide assistance has caused the death of a person or other serious consequences, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to four years or short-term deprivation of liberty, or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.

(3) For the acts specified in paragraph one or two of this Section, if the failure to provide assistance has caused the death of two or more persons, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or short-term deprivation of liberty, or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.

(As amended by laws of 13.12.2012., 25.09.2014., 07.01.2021. and 17.12.2020. and 06.07.2021., which enters into force 05.08.2021. The amendment to paragraph 2.1 regarding the replacement of the words "vai ar piespiedu darbu" with the words "vai ar probācijas uzraudzību, vai ar sabiedrisko darbu" enters into force 01.01.2022. See transitional provision point 26.)

### 142.pants. Failure to Provide Assistance to Victims at Sea

(1) For failure to proceed to the scene of a disaster at sea, if information has been received that assistance is required, and if this has been committed by a ship's captain, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to two years or short-term deprivation of liberty, or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.

(2) For failure to provide assistance to persons who are perishing at sea or on another waterway, if this assistance could have been provided without serious danger to the ship, its crew and passengers, and if this has been committed by a ship's captain, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or short-term deprivation of liberty, or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.

(In the wording of the law of 13.12.2012. as amended by law of 17.12.2020., which enters into force 01.01.2022.)

## XIV nodaļa

Criminal Offences Against Fundamental Rights and Freedoms of a Person

### 143.pants. Violation of Inviolability of a Person's Dwelling

(1) For unlawful entry into a dwelling against the will of the person residing therein — shall be punished by short-term deprivation of liberty or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.

(2) For the same acts, using violence, threats, fraud or impersonating a state official, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to two years or short-term deprivation of liberty, or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.

(As amended by laws of 12.02.2004., 13.12.2012., 20.03.2014. and 17.12.2020., which enters into force

### 144.pants. Breach of Secrecy of Correspondence and Information Transmitted through Electronic Communications Networks

(1) For intentional breach of secrecy of a person's correspondence — shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of up to two years, or by short-term imprisonment, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2) For unlawful interception of transmission of publicly unavailable data or signals in electronic communications networks, as well as for unlawful acquisition of publicly unavailable electromagnetic data from an electronic communications network in which such data is located — shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of up to three years, or by short-term imprisonment, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(3) For the acts provided for in paragraph one or two of this Article, if committed with mercenary intent — shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of up to five years, or by short-term imprisonment, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(In the version of the law of 25.09.2014. with amendments made by the law of 17.12.2020., which comes into force on 01.01.2022.)

### 145.pants. Unlawful Actions with Personal Data

(1) For unlawful actions with personal data, if they have caused substantial harm — shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of up to two years, or by short-term imprisonment, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2) For unlawful actions with personal data, if committed by a personal data controller or processor for the purpose of revenge, mercenary gain or blackmail — shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of up to four years, or by short-term imprisonment, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(3) For influencing a personal data controller or processor or data subject by using violence or threats, or by abusing trust, or by fraud for the purpose of committing unlawful actions with personal data — shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of up to five years, or by short-term imprisonment, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(In the version of the law of 10.09.2009. with amendments made by the laws of 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which comes into force

### 146.pants. Violation of Occupational Safety Regulations

(1) For violation of the requirements of regulatory enactments regulating occupational safety or technical safety, if committed by a head of an enterprise (undertaking), institution or organisation or another person responsible for compliance with these regulations, and if this offence has caused bodily injury with health impairment or permanent loss of working capacity — shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of up to one year, or by short-term imprisonment, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2) For the same offence, if it has caused the death of a person or serious bodily injury to several persons — shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of up to five years, or by short-term imprisonment, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(With amendments made by the laws of 12.02.2004., 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which comes into force on 01.01.2022.)

### 147.pants. Violation of Inventor and Designer Rights

(1) For intentional disclosure of an invention or design without the permission of the inventor, designer or their successor in title, before the invention or design has been disclosed by the respective person or before it has been disclosed with that person's consent, as well as for appropriation of authorship of an invention or design or imposition of co-authorship — shall be punished by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2) For forcing by violence or threats thereof, or by blackmail to renounce authorship of an invention or design, or for imposition of co-authorship — shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of up to three years, or by short-term imprisonment, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(In the version of the law of 17.10.2002. with amendments made by the laws of 12.02.2004., 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which comes into force on 01.01.2022.)

### 148.pants. Violation of Copyright and Related Rights

(1) For violation of copyright or related rights, if it has caused substantial harm — shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of up to two years, or by short-term imprisonment, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2) For the criminal offence provided for in paragraph one of this Article, if committed by a group of persons pursuant to prior agreement — shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of up to four years, or by short-term imprisonment, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(3) For violation of copyright or related rights, if committed on a large scale, or for the criminal offence provided for in paragraph one of this Article, if committed by an organised group, or for forcing by violence, threats or blackmail to renounce authorship, or for imposition of co-authorship, if committed by violence, threats or blackmail — shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of up to six years, with deprivation of the right to engage in specific employment for a term of up to five years, and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years or without it.

(In the version of the law of 21.10.2010. with amendments made by the laws of 08.07.2011., 13.12.2012., 08.06.2017., 17.12.2020. and 07.04.2022., which comes into force on 04.05.2022.)

### 149.pants. Unlawful Actions with Copyright and Related Rights Objects

(Deleted by the law of 21.10.2010., which comes into force on 01.01.2011.)

149.1 pants. Violation of the Prohibition of Discrimination

(1) For discrimination on grounds of race, national, ethnic or religious affiliation, or for violation of the prohibition of other types of discrimination, if it has caused substantial harm — shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of up to one year, or by short-term imprisonment, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2) For the criminal offence provided for in paragraph one of this Article, if committed by a State official or a responsible employee of an enterprise (undertaking) or organisation, or by a group of persons, or if committed using an automated data processing system, or for the acts provided for in paragraph one of this Article, if they are related to torture — shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of up to three years, or by short-term imprisonment, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(In the version of the law of 25.09.2014. with amendments made by the laws of 17.12.2020. and 07.04.2022., which comes into force

### 150.pants. Incitement of Social Hatred and Discord

(1) For an act aimed at inciting hatred or discord based on a person's gender, age, disability or any other characteristics, if substantial harm has been caused thereby, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to one year or by short-term deprivation of liberty or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2) For the criminal offence specified in paragraph one of this Section, if committed by a public official or a responsible employee of an enterprise (company) or organisation, or by a group of persons or if it has been committed using an automated data processing system, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to three years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(3) For the act specified in paragraph one of this Section, if it is related to violence or threats, or for the criminal offence specified in paragraph one of this Section, if committed by an organised group, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to four years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(As worded by the Law of 25.09.2014. with amendments made by the Laws of 08.06.2017. and 17.12.2020., which come into force

### 151.pants. Disruption of Religious Rituals

(Deleted by the Law of 13.12.2012., which comes into force on 01.04.2013.)

## XV nodaļa

Criminal Offences Against Personal Freedom, Honour and Dignity

### 152.pants. Unlawful Deprivation of Liberty

(1) For an unlawful act depriving a person of the possibility to freely determine their location (unlawful deprivation of liberty), if there are no elements of a criminal offence by a public official, — shall be punished by short-term deprivation of liberty or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2) For the same acts, if they have been committed in a manner dangerous to the victim's life or health or if they are related to inflicting physical suffering on them, or if they have lasted more than one week, or if they have been committed by a group of persons pursuant to prior agreement, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to three years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(3) For unlawful deprivation of liberty, if it has caused serious consequences or if it has been committed by an organised group, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to seven years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property, and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years or without it.

(With amendments made by the Laws of 12.02.2004., 13.12.2007., 08.07.2011., 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which come into force on 01.01.2022.)

### 153.pants. Kidnapping of a Person

(1) For the seizure of a person using violence or threats, or abduction by deceit or by exploiting the person's helpless state (kidnapping of a person), — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2) For the same acts, if they have been committed against a minor, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to seven years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property, and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years.

(3) For kidnapping of a person, if it has caused serious consequences or it has been committed against a young child, or it has been committed by an organised group, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term from three to twelve years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property, and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years or without it.

(With amendments made by the Laws of 13.12.2007., 30.10.2008., 08.07.2011., 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which come into force on 01.01.2022.)

### 154.pants. Taking of Hostages

(1) For the seizure or detention of a person as a hostage, if it is related to threats to kill, inflict bodily injury or further detain this person with the purpose of forcing a natural or legal person or a group of persons to commit an action or to refrain from it, making this a condition for the release of the hostage, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term from two to ten years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property, and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years or without it.

(2) For the same acts, if they have been committed against a minor or if they have been committed by a group of persons pursuant to prior agreement, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term from three to twelve years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property, and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years.

(3) For the acts specified in paragraph one of this Section, if they have caused serious consequences or if they have been committed against a young child, or if they have been committed by an organised group, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term from five to fifteen years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property, and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years.

(With amendments made by the Laws of 08.12.2005., 13.12.2007., 08.07.2011. and 13.12.2012., which come into force on 01.04.2013. See paragraph 11 of the transitional provisions)

154.1 pants. Trafficking in Human Beings

(1) For trafficking in human beings — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to eight years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property.

(2) For trafficking in human beings, if it has been committed against a minor or if it has been committed by a group of persons pursuant to prior agreement, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term from three to twelve years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property, and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years or without it.

(3) For trafficking in human beings, if it has endangered the victim's life or caused serious consequences or if it has been committed with particular cruelty or against a young child, or if it has been committed by an organised group, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term from five to fifteen years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property, and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years or without it.

(As worded by the Law of 25.04.2002. with amendments made by the Laws of 16.12.2004., 13.12.2007., 08.07.2011., 13.12.2012. and 14.03.2013., which come into force on 01.04.2013. See paragraph 11 of the transitional provisions)

154.2 pants. Concept of Trafficking in Human Beings

(1) Trafficking in human beings is the recruitment, transportation, transfer, harbouring, accommodating or receipt of persons for the purpose of exploitation, using violence or threats, or abduction by deceit or by exploiting the person's dependence on the perpetrator or their vulnerable or helpless state, or by giving or receiving material or other benefits in order to obtain the consent to trafficking of the person on whom the victim is dependent.

(2) The recruitment, transportation, transfer, harbouring, accommodating or receipt of a minor for the purpose of exploitation shall be recognised as trafficking in human beings also in such cases where it is not related to the use of any of the means referred to in paragraph one of this Section.

(3) Exploitation in the meaning of this Section is the involvement of a person in prostitution or other forms of sexual exploitation, forcing them to perform work, provide services or commit criminal offences, keeping them in slavery or other similar forms (debt bondage, serfdom or other forms of forced placing of a person into dependence on another person), keeping them in servitude or the unlawful removal of a person's tissues or organs.

(4) Vulnerable state in the meaning of this Section means that circumstances are exploited where the person has no other real or acceptable choice but to submit to exploitation.

(As worded by the Law of 25.04.2002. with amendments made by the Laws of 13.12.2012. and 25.09.2014., which come into force

### 155.pants. Unlawful Placement in a Psychiatric Hospital

For deliberately unlawful placement of a person in a psychiatric hospital — punishable by short-term deprivation of liberty or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine, with deprivation of the right to engage in specific activities for a period of up to five years.

(With amendments made by the laws of 12.02.2004., 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which come into force on 01.01.2022.)

### 156.pants. Defamation of Honour

(Deleted by the law of 19.11.2009., which comes into force on 23.12.2009.)

### 157.pants. Defamation

(1) For deliberate dissemination of knowingly false, defamatory fabrications about another person in a printed or otherwise reproduced work, as well as orally, if done publicly (defamation), — punishable by probation supervision or community service or a fine.

(2) For defamation in a mass media — punishable by short-term deprivation of liberty or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine. (In the version of the law of 12.06.2003. with amendments made by the laws of 12.02.2004., 19.11.2009., 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which come into force on 01.01.2022.)

### 158.pants. Defamation of Honour and Defamation in Mass Media

(Deleted by the law of 19.11.2009., which comes into force on 23.12.2009.)

## XVI nodaļa

Criminal Offences Against Morality and Sexual Inviolability

### 159.pants. Rape

(1) For sexual intercourse by exploiting the victim's state of helplessness or for sexual intercourse against the victim's will, using violence, threats or exploiting trust, authority or other influence over the victim (rape), — punishable by deprivation of liberty for a term of four to ten years and probation supervision for a term of up to five years.

(2) For rape committed by a group of persons, or for rape of a minor — punishable by life imprisonment or deprivation of liberty for a term of five to twenty years and probation supervision for a term of up to five years.

(3) For rape that has caused serious consequences, or for rape of a person who has not reached the age of sixteen, — punishable by life imprisonment or deprivation of liberty for a term of ten to twenty years and probation supervision for a term of up to five years.

(In the version of the law of 15.05.2014. with amendments made by the law of 12.11.2015., which comes into force on 02.12.2015.)

### 160.pants. Sexual Violence

(1) For sexual actions for the purpose of satisfying one's sexual desire in physical contact with the victim's body, if done by exploiting the victim's state of helplessness or against the victim's will, using violence, threats or exploiting trust, authority or other influence over the victim, — punishable by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to seven years and probation supervision for a term of up to five years.

(2) For anal or oral acts or satisfaction of sexual desire in an unnatural way involving vaginal, anal or oral penetration of the victim's body, if done by exploiting the victim's state of helplessness or against the victim's will, using violence, threats or exploiting trust, authority or other influence over the victim, — punishable by deprivation of liberty for a term of four to ten years and probation supervision for a term of up to five years.

(3) For the criminal offence provided for in paragraph one of this Article, if committed by a group of persons or if committed against a minor, — punishable by deprivation of liberty for a term of three to twelve years and probation supervision for a term of up to five years.

(4) For the criminal offence provided for in paragraph one of this Article, if it has caused serious consequences or if committed against a person who has not reached the age of sixteen, — punishable by deprivation of liberty for a term of five to fifteen years and probation supervision for a term of up to five years.

(5) For the criminal offence provided for in paragraph two of this Article, if committed by a group of persons or if committed against a minor, — punishable by life imprisonment or deprivation of liberty for a term of five to twenty years and probation supervision for a term of up to five years.

(6) For the criminal offence provided for in paragraph two of this Article, if it has caused serious consequences or if committed against a person who has not reached the age of sixteen, — punishable by life imprisonment or deprivation of liberty for a term of ten to twenty years and probation supervision for a term of up to five years.

(In the version of the law of 15.05.2014. with amendments made by the laws of 25.09.2014. and 12.11.2015., which come into force on

### 161.pants. Sexual Acts with a Person Who Has Not Reached the Age of Sixteen

For committing sexual intercourse, anal or oral acts or satisfaction of sexual desire in an unnatural way, or committing other sexual actions in physical contact with the victim's body, if done with a person who has not reached the age of sixteen, and committed by an adult, — punishable by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or short-term deprivation of liberty, or probation supervision, or community service.

(In the version of the law of 15.05.2014. with amendments made by the laws of 12.11.2015. and 17.12.2020., which come into force on

### 162.pants. Enticement to Debauchery

(1) For enticing a person who has not reached the age of sixteen or a person in a state of helplessness to debauchery, that is, for committing sexual actions without physical contact with the victim's body for the purpose of satisfying one's sexual desire or arousing sexual instinct in the victim, if committed by an adult or if done by exploiting the victim's state of helplessness or against the victim's will, using violence, threats or exploiting trust, authority or other influence over the victim, — punishable by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or short-term deprivation of liberty, or probation supervision.

(2) For the criminal offence provided for in paragraph one of this Article, if it has caused serious consequences or if committed against a minor child, — punishable by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to seven years and probation supervision for a term of up to five years.

(In the version of the law of 15.05.2014. with amendments made by the laws of 12.11.2015. and 17.12.2020., which come into force on

162.1 pants. Inducement to Engage in Sexual Activities

(1) For inducing a person who has not reached the age of sixteen to engage in sexual activities or inducing such person to meet for the purpose of committing sexual acts or engaging in sexual relations, using information or communication technologies or other means of communication, if committed by an adult, — punishable by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to four years or short-term deprivation of liberty, or probation supervision.

(2) For the criminal offence provided for in paragraph one of this Article, if committed against a minor child, — punishable by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or short-term deprivation of liberty, or probation supervision.

(In the version of the law of 15.05.2014. with amendments made by the laws of 12.11.2015. and 17.12.2020., which come into force on

### 163.pants. Violation of Provisions Restricting Prostitution

(Deleted by the law of 13.12.2012., which comes into force on 01.04.2013.)

163.1 pants. Establishment, Maintenance, Management and Financing of a Brothel

For establishment, maintenance, management or financing of a brothel (an unauthorised place for organising and providing prostitution services) — punishable by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to three years or short-term deprivation of liberty, or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.

(In the version of the law of 21.05.2009. with amendments made by the laws of 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which come into force on

### 164.pants. Involvement of Persons in Prostitution and Exploitation of Prostitution

(1) For involving a person in prostitution — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to three years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property.

(2) For coercing into engaging in prostitution or for involving a person in prostitution by maliciously abusing their trust or by fraud, or by exploiting the person's dependence on the perpetrator or their state of helplessness, or for knowingly exploiting the prostitution of a victim of human trafficking — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property.

(3) For the acts provided for in paragraph one or two of this Section, if committed by a group of persons, or for inciting, involving or coercing a minor into engaging in prostitution, or for providing premises to minors for prostitution — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term from three to eight years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property, and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years or without it.

(31) For exploitation of prostitution of a minor — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision.

(4) For inciting, involving or coercing a person who has not reached sixteen years of age into engaging in prostitution — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term from five to twelve years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property, and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years.

(5) For the acts provided for in this Section, if committed by an organised group, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term from five to fifteen years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property, and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years.

(With amendments made by the laws of 18.05.2000., 12.02.2004., 13.12.2007., 21.05.2009., 08.07.2011., 13.12.2012., 15.05.2014., 11.06.2020. and 17.12.2020., which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

### 165.pants. Pimping

(1) For exploiting a person who engages in prostitution for the purpose of subsistence, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property.

(2) For the same acts, if committed by a group of persons or if committed in relation to a minor, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to eight years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property, and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years.

(3) For the same acts, if committed by an organised group or if committed in relation to a person who has not reached sixteen years of age, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term from five to fifteen years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property, and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years.

(With amendments made by the laws of 18.05.2000., 13.12.2007., 21.05.2009., 08.07.2011., 13.12.2012., 15.05.2014. and 17.12.2020., which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

165.1 pants. Sending of a Person for Sexual Exploitation

(1) For sending a person with their consent for sexual exploitation, that is, for any actions that for this purpose facilitate legal or illegal movement, transit or stay of a person in the territory of one or several countries, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2) For the same acts, if committed for the purpose of subsistence or if committed by a group of persons by prior agreement, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to ten years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property, and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years or without it.

(3) For the acts provided for in paragraph one or two of this Section, if committed by an organised group, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term from five to fifteen years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property, and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years.

(In the wording of the law of 18.05.2000. with amendments made by the laws of 16.12.2004., 13.12.2007., 21.05.2009., 08.07.2011., 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

165.2 pants. Sending Abroad

(Deleted by the law of 21.05.2009., which enters into force on 01.07.2009.)

### 166.pants. Violation of Regulations on Demonstration of Pornographic Performance, Restriction of Entertainment of an Intimate Nature and Circulation of Pornographic Material

(1) For violation of regulations on demonstration of pornographic performances or other entertainment of an intimate nature or for violation of regulations on circulation of pornographic material, if committed on a significant scale or if it has caused substantial harm, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to one year or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2) For attending or demonstrating a pornographic performance or for circulation of pornographic material that contains child pornography, sexual acts of a human with an animal, necrophilia or satisfaction of sexual desire in a violent manner, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to three years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property.

(3) For inciting, involving, coercing a minor to participate in or exploiting them in a pornographic performance or in the production of pornographic material — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to six years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property, and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years.

(4) For inciting, involving, coercing a person who has not reached sixteen years of age to participate in or exploiting them in a pornographic performance or in the production of pornographic material — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term from three to twelve years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property, and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years.

(5) For the acts provided for in paragraph three or four of this Section, if committed by an organised group or committed using violence, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term from five to fifteen years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property, and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years.

(In the wording of the law of 15.05.2014. with amendments made by the laws of 29.10.2015. and 17.12.2020., which enters into force

## XVII nodaļa

Criminal Offences against the Family and Minors

### 167.pants. Substitution of a Child

(1) For intentional substitution of a newborn child — shall be punished by short-term deprivation of liberty or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2) For substitution of a newborn child for a mercenary purpose — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to two years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(With amendments made by the laws of 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

### 168.pants. Non-fulfillment of Decisions on Custody, Care and Contact Rights with a Child

For evasion of the execution of a court or orphan's court decision arising from custody, care or contact rights with a child, as well as a court decision providing for the return of a child to the country of his or her residence, or malicious non-fulfillment of such a decision, or malicious obstruction of the execution of such a decision — shall be punished by short-term deprivation of liberty or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine. (In the wording of the law of 21.10.2010 with amendments made by the laws of 08.07.2011., 13.12.2012., 29.10.2015. and 17.12.2020., which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

168.1 pants. Non-fulfillment of a Decision on Protection against Violence

For malicious non-fulfillment of a decision on protection against violence — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a period of up to three years or short-term deprivation of liberty, or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.

(In the wording of the law of 20.03.2014 with amendments made by the laws of 29.10.2015., 17.12.2020. and 15.06.2023., which

### 169.pants. Disclosure of Adoption Secrets

For disclosure of an adoption secret against the will of the adoptee — shall be punished by short-term deprivation of liberty or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.

(With amendments made by the laws of 12.02.2004., 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

169.1 pants. Unlawful Actions in Adoption Arrangements

(1) For giving consent to the adoption of a minor, if given by the mother, father or guardian of such minor with mercenary intent, — shall be punished by short-term deprivation of liberty or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.

(2) For requesting consent to the adoption of a minor from the mother, father or guardian of such minor personally or through an intermediary, using violence, threats, fraud, bribery or other unlawful means, as well as for such mediation — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a period of up to two years or short-term deprivation of liberty, or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.

(3) For the actions provided for in paragraph two of this Section, if committed by a group of persons by prior agreement, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a period of up to five years or short-term deprivation of liberty, or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.

(In the wording of the law of 21.06.2007 with amendments made by the laws of 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which enters into force on

### 170.pants. Evasion of Maintenance
