For evasion of the execution of a court decision or a decision of a competent state institution imposing the obligation to support and provide maintenance to one's parents, grandparents, children, grandchildren or other persons, — shall be punished by short-term deprivation of liberty or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine. (With amendments made by the laws of 12.02.2004., 13.12.2012., 29.10.2015., 08.06.2017. and 17.12.2020., which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

### 171.pants. Malicious Use of Guardian's Rights

For the use of guardianship or trusteeship to the detriment of the ward or person under guardianship, — shall be punished by short-term deprivation of liberty or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.

(With amendments made by the laws of 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

### 172.pants. Involvement of a Minor in a Criminal Offense

For involvement of a minor in a criminal offense — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a period of up to two years or short-term deprivation of liberty, or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.

(With amendments made by the laws of 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

### 173.pants. Bringing a Minor to a State of Intoxication, Involvement in Non-medical Use of Medicinal and Other

Substances that Cause Intoxication

(1) (Deleted by the law of 13.12.2012.)

(2) For intentionally bringing a minor to a state of intoxication or for involving a minor in the non-medical use of medicinal or other substances that are not narcotic or psychotropic substances but cause intoxication, if committed using violence or threats, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a period of up to two years or short-term deprivation of liberty, or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.

(With amendments made by the laws of 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

### 174.pants. Cruelty and Violence against a Minor

(1) For cruel or violent treatment of a minor, if it has caused physical or mental suffering to the minor and if it has been committed by persons on whom the victim is materially or otherwise dependent and if these actions have not had the consequences provided for in Sections 125 or 126 of this law, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a period of up to three years or short-term deprivation of liberty, or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.

(2) For the criminal offense provided for in paragraph one of this Section, if committed against a young child, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a period of up to five years or short-term deprivation of liberty, or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.

(With amendments made by the laws of 30.10.2008., 08.07.2011., 13.12.2012., 17.12.2020. and 07.01.2021., which enters into force on 02.02.2021.)

## XVIII nodaļa

Criminal Offenses against Property

### 175.pants. Theft

(1) For secret or open misappropriation of another's movable property (theft) — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a period of up to two years or short-term deprivation of liberty, or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.

(2) For theft, if committed on a significant scale or if committed by a group of persons by prior agreement, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a period of up to four years or short-term deprivation of liberty, or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property.

(3) For theft, if committed by entering a vehicle, dwelling or other premises, or if committed from a storage facility or a device connecting storage facilities, as well as for theft of a vehicle — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a period of up to five years or short-term deprivation of liberty, or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property.

(4) For theft, if committed on a large scale or if committed by an organised group, as well as for theft of narcotic, psychotropic, highly potent, toxic or radioactive substances, explosives, firearms or ammunition — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a period from two to ten years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property, and with probation supervision for a period of up to three years.

(With amendments made by the laws of 25.04.2002., 08.07.2011., 13.12.2012., 06.06.2019. and 17.12.2020., which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

### 176.pants. Robbery

(1) For the robbery of another's movable property, if it is associated with violence or the threat of violence (robbery), — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property.

(2) For robbery, if it has been committed in a considerable amount or if it has been committed by a group of persons by prior agreement, or if it has been committed by entering a vehicle, dwelling or other premises, or if it has been committed from a depository or a device connecting depositories, as well as for the robbery of a vehicle — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to eight years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years.

(3) For robbery, if it has been committed in a large amount or if it has been committed by an organized group, as well as for the robbery of narcotic, psychotropic, potent, toxic or radioactive substances, explosives, firearms or ammunition — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of three to twelve years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property, and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years.

(4) For robbery, if it has been committed using firearms or explosive substances, or if it is associated with inflicting serious bodily injury on the victim, or if it has caused other serious consequences, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of five to fifteen years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property, and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years.

(With amendments made by the laws of 25.04.2002., 12.02.2004., 08.07.2011., 13.12.2012., 06.06.2019. and 17.12.2020., which enter into force on 01.01.2022.)

### 177.pants. Fraud

(1) For the acquisition of another's property or rights to such property by abuse of trust or by deceit (fraud), — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to three years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2) For fraud, if it has been committed in a considerable amount or if it has been committed by a group of persons by prior agreement, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property.

(3) For fraud, if it has been committed in a large amount or if it has been committed by an organized group, or if it has been committed by acquiring narcotic, psychotropic, potent, toxic or radioactive substances, explosives, firearms or ammunition, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of two to ten years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property, and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years or without it.

(With amendments made by the laws of 12.02.2004., 13.12.2007., 08.07.2011., 13.12.2012., 17.12.2020. and 07.04.2022., which enter into force on 04.05.2022.)

177.1 pants. Fraud in an automated data processing system

(1) For the acquisition of another's property or rights to such property, or other material benefits, by knowingly entering false data into an automated data processing system in order to influence the operation of its resources (computer fraud), — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to three years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2) For computer fraud, if it has been committed by a group of persons by prior agreement, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property.

(3) For computer fraud, if it has been committed in a large amount or if it has been committed by an organized group, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of two to ten years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property, and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years or without it.

(In the wording of the law of 28.04.2005. with amendments made by the laws of 13.12.2007., 08.07.2011., 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which enter into force on 01.01.2022.)

### 178.pants. Insurance fraud

(1) For the intentional destruction, damage or concealment of one's own property with the aim of receiving insurance compensation — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to two years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2) For forcing or persuading another person to destroy, damage or conceal insured property or other influence with the same aim, if it has been committed by the owner of the property with the aim of receiving the insurance sum, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to three years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(3) For the acts provided for in paragraph one or two of this Article, if they have been committed with the aim of receiving the insurance sum in a large amount, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(With amendments made by the laws of 12.02.2004., 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which enter into force on 01.01.2022.)

### 179.pants. Misappropriation

(1) For the unlawful acquisition or squandering of another's property, if it has been committed by a person to whom this property was entrusted or in whose charge it was (misappropriation), — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to two years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property.

(2) For misappropriation, if it has been committed in a considerable amount or if it has been committed by a group of persons by prior agreement, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property.

(3) For misappropriation, if it has been committed in a large amount, as well as for the misappropriation of narcotic, psychotropic, potent, toxic or radioactive substances, explosives, firearms or ammunition — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to ten years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property, and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years or without it.

(With amendments made by the laws of 12.02.2004., 13.12.2012., 17.12.2020. and 07.04.2022., which enter into force on

### 180.pants. Theft, fraud, misappropriation in a minor amount

(1) For theft, fraud or misappropriation in a minor amount, except for the offences provided for in paragraphs three and four of Article 175, paragraph three of Article 177 and paragraph three of Article 179 of this law, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to one year or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2) (Deleted by the law of 13.12.2012.)

(In the wording of the law of 15.01.2004. with amendments made by the laws of 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which enter into force on

### 181.pants. Repeated theft, fraud, misappropriation

(Deleted by the law of 13.12.2012., which enters into force on 01.04.2013.)

### 182.pants. Unauthorized Consumption of Electricity, Thermal Energy and Gas, Unauthorized Use of Electronic Communications Services

(1) For unauthorized consumption of electricity, thermal energy or gas or for unauthorized use of electronic communications services, if committed on a significant scale, — shall be punished by short-term deprivation of liberty or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.

(2) For unauthorized consumption of electricity, thermal energy or gas or for unauthorized use of electronic communications services, if committed on a large scale, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to two years or short-term deprivation of liberty, or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.

(21.06.2007. likuma redakcijā ar grozījumiem, kas izdarīti ar 13.12.2012., 29.10.2015. un 17.12.2020. likumu, kas stājas spēkā 01.01.2022.)

182.1 pants. Unlawful Actions with Commercial Metering of Consumed Electricity, Thermal Energy and Gas

(1) For interference with the operation of commercial metering devices for electricity, thermal energy or gas or for disruption thereof, or for manufacturing, adapting, distributing or installing equipment, devices or software, if it enables persons to consume electricity, thermal energy or gas without authorization, — shall be punished by short-term deprivation of liberty or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.

(2) For the same actions, if committed by a group of persons pursuant to prior agreement or if they have caused serious consequences, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or short-term deprivation of liberty, or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.

(3) For the actions specified in paragraph one of this Article, if they have caused the death of two or more persons, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to eight years.

(21.10.2010. likuma redakcijā ar grozījumiem, kas izdarīti ar 13.12.2012., 25.09.2014. un 17.12.2020. likumu, kas stājas spēkā 01.01.2022.)

### 183.pants. Extortion

(1) For a demand without legal basis to surrender property or rights to property or to perform any actions of a proprietary nature, threatening violence against the victim or their relatives, threatening to disclose disgraceful information about the victim or their relatives, threatening to destroy their property or cause them other substantial harm (extortion), — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or short-term deprivation of liberty, or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property.

(2) For extortion, if committed by a group of persons pursuant to prior agreement or if committed using violence, weapons or explosives, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to ten years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property, and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years.

(Ar grozījumiem, kas izdarīti ar 08.07.2011., 13.12.2012. un 17.12.2020. likumu, kas stājas spēkā 01.01.2022.)

### 184.pants. Extortion by an Organised Group

(1) For establishing an organised group or participation therein for the purpose of extortion — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to eight years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years.

(2) For extortion as part of an organised group, if the extortion was committed using violence or threats, weapons or explosives, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term from three to twelve years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property, and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years.

(3) For the actions specified in paragraph two of this Article, if they have caused serious consequences, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term from five to fifteen years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property, and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years.

(Ar grozījumiem, kas izdarīti ar 08.07.2011. un 13.12.2012. likumu, kas stājas spēkā 01.04.2013. Sk. pārejas noteikumu 11.punktu)

### 185.pants. Intentional Destruction and Damage of Property

(1) For intentional destruction or damage of another's property — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to two years or short-term deprivation of liberty, or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.

(2) For intentional destruction or damage of another's property, if committed by burning or in another generally dangerous manner or if committed on a large scale, or if as a result thereof death of a person has occurred through negligence of the guilty person or other serious consequences have been caused, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to ten years and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years or without it.

(Ar grozījumiem, kas izdarīti ar 12.02.2004., 13.12.2012., 29.10.2015. un 17.12.2020. likumu, kas stājas spēkā

### 186.pants. Destruction and Damage of Property by Negligence

(1) For destruction or damage of another's property by negligence, by careless handling of fire or in another generally dangerous manner and if as a result thereof substantial harm has been caused, — shall be punished by short-term deprivation of liberty or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.

(2) For destruction or damage of another's property by negligence, if as a result thereof death of a person has occurred or other serious consequences have been caused, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to three years or short-term deprivation of liberty, or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.

(3) For destruction or damage of another's property by negligence, if as a result thereof death of two or more persons has occurred, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or short-term deprivation of liberty, or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.

(Ar grozījumiem, kas izdarīti ar 12.02.2004., 13.12.2012., 25.09.2014., 11.06.2020. un 17.12.2020. likumu, kas stājas spēkā 01.01.2022.)

### 187.pants. Intentional Destruction and Damage of Electricity Network, Public Electronic Communications Network, Heat Network, Gas, Oil and Oil Product Pipeline

(1) For intentional destruction or damage of an electricity network, public electronic communications network, heat network, gas, oil or oil product pipeline or an installation thereof — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to three years or short-term deprivation of liberty, or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.

(2) For the same actions, if as a result thereof death of a person has occurred or if they have caused an accident, catastrophe or other serious consequences, or if committed by an organised group, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term from two to ten years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property, and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years or without it.

(Ar grozījumiem, kas izdarīti ar 13.12.2007., 21.10.2010., 08.07.2011., 13.12.2012. un 17.12.2020. likumu, kas stājas spēkā 01.01.2022.)

### 188.pants. Destruction and Damage of Gas and Oil Pipelines by Negligence

(1) For destruction or damage of gas, oil or oil product pipelines and their installations by negligence — shall be punished by short-term deprivation of liberty or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.

(2) For the same actions, if as a result thereof death of a person has occurred or if they have caused an accident, catastrophe or other serious consequences, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or short-term deprivation of liberty, or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.

(3) For the actions specified in paragraph one of this Article, if as a result thereof death of two or more persons has occurred, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to eight years.

(Ar grozījumiem, kas izdarīti ar 12.02.2004., 21.10.2010., 13.12.2012., 25.09.2014. un 17.12.2020. likumu, kas stājas spēkā 01.01.2022.)

### 189.pants. Unconscientious and Careless Protection of Property

(Izslēgts ar 11.06.2020. likumu, kas stājas spēkā 06.07.2020.)

## XIX nodaļa

Criminal Offences in the National Economy

### 190.pants. Smuggling

(1) For the importation into the customs territory of the Republic of Latvia or exportation from it of goods subject to customs duties or other valuables, bypassing customs control or concealing them from such control, or without declaring them, or using forged customs or other documents, or in any other unlawful manner (smuggling), if committed on a considerable scale, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to one year or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property.

(2) For the criminal offence provided for in paragraph one of this Article, if committed by a group of persons pursuant to prior agreement, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to four years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property.

(3) For the criminal offence provided for in paragraph one of this Article, if committed by an organised group, or for smuggling, if committed on a large scale, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to ten years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property, and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years or without it, with deprivation of the right to engage in specific or all types of commercial activity or in specific occupation or the right to hold a specific position for a term of up to five years.

(4) (Deleted by the law of 13.12.2012.)

(28.04.2005. likuma redakcijā ar grozījumiem, kas izdarīti ar 13.12.2007., 13.12.2012., 29.10.2015., 08.06.2017., 03.09.2020. un 17.12.2020. likumu, kas stājas spēkā 01.01.2022.)

190.1 pants. Movement of goods and substances the circulation of which is prohibited or specially regulated across the state border of the Republic of Latvia

(1) For the movement across the state border of the Republic of Latvia in any unlawful manner of a narcotic or psychotropic substance, precursor intended for the manufacture of such substances, a new psychoactive substance or a product containing it, the circulation of which is prohibited or restricted, as well as of a radioactive or hazardous substance, strategic goods or other valuables, explosives, weapons, ammunition — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property.

(2) For the same acts, if committed by a group of persons pursuant to prior agreement or if committed on a large scale, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to ten years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property.

(3) For the same acts, if committed by an organised group, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to twelve years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property, and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years, with deprivation of the right to engage in specific or all types of commercial activity or in specific occupation or the right to hold a specific position for a term of up to five years.

(28.04.2005. likuma redakcijā ar grozījumiem, kas izdarīti ar 21.06.2007., 13.12.2007., 19.11.2009., 08.07.2011., 13.12.2012., 25.09.2014. un 17.12.2020. likumu, kas stājas spēkā 01.01.2022.)

### 191.pants. Unauthorised activities with goods subject to customs duties and other valuables

(1) For the storage, movement, transfer or sale in the customs territory of the Republic of Latvia of goods subject to customs duties or other valuables without the permission of the customs authorities, if committed on a considerable scale, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to two years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property.

(2) For the criminal offence provided for in paragraph one of this Article, if committed by a group of persons pursuant to prior agreement, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to three years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property.

(3) For the storage, movement, transfer or sale in the customs territory of the Republic of Latvia of goods subject to customs duties or other valuables without the permission of the customs authorities, if committed on a large scale, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to six years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property, and with probation supervision or without it, with deprivation of the right to engage in specific or all types of commercial activity or in specific occupation or the right to hold a specific position for a term of up to five years.

(28.04.2005. likuma redakcijā ar grozījumiem, kas izdarīti ar 13.12.2012., 29.10.2015., 03.09.2020. un 17.12.2020. likumu, kas stājas spēkā 01.01.2022.)

### 192.pants.

Manufacture, distribution, transportation, transfer, acquisition and storage of counterfeit money and state financial instruments

(1) For the transportation, transfer, acquisition or storage for the purpose of distribution of counterfeit banknotes, coins, state financial instruments or foreign currency in circulation in the Republic of Latvia or intended for circulation, or for the manufacture thereof, as well as for the distribution of such counterfeit — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property.

(2) For the manufacture of counterfeit banknotes, coins, state financial instruments or foreign currency in circulation in the Republic of Latvia or intended for circulation, or for the distribution of such counterfeit, if committed on a considerable scale, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to eight years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property.

(3) For the acts provided for in paragraph one of this Article, if committed on a large scale or if committed by an organised group, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term from two to ten years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property, and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years or without it.

(Ar grozījumiem, kas izdarīti ar 10.04.2003., 18.12.2003., 05.05.2005., 13.12.2007., 08.07.2011., 13.12.2012., 28.01.2016. un 17.12.2020. likumu, kas stājas spēkā 01.01.2022.)

192.1 pants. Manufacture, acquisition, storage and distribution of equipment, software, data and other means adapted for counterfeiting money

For the manufacture, acquisition, storage or distribution of such equipment, software, data, security (anti-counterfeiting) elements or any other means adapted for counterfeiting banknotes, coins, state financial instruments or foreign currency in circulation in the Republic of Latvia or intended for circulation, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to three years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(28.01.2016. likuma redakcijā ar grozījumiem, kas izdarīti ar 17.12.2020. likumu, kas stājas spēkā 01.01.2022.)

### Section 193. Illegal Actions with Financial Instruments and Means of Payment

(1) (Deleted by the law of 28.01.2016.)

(2) For the robbery, destruction, damage or illegal use of another person's financial instrument or means of payment, or for the acquisition, storage, transportation or distribution of a stolen means of payment — shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of up to five years, or by short-term imprisonment, or by community service, or by a fine, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property.

(3) For the forgery of a financial instrument or means of payment, as well as for the acquisition, storage, transportation, distribution or use of such a forgery, if there are no elements of the crime provided for in Section 192 of this law, — shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of up to seven years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property.

(4) For the actions provided for in paragraph two or three of this Section, if they have been committed on a large scale or if they have been committed by an organized group, — shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of two to ten years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property, and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years or without it.

(In the wording of the law of 18.12.2003. with amendments made by the laws of 13.12.2007., 08.07.2011., 13.12.2012., 29.10.2015., 28.01.2016., 06.07.2021. and 17.12.2020., which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

Section 193.1 Acquisition, production, distribution, use and storage of data, software and equipment for illegal actions with financial instruments and means of payment

(1) For the acquisition, transportation or distribution of data that enables the illegal use of a financial instrument or means of payment, — shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of up to three years, or by short-term imprisonment, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2) For the use of data that enables the illegal use of a financial instrument or means of payment, or for the production or adaptation of software or equipment for the commission of crimes provided for in Section 193 of this law, as well as for the acquisition, transportation, storage or distribution of such software or equipment for the same purpose — shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of up to five years, or by short-term imprisonment, or by community service, or by a fine, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property.

(3) For the actions provided for in paragraph one or two of this Section, if they have been committed by an organized group, — shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of two to ten years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property, and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years.

(In the wording of the law of 01.06.2000. with amendments made by the laws of 18.12.2003., 13.12.2007., 08.07.2011., 13.12.2012., 06.07.2021. and 17.12.2020., which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

Section 193.2 Illegal use of inside information and market manipulation in financial markets

(1) For the illegal use of inside information in financial markets, recommending to another person or inducing another person to engage in the illegal use of inside information in financial markets, as well as for market manipulation in financial markets, if it has caused serious consequences, — shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of up to four years, or by short-term imprisonment, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2) For the criminal offense provided for in paragraph one of this Section, if it has been committed by an organized group, — shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of two to ten years and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years or without it.

(In the wording of the law of 28.01.2016. with amendments made by the law of 17.12.2020., which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

### Section 194. Unauthorized issue of financial instruments

(1) For placing financial instruments of a legal person in circulation before the legal person has commenced operations or without the registration of financial instruments required by law, or for the provision of deliberately false information regarding the placing of financial instruments in public circulation — shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of up to two years, or by short-term imprisonment, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2) For the production and placing in circulation of financial instruments if they do not comply with the provisions of the articles of association, the prospectus or other document issued for this purpose, or for the issue of a deposit certificate without acceptance of the respective deposit — shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of up to five years, or by short-term imprisonment, or by community service, or by a fine.

(With amendments made by the laws of 18.12.2003., 12.02.2004., 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which enters into force Section 194.1 Dissemination of false data or information about the state of the financial system of the Republic of Latvia

(1) For the dissemination of deliberately false data or information orally, in writing or in any other way about the state of the financial system of the Republic of Latvia — shall be punished by short-term imprisonment, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2) For the same actions, if they have been committed by a group of persons by prior agreement or if they have caused substantial harm, — shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of up to three years, or by short-term imprisonment, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(3) For the actions provided for in paragraph one of this Section, if they have been committed with mercenary intent, — shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of up to five years, or by short-term imprisonment, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property. (In the wording of the law of 13.12.2007. with amendments made by the laws of 13.12.2012., 17.12.2020. and 07.04.2022., which enters into force on 04.05.2022.)

### 195.pants. Legalization of Criminally Obtained Funds

(1) For the legalization of criminally obtained financial resources or other property — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to four years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property.

(2) For the same acts, if committed by a group of persons by prior agreement, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property.

(3) For the acts provided for in the first paragraph of this Section, if committed on a large scale or if committed by an organized group, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of three to twelve years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property, and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years or without it.

(28.04.2005. likuma redakcijā ar grozījumiem, kas izdarīti ar 13.12.2007., 08.07.2011., 13.12.2012., 06.06.2019. un 17.12.2020. likumu, kas stājas spēkā 01.01.2022.)

195.1 pants. Failure to Provide Information and Provision of False Information about Ownership of Funds and Beneficial Owner

(1) For the deliberate provision of false information to a natural or legal person authorized by law to request information about a transaction and the actual owner or beneficial owner of the financial resources or other property involved therein, as well as for failure to provide the information on the beneficial owner prescribed by law or deliberate provision of false information to a state institution or legal person — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to one year or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2) For the same acts, if they have caused substantial harm, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to two years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(13.12.2012. likuma redakcijā ar grozījumiem, kas izdarīti ar 06.06.2019., 17.12.2020. un 07.04.2022. likumu, kas stājas spēkā 04.05.2022.)

195.2 pants. Evasion of Cash Declaration

(1) (Izslēgta ar 13.12.2012. likumu)

(2) For failure to declare or false declaration of a large amount of cash brought into the Republic of Latvia or taken out of it when crossing the internal border of the state, if the completion of the cash declaration in the manner prescribed by law has been requested by an official of the competent authority, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to two years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by community service, or by a fine.

(21) For failure to declare or false declaration of a large amount of cash brought into the Republic of Latvia or taken out of it when crossing the external border of the state — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to three years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by community service, or by a fine.

(3) For the criminal offence provided for in the second and 2.1 paragraphs of this Section, if committed by an organized group, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to four years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by community service, or by a fine.

(08.12.2005. likuma redakcijā ar grozījumiem, kas izdarīti ar 13.12.2012., 08.06.2017., 20.06.2019., 06.07.2021. un 17.12.2020. likumu, kas stājas spēkā 01.01.2022. Grozījums 2.1 daļā par vārda "piespiedu" aizstāšanu ar vārdu "sabiedrisko" stājas spēkā 01.01.2022. Sk. pārejas noteikumu 26. punktu)

### 196.pants. Abuse and Exceeding of Authority

(1) For deliberate acts committed by a responsible employee of an enterprise (company) or organization, that is, a person who has the right in the enterprise (company) or organization to make decisions binding on other persons, or the right to dispose of the property or financial resources of the enterprise (company) or organization, as well as such person authorized by the enterprise (company) or organization, by abusing their authority or exceeding it, if such acts have caused substantial harm, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to one year or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2) For the criminal offence provided for in the first paragraph of this Section, if committed with mercenary intent, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to three years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by community service, or by a fine, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property and deprivation of the right to hold a specific position for a term of up to three years.

(Ar grozījumiem, kas izdarīti ar 11.12.2003., 12.02.2004., 13.12.2012., 17.12.2020. un 07.04.2022. likumu, kas stājas spēkā 04.05.2022.)

### 197.pants. Negligence

For negligent performance of work duties committed by a responsible employee of an enterprise (company) or organization or such person authorized by the enterprise (company) or organization, if it has caused substantial harm, — shall be punished by short-term deprivation of liberty or by community service, or by a fine.

(Ar grozījumiem, kas izdarīti ar 12.02.2004., 13.12.2012., 17.12.2020. un 07.04.2022. likumu, kas stājas spēkā

### 198.pants. Unlawful Acceptance of Benefits

(1) For unlawful acceptance of material values, benefits of a property or other nature, or offers thereof, committed by oneself or through an intermediary by an employee of an enterprise (company) or organization or a person authorized on the basis of law or a legal transaction to conduct another person's affairs, for performing or failing to perform any act by using their authority, irrespective of whether the accepted material values, benefits of a property or other nature are intended for that person or any other person, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to two years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2) For the acts provided for in the first paragraph of this Section, if committed on a large scale or committed by a group of persons by prior agreement, or if material values, benefit of a property or other nature has been requested, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to three years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property.

(3) For unlawful acceptance of material values, benefits of a property or other nature, or offers thereof, committed by oneself or through an intermediary by a responsible employee of an enterprise (company) or organization or such person authorized by the enterprise (company) or organization, or a person authorized on the basis of law or a legal transaction to resolve disputes or make binding decisions, but who is not a public official, for performing or failing to perform any act by using their authority, irrespective of whether the accepted material values, benefits of a property or other nature are intended for that person or any other person, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to four years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property and deprivation of the right to hold a specific position for a term of up to three years.

(4) For the acts provided for in the third paragraph of this Section, if committed on a large scale or committed by a group of persons by prior agreement, or if material values, benefit of a property or other nature has been requested, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property and deprivation of the right to hold a specific position for a term of two to five years.

(16.02.2006. likuma redakcijā ar grozījumiem, kas izdarīti ar 19.11.2009., 13.12.2012., 06.06.2019. un 17.12.2020. likumu, kas stājas spēkā 01.01.2022.)


This excerpt sits under: Izdevējs: Saeima Publicēts: > XIX nodaļa

### 199.pants. Commercial Bribery

(1) For offering or handing over material values, benefits of a proprietary or other nature, personally or through an intermediary, to an employee of an enterprise (commercial company) or organisation or to a person who is authorised on the basis of law or a legal transaction to conduct the affairs of another person, to a responsible employee of an enterprise (commercial company) or organisation or to such a person authorised by an enterprise (commercial company) or organisation, or to a person who is authorised on the basis of law or a legal transaction to resolve disputes, so that, by using their authority, they perform or fail to perform an action, regardless of whether the material values, benefits of a proprietary or other nature are intended for that or any other person, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to three years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2) For the same acts, if they are committed on a large scale, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine and by deprivation of the right to hold a specific position for a term of up to three years.

(Ar grozījumiem, kas izdarīti ar 25.04.2002., 12.02.2004., 19.11.2009., 13.12.2012., 06.06.2019. un 17.12.2020. likumu, kas stājas spēkā 01.01.2022.)

A person who has unlawfully offered or given material values, benefits of a proprietary or other nature, may be released from criminal liability if, after committing the criminal acts, they voluntarily report what has happened and actively facilitate the detection and investigation of the criminal offence.

(19.11.2009. likuma redakcijā ar grozījumiem, kas izdarīti ar 13.12.2012. likumu, kas stājas spēkā 01.04.2013.)

### 200. pants.

Disclosure of Non-Disclosable Information That Is Not Confidential, Secret or Top Secret Information, Unauthorised Acquisition and Disclosure of Information Containing Trade Secrets and Illegal Disclosure of Financial Market Insider Information

(1) For disclosure of non-disclosable information that is not confidential, secret or top secret information, if it has been done by a person who is not a State official and who is responsible for the storage of information in accordance with law, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to one year or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2) For unauthorised acquisition of a trade secret for one's own or another person's use or disclosure, or for unauthorised disclosure of a trade secret to another person for the same purpose, as well as for illegal disclosure of financial market insider information — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to two years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by community service, or by a fine.

(3) For theft of the information referred to in the first or second paragraph of this Article — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to three years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by community service, or by a fine.

(26.05.2005. likuma redakcijā ar grozījumiem, kas izdarīti ar 13.12.2012., 28.01.2016., 06.06.2019., 17.12.2020. un 20.04.2023. likumu, kas stājas spēkā 01.05.2023.)

### 201.pants. Usury

For various types of loans that have been made by deliberately exploiting the difficult financial situation of the borrower and the conditions of which are excessively onerous for them (usury), — shall be punished by short-term deprivation of liberty or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(Ar grozījumiem, kas izdarīti ar 12.02.2004., 13.12.2012. un 17.12.2020. likumu, kas stājas spēkā 01.01.2022.)

### 202.pants. Failure to Ensure Quality of Goods and Services

For deliberate manufacture and sale of such goods or provision of such a service to a consumer that does not comply with the quality requirements set out in regulatory enactments, regulatory technical documents or contracts, as a result of which significant harm has been caused, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to two years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by community service, or by a fine.

(Ar grozījumiem, kas izdarīti ar 12.02.2004., 13.12.2007., 13.12.2012., 17.12.2020. un 07.04.2022. likumu, kas stājas spēkā 04.05.2022.)

### 203.pants. Non-Compliance with Safety Requirements for Goods and Services

For non-compliance with the safety requirements for goods or services set out in regulatory enactments, regulatory technical documents, contracts or regulations approved by appropriately authorised State institutions, as a result of which significant harm has been caused, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to two years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by community service, or by a fine.

(Ar grozījumiem, kas izdarīti ar 12.02.2004., 13.12.2007., 13.12.2012., 17.12.2020. un 07.04.2022. likumu, kas stājas spēkā 04.05.2022.)

### 204.pants. Fraud of Buyers and Customers

(Izslēgts ar 13.12.2012. likumu, kas stājas spēkā 01.04.2013.)

### 205.pants. Violation of Trade Regulations

(1) (Izslēgta ar 13.12.2012. likumu)

(2) For deliberate violation of trade regulations issued by State institutions, if significant harm has been caused thereby, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to one year or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by community service, or by a fine.

(25.04.2002. likuma redakcijā ar grozījumiem, kas izdarīti ar 13.12.2012., 17.12.2020. un 07.04.2022. likumu, kas stājas spēkā 04.05.2022.)

### 206.pants. Illegal Use of a Trademark, Other Distinctive Sign and Design Pattern

(1) For illegal use of a trademark, other distinctive sign of goods or services or a design pattern, counterfeiting of a sign or deliberate use or distribution of a counterfeit sign, if it has been committed on a substantial scale or if significant harm has been caused thereby, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to two years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2) For the criminal offence provided for in the first paragraph of this Article, if it has been committed by a group of persons by prior conspiracy, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to four years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by community service, or by a fine.

(3) For illegal use of a trademark, other distinctive sign of goods or services or a design pattern, counterfeiting of a sign or deliberate use or distribution of a counterfeit sign, if it has been committed on a large scale, or for the criminal offence provided for in the first paragraph of this Article, if it has been committed by an organised group, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to six years, by deprivation of the right to engage in specific or all types of commercial activity or a specific occupation or the right to hold a specific position for a term of up to five years, and by probation supervision for a term of up to three years or without it.

(21.10.2010. likuma redakcijā ar grozījumiem, kas izdarīti ar 08.07.2011., 13.12.2012., 29.10.2015., 08.06.2017., 17.12.2020. un 07.04.2022. likumu, kas stājas spēkā 04.05.2022.)

### 207.pants. Business Activities Without Registration and Without a Permit (Licence)

(1) (Izslēgta ar 13.12.2012. likumu)

(2) For business activities without registration or without a special permit (licence), if the need for such is prescribed by law, or for continuation of the activities of an enterprise (commercial company) after an order to suspend its activities, if such business activities or continuation of activities has been committed on a substantial scale or if it has caused significant harm, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to three years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by community service, or by a fine, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property and by deprivation of the right to engage in specific or all types of commercial activity or a specific occupation or the right to hold a specific position for a term from two to five years.

(Ar grozījumiem, kas izdarīti ar 12.02.2004., 13.12.2012., 29.10.2015., 17.12.2020. un 07.04.2022. likumu, kas stājas spēkā 04.05.2022.)

### 208.pants. Prohibited Entrepreneurial Activity

For engaging in entrepreneurial activity in respect of which there is a special prohibition, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a period of up to three years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by community service, or by a fine, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property and with deprivation of the right to engage in a specific or all types of commercial activity or a specific occupation or the right to hold a specific position for a period of two to five years.

(As amended by the Laws of 12.02.2004., 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which comes into force on 01.01.2022.)

### 209.pants. Fictitious Entrepreneurial Activity

(Deleted by the Law of 21.05.2009., which comes into force on 01.07.2009.)

### 210.pants. Fraudulent Receipt and Use of Credit and Other Loans

(1) For providing deliberately false information for the purpose of receiving subsidies, grants, credit or other loans or during the use of subsidies, grants, credit or other loans, if substantial harm has been caused thereby, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a period of up to one year or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2) For the same acts, if they have been committed for the purpose of receiving or using subsidies, grants, credit or other loans on a large scale, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a period of up to four years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by community service, or by a fine.

(In the version of the Law of 13.12.2012. as amended by the Laws of 08.06.2017., 06.06.2019., 17.12.2020. and 07.04.2022., which comes into force on 04.05.2022.)

### 211.pants. Unfair Competition, Misleading Advertising and Unfair Commercial Practices

For unfair competition, misleading advertising or unfair commercial practices, if substantial harm has been caused thereby, — shall be punished by short-term deprivation of liberty or by community service, or by a fine.

(In the version of the Law of 13.12.2012. as amended by the Laws of 17.12.2020. and 07.04.2022., which comes into force on

### 212.pants. Failure to Comply with Requirements of the State Institution for Competition Protection and Promotion

For failure to comply with the lawful requirements of the state institution for competition protection and promotion, if substantial harm has been caused thereby, — shall be punished by short-term deprivation of liberty or by community service, or by a fine.

(In the version of the Law of 13.12.2012. as amended by the Laws of 17.12.2020. and 07.04.2022., which comes into force on 212.1 pants. Manipulation of Sports Competitions

(1) For manipulation of sports competitions organised by a sports organisation — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a period of up to one year or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2) For the acts provided for in paragraph one of this Article, if they are related to the acceptance, transfer or offering of material values, benefits of a proprietary or other nature, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a period of up to three years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(3) For the criminal offence provided for in paragraph two of this Article, if it has been committed on a large scale or if it has been committed by an organised group, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a period of up to five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(In the version of the Law of 28.01.2016. as amended by the Law of 17.12.2020., which comes into force on 01.01.2022.)

### 213.pants. Leading to Insolvency

(1) For leading a legal person subject to insolvency proceedings to insolvency through negligence, if substantial harm has been caused thereby to the interests of another person protected by law, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a period of up to one year or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine, with deprivation of the right to engage in a specific or all types of commercial activity or a specific occupation or the right to hold a specific position for a period of up to three years.

(2) For intentionally leading a legal person subject to insolvency proceedings to insolvency, if substantial harm has been caused thereby to the interests of another person protected by law, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a period of up to three years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine, with deprivation of the right to engage in a specific or all types of commercial activity or a specific occupation or the right to hold a specific position for a period of two to five years. (In the version of the Law of 13.12.2007. as amended by the Laws of 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which comes into force on

### 214.pants. Submission of a False Insolvency Application

(1) (Deleted by the Law of 21.10.2010.)

(2) For submission of an insolvency application in which deliberately false information has been provided or information has been concealed, if insolvency proceedings may be or have been declared on the basis of the application (a deliberately false insolvency application), — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a period of up to two years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(In the version of the Law of 13.12.2007. as amended by the Laws of 21.10.2010., 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which comes into force on 01.01.2022.)

### 215.pants. Obstruction of Insolvency Proceedings

(1) (Deleted by the Law of 21.10.2010.)

(2) For providing deliberately false information to the court, a creditors' meeting or other institutions or persons provided for by law, as well as for carrying out transactions in favour of one or more creditors at the expense of other creditors, if committed by an administrator in insolvency proceedings, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a period of up to two years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(3) For obstructing the course of insolvency proceedings, which is manifested in the actions of the representative of the debtor (legal person subject to insolvency proceedings) or the natural person (in natural person insolvency proceedings) as providing deliberately false information to the court or administrator, unlawfully carrying out transactions, concealing property or transactions, concealing, destroying or falsifying documents, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a period of up to five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine, with deprivation of the right to engage in a specific or all types of commercial activity or a specific occupation or the right to hold a specific position for a period of two to five years. (In the version of the Law of 13.12.2007. as amended by the Laws of 21.10.2010., 13.12.2012., 08.06.2017. and 17.12.2020., which comes into force on 01.01.2022.)

215.1 pants. Violation of Legal Protection Proceedings Rules

(1) For using legal protection proceedings for the purpose of avoiding the fulfilment of obligations — shall be punished by probation supervision or by community service or by a fine.

(2) For obstructing legal protection proceedings, which is manifested as providing deliberately false information to the court or the person supervising the legal protection proceedings, unlawfully carrying out transactions, concealing property or transactions, concealing, destroying or falsifying documents, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a period of up to two years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine, with deprivation of the right to engage in a specific or all types of commercial activity or a specific occupation or the right to hold a specific position for a period of two to five years. (In the version of the Law of 13.12.2007. as amended by the Laws of 13.12.2012., 08.06.2017. and 17.12.2020., which comes into force on 01.01.2022.)

### 216.pants. Unlawful Alienation, Damage and Destruction of Pledged Property

For alienation of property pledged in the form of a commercial pledge without the permission of the pledgee, or for its damage or destruction, if substantial harm has been caused thereby, — shall be punished by short-term deprivation of liberty, or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine. (As amended by the Law of 13.12.2012., with amendments made by the Laws of 17.12.2020. and 07.04.2022., which enter into force

### 217.pants. Violation of Accounting and Statistical Information Regulations

(1) For concealment or falsification of accounting documents, annual reports, statistical reports or statistical information prescribed by law for an enterprise (commercial company), institution or organisation — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to one year, or short-term deprivation of liberty, or community service, or a fine.

(2) For the same acts, if they have caused substantial harm, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to four years, or short-term deprivation of liberty, or community service, or a fine.

(3) For the criminal offence provided for in paragraph two of this Section, if it has been committed for mercenary purpose, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years, or short-term deprivation of liberty, or community service, or a fine.

(As amended by the Law of 13.12.2012., with amendments made by the Laws of 06.06.2019., 17.12.2020. and 07.04.2022., which enter into force 04.05.2022.)

217.1 pants. Violation of Remuneration Regulations

For payment of remuneration not shown in accounting records, if it has been committed in a significant amount, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to two years, or short-term deprivation of liberty, or community service, or a fine, and by withdrawal of the right to engage in a specific or any type of commercial activity or a specific occupation, or the right to hold a specific position, for a term of up to three years.

(As amended by the Law of 10.03.2016., with amendments made by the Law of 17.12.2020., which enter into force 01.01.2022.)

### 218.pants. Evasion of Payment of Taxes and Payments Equated Thereto

(1) (Deleted by the Law of 13.12.2012.)

(2) For evasion of payment of taxes or payments equated thereto, or for concealment or reduction of income, profit or other objects subject to taxation, if losses have been caused to the State or local government in a large amount, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to four years, or short-term deprivation of liberty, or community service, or a fine, with or without confiscation of property, and by withdrawal of the right to engage in a specific or any type of commercial activity or a specific occupation, or the right to hold a specific position, for a term from two to five years.

(3) For the criminal offence provided for in paragraph two of this Section, if it has been committed by an organized group, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to ten years, with or without confiscation of property, and by withdrawal of the right to engage in a specific or any type of commercial activity or a specific occupation, or the right to hold a specific position, for a term from two to five years, and probation supervision for a term of up to three years.

(With amendments made by the Laws of 12.02.2004., 13.12.2007., 08.07.2011., 13.12.2012., 08.06.2017. and 17.12.2020., which enter into force 01.01.2022.)

218.1 pants. Indication of a Transaction That Has Not Actually Taken Place in a Value Added Tax Declaration

(1) For indication of a transaction subject to value added tax that has not actually taken place in a value added tax declaration, if the total value of the transaction or several such transactions that have not actually taken place indicated in the declaration reaches a large amount, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to two years, or short-term deprivation of liberty, or community service, or a fine.

(2) For indication of a transaction subject to value added tax that has not actually taken place in a value added tax declaration or in several such declarations, if losses have been caused to the State in a significant amount thereby, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years, or short-term deprivation of liberty, or community service, or a fine, with withdrawal of the right to engage in a specific or any type of commercial activity or a specific occupation, or the right to hold a specific position, for a term from two to five years.

(3) For the acts provided for in paragraph two of this Section, if losses have been caused to the State in a large amount thereby, or for the criminal offence provided for in paragraph one or two of this Section, if it has been committed by an organized group, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to ten years, with or without confiscation of property, and by withdrawal of the right to engage in a specific or any type of commercial activity or a specific occupation, or the right to hold a specific position, for a term from two to five years, and probation supervision for a term of up to three years.

(As amended by the Law of 06.07.2021., which enters into force 05.08.2021. See transitional provision 27.)

### 219.pants. Evasion of Submission of Declaration

(1) (Deleted by the Law of 13.12.2012.)

(2) For indication of false information in a declaration of income, property, transactions or other property nature prescribed by law, if false information has been indicated regarding property or other income in a large amount, — shall be punished by short-term deprivation of liberty, or community service, or a fine.

(3) For failure to indicate the source of origin of declarable property or other income prescribed by law, or for providing false information regarding the source of origin of declarable property or other income, if such information has been requested by a duly authorised State institution in the manner prescribed by law and if false information has been indicated regarding property or other income in a large amount, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to two years, or short-term deprivation of liberty, or community service, or a fine, with or without confiscation of property.

(As amended by the Law of 17.10.2002., with amendments made by the Laws of 12.02.2004., 13.12.2007., 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which enter into force 01.01.2022.)

### 220.pants. Absconding with Property

For alienation, damage, destruction, dissipation, concealment or other absconding with property or means of payment with the intent to evade payment of debt or fulfilment of other obligations — shall be punished by short-term deprivation of liberty, or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.

(With amendments made by the Laws of 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which enter into force 01.01.2022.)

220.1 pants. Unlawful Storage, Movement (Transportation) and Sale of Petroleum Products

(1) For unlawful storage, movement (transportation) or sale of petroleum products, if it has been committed in a significant amount, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to one year, or short-term deprivation of liberty, or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.

(2) For unlawful storage, movement (transportation) or sale of petroleum products, if it has been committed in a large amount, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to two years, or short-term deprivation of liberty, or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine, with or without confiscation of property.

(3) For the criminal offence provided for in paragraph one or two of this Section, if it has been committed by a group of persons by prior agreement, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years, or short-term deprivation of liberty, or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine, with or without confiscation of property.

(4) For the criminal offence provided for in paragraph one or two of this Section, if it has been committed by an organized group, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to seven years, with or without confiscation of property, and probation supervision for a term of up to three years.

(As amended by the Law of 13.12.2012., with amendments made by the Laws of 08.07.2011., 29.10.2015., 08.06.2017. and 17.12.2020., which enter into force 01.01.2022.)


This excerpt sits under: Issuer: Saeima Published: > XIX nodaļa

### 221. Article.

Illegal Manufacturing (Production), Storage, Transportation and Sale of Alcoholic Beverages and Tobacco Products

(1) For illegal manufacturing (production), storage, transportation or sale of alcoholic beverages or tobacco products, if committed on a significant scale, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a period of up to two years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2) For the criminal offence provided for in paragraph one of this Article, if committed by a group of persons pursuant to a prior agreement, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a period of up to three years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property.

(3) For illegal manufacturing (production), storage, transportation or sale of alcoholic beverages or tobacco products, if committed on a large scale, or for the criminal offence provided for in paragraph one of this Article, if committed by an organised group, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a period of up to five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property. (As worded by the Law of 21.05.2009., with amendments made by the Laws of 08.07.2011., 13.12.2012., 29.10.2015., 08.06.2017., 03.09.2020. and 17.12.2020., which come into force on 01.01.2022.)

221.1 Article. Sale of Illegal Alcoholic Beverages

(1) For the sale of illegally manufactured (produced) or counterfeit alcoholic beverages or spirit-containing liquids that are not alcoholic beverages but are offered as alcoholic beverages (illegal alcoholic beverages) — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a period of up to one year or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2) For the actions provided for in paragraph one of this Article, if committed on a significant scale or if committed by a group of persons pursuant to a prior agreement, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a period of up to three years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property.

(3) For the actions provided for in paragraph one of this Article, if they have caused serious consequences or if committed on a large scale, or if committed by an organised group, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a period of up to eight years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property, and by probation supervision for a period of up to three years.

(As worded by the Law of 21.06.2007., with amendments made by the Laws of 08.07.2011., 13.12.2012., 03.09.2020. and 17.12.2020., which come into force on 01.01.2022.)

221.2 Article. Illegal Manufacturing (Production), Storage and Transportation of Alcoholic Beverages

(1) For illegal manufacturing (production), storage or transportation of alcoholic beverages, if committed on a significant scale, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a period of up to three years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2) For the criminal offence provided for in paragraph one of this Article, if committed by a group of persons pursuant to a prior agreement, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a period of up to four years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(3) For illegal manufacturing (production), storage or transportation of alcoholic beverages, if committed on a large scale or if committed by an organised group, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a period of up to five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property. (As worded by the Law of 21.06.2007., with amendments made by the Laws of 19.11.2009., 13.12.2012., 29.10.2015., 03.09.2020. and 17.12.2020., which come into force on 01.01.2022.)

221.3 Article. Acquisition of Illegal Alcoholic Beverages

(Deleted by the Law of 13.12.2012., which comes into force on 01.04.2013.)

221.4 Article. Release of a Person from Criminal Liability for Storage and Transportation of Illegal Alcoholic Beverages

A person who has voluntarily surrendered illegal alcoholic beverages and voluntarily reported the manufacturing (production), storage, transportation or sale of these illegal alcoholic beverages shall be released from criminal liability for their storage or transportation.

(As worded by the Law of 21.06.2007., with amendments made by the Law of 13.12.2012., which comes into force on 01.04.2013.)

221.5 Article. Provision of Premises for Illegal Manufacturing (Production), Storage and Sale of Alcoholic Beverages

(Deleted by the Law of 13.12.2012., which comes into force on 01.04.2013.)

221.6 Article. Illegal Sale of Tobacco Products in Small Quantities

For the sale in small quantities of illegally manufactured (produced) tobacco products or tobacco products that are not marked with excise tax stamps of the Republic of Latvia, except in cases provided for in regulatory enactments, or smokeless tobacco products the placing on the market of which is prohibited, if committed with mercenary intent, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a period of up to one year or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(As worded by the Law of 03.09.2020., with amendments made by the Law of 06.07.2021., which comes into force on 05.08.2021. The amendment replacing the words "or by forced labour" with the words "or by probation supervision, or by community service" comes into force on 01.01.2022. See transitional provision 26)

### 222. Article. Violation of Veterinary Regulations

(1) For intentional violation of veterinary regulations, if it has caused the spread of an epizootic or other serious consequences, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a period of up to two years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2) For the actions provided for in paragraph one of this Article, if they have caused the death of two or more persons, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a period of up to five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by community service, or by a fine.

(With amendments made by the Laws of 13.12.2012., 25.09.2014. and 17.12.2020., which come into force on 01.01.2022.)

### 223. Article. Violation of Regulations on Combating Plant Diseases and Pests

(1) For violation of regulations on combating plant diseases or pests, if it has caused serious consequences, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a period of up to two years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2) For the actions provided for in paragraph one of this Article, if they have caused the death of two or more persons, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a period of up to five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by community service, or by a fine.

(With amendments made by the Laws of 13.12.2012., 25.09.2014. and 17.12.2020., which come into force on 01.01.2022.)

## Chapter XX

Criminal Offences against General Security and Public Order

### 224. Article. Banditry

(1) For the association of more than two persons into an organised armed group (gang) for the commission of crimes — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a period of up to ten years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property and by probation supervision for a period of up to three years.

(2) For participation in crimes committed by the gang or for leading it — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a period from three to fifteen years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property, and by probation supervision for a period of up to three years.

(With amendments made by the Laws of 08.07.2011. and 13.12.2012., which come into force on 01.04.2013. See transitional provision 11)

### 225.pants. Mass Disorders

For organizing mass disorders connected with riots, destruction, arson, property damage or with violence against persons, or with resistance to representatives of authority, or for active participation therein, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a period from three to twelve years and with probation supervision for a period up to three years.

(21.10.2010. likuma redakcijā ar grozījumiem, kas izdarīti ar 08.07.2011. likumu, kas stājas spēkā 01.10.2011. Sk. pārejas noteikumu 11.punktu)

225.1 pants. Violation of Restrictions and Prohibitions Imposed During State of Emergency and State of Exception

(1) For violation of restrictions or prohibitions imposed during a state of emergency, if substantial harm has been caused thereby, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a period up to three years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2) For violation of restrictions or prohibitions imposed during a state of exception, if substantial harm has been caused thereby, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a period up to five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(3) For acts provided for in paragraph one or two of this Article, if they have caused serious consequences, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a period up to eight years.
