(26.04.2018. likuma redakcijā ar grozījumiem, kas izdarīti ar 17.12.2020. un 07.04.2022. likumu, kas stājas spēkā

### 226.pants. Violation of Public Event Organization and Conduct Procedures

(1) For violation of public event organization or conduct procedures committed by the organizer of an event or other person, if substantial harm has been caused thereby, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a period up to one year or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2) For the same acts, if they have caused serious consequences, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a period up to five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(3) For acts provided for in paragraph one of this Article, if death of two or more persons has occurred as a result thereof, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a period up to eight years.

(16.12.2004. likuma redakcijā ar grozījumiem, kas izdarīti ar 13.12.2012., 25.09.2014., 17.12.2020. un 07.04.2022. likumu, kas stājas spēkā 04.05.2022.)

### 227.pants. Threat to Public Safety, Order and Personal Health Through Religious Activity

For organizing or leading a group whose activities, manifesting as the preaching of religious doctrine and performance of religious rituals, are connected with causing harm to public safety and order, personal health, or legally protected personal interests, or for participation by a person in such activities — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a period up to two years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(Ar grozījumiem, kas izdarīti ar 12.02.2004., 13.12.2012. un 17.12.2020. likumu, kas stājas spēkā 01.01.2022.)

### 228.pants. Desecration of Grave and Corpse

(1) For desecration of a grave, burial urn, buried or unburied corpse — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a period up to one year or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2) For the same acts, if they have been committed by a group of persons pursuant to prior agreement, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a period up to three years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(3) For acts provided for in paragraph one or two of this Article, if they are connected with theft of a monument, burial urn or other objects located on a grave or in a grave or near a burial urn, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a period up to five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property.

(Ar grozījumiem, kas izdarīti ar 12.02.2004., 13.12.2012. un 17.12.2020. likumu, kas stājas spēkā 01.01.2022.)

### 229. pants. Unlawful Acts with Cultural Objects

(1) For unlawful possession, relocation, dispatch, alienation of a cultural object, that is, a cultural monument under protection of the Republic of Latvia or another state, a museum collection item or a specially protected library collection document, or for its unlawful export from the Republic of Latvia or import into the Republic of Latvia, if substantial harm has been caused thereby, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a period up to two years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2) For destruction, damage or desecration of a cultural object — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a period up to three years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(07.01.2021. likuma redakcijā ar grozījumiem, kas izdarīti ar 17.12.2020. likumu, kas stājas spēkā 01.01.2022.)

229.1 pants. Unlawful Acts with Antiquities

(1) For unlawful acquisition, possession, relocation, dispatch, alienation of an antiquity under protection of the Republic of Latvia which is not located in a museum collection, or an antiquity under protection of another state, or for its unlawful export from the Republic of Latvia or import into the Republic of Latvia — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a period up to one year or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2) For destruction or damage of an antiquity under protection of the Republic of Latvia which is not located in a museum collection, or an antiquity under protection of another state — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a period up to two years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(07.01.2021. likuma redakcijā ar grozījumiem, kas izdarīti ar 17.12.2020. likumu, kas stājas spēkā 01.01.2022.)

229.2 pants. Exemption of Person from Criminal Liability for Unlawful Acquisition, Possession, Relocation and Dispatch of Antiquities

A person who has voluntarily surrendered an unlawfully acquired, possessed, relocated or dispatched antiquity under protection of the Republic of Latvia which is not located in a museum collection, or an antiquity under protection of another state, shall be exempted from criminal liability for the unlawful acquisition, possession, relocation or dispatch of an antiquity under protection of the Republic of Latvia which is not located in a museum collection, or an antiquity under protection of another state.

(07.01.2021. likuma redakcijā, kas stājas spēkā 02.02.2021.)


This excerpt sits under: Izdevējs: Saeima Publicēts: > XX nodaļa

### 230.pants. Cruel treatment of animals and animal torture

(1) For cruel treatment of an animal as a result of which it has died or been maimed, or for animal torture — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a period of up to three years, or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine, with deprivation of the right to keep animals of a certain or all species for a period of up to three years.

(2) For the same acts, if committed in a public place or in the presence of a minor, or if committed by a group of persons by prior agreement, or if substantial harm has been caused thereby, or for animal torture as a result of which it has died or been maimed, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a period of up to five years, or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine, with deprivation of the right to keep animals of a certain or all species for a period of up to five years.

(In the wording of the law of 21.05.2009. with amendments made by the laws of 13.12.2012., 22.06.2017., 17.12.2020. and 07.04.2022., which enters into force on 04.05.2022.)

230.1 pants. Violation of animal keeping regulations

(1) For violation of animal keeping regulations, if as a result thereof medium severity bodily injury has been caused to the victim, — shall be punished by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2) For violation of animal keeping regulations, if as a result thereof severe bodily injury has been caused to the victim or it has caused a person's death, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a period of up to three years, or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(In the wording of the law of 25.04.2002. with amendments made by the laws of 12.02.2004., 13.12.2012., 11.06.2020. and 17.12.2020., which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

### 231.pants. Hooliganism

(1) For gross disturbance of public order manifested in obvious disrespect for society or in shamelessness, ignoring generally accepted norms of behaviour and disturbing the peace of people, the work of institutions or enterprises (companies), or organisations (hooliganism), — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a period of up to two years, or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2) For hooliganism, if committed by a group of persons or if it is related to causing bodily injury to the victim or to damage or destruction of property, or to resisting a representative of authority or a person who opposes a violation of public order, or if it has been committed using weapons or other objects usable for causing bodily injury, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a period of up to five years, or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(With amendments made by the laws of 08.07.2011., 13.12.2012., 15.05.2014., 25.09.2014. and 17.12.2020., which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

231.1 pants. Knowingly false notification of the placement or positioning of explosive, toxic, radioactive or bacteriological substance or material or explosive device

(1) For knowingly false notification of the placement of an explosive, toxic, radioactive or bacteriological substance or material or explosive device in an institution, enterprise or other facility or positioning outside an institution, enterprise or other facility — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a period of up to one year, or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2) For the same acts, if substantial harm has been caused thereby or if the knowingly false notification is directed at two or more facilities, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a period of up to four years, or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by a fine.

(3) For the acts provided for in the first paragraph of this Section, if they have caused serious consequences, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a period of up to five years, or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by a fine.

(In the wording of the law of 25.04.2002. with amendments made by the laws of 12.02.2004., 13.12.2012., 10.03.2016., 11.06.2020., 17.12.2020. and 14.12.2023., which enters into force on 30.12.2023.)

### 232.pants. Involving mentally ill persons in a criminal offence

For involving a person in a criminal offence, knowing that he or she suffers from mental disorders, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a period of up to two years, or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(With amendments made by the laws of 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

### 233.pants.

Unauthorised manufacture, repair, acquisition, storage, carrying, transportation, forwarding, sale and violation of the sale regulations of firearms, essential components of firearms, firearms ammunition, high-energy pneumatic weapons, explosives and explosive devices

(1) For the sale of a firearm, essential components of a firearm, firearm ammunition, a high-energy pneumatic weapon, explosives or an explosive device to a person who does not have the relevant permit or special permit (licence), if committed by a person who has the relevant sale permit or special permit (licence), — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a period of up to one year, or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2) For the manufacture, repair, acquisition, storage, carrying, transportation, forwarding or sale of a firearm, essential components of a firearm, firearm ammunition, a high-energy pneumatic weapon, explosives or an explosive device without the relevant permit — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a period of up to three years, or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(3) For the acts provided for in the second paragraph of this Section, if committed by an organised group, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a period of up to ten years, with or without confiscation of property, and with probation supervision for a period of up to three years.

(In the wording of the law of 20.05.2004. with amendments made by the laws of 13.12.2007., 08.07.2011., 13.12.2012., 29.10.2015. and 17.12.2020., which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

### 234.pants. Unauthorised manufacture and use of gas pistols (revolvers) and their ammunition

(1) For the manufacture of a gas pistol (revolver) or ammunition intended for it filled with irritating or paralysing substances without the relevant permit — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a period of up to one year, or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2) For violation of the conditions or procedure for the use or utilisation of a gas pistol (revolver), if substantial harm has been caused thereby, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a period of up to three years, or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(3) For violation of the conditions or procedure for the use or utilisation of a gas pistol (revolver), if serious consequences have been caused thereby, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a period of up to five years, or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(In the wording of the law of 13.12.2012. with amendments made by the laws of 29.10.2015. and 17.12.2020., which enters into force on

### 235.pants.

Release of persons from criminal liability for the manufacture, acquisition, carrying and storage of a firearm, essential components of a firearm, firearm ammunition, high-energy pneumatic weapon, explosives or explosive device and for the manufacture of a gas pistol (revolver) and its ammunition

A person who has voluntarily surrendered a firearm, essential components of a firearm, firearm ammunition, high-energy pneumatic weapon, explosives or explosive device manufactured, acquired, carried or stored without appropriate permission, or a gas pistol (revolver) or its ammunition manufactured without appropriate permission, shall be released from criminal liability if the person's actions do not contain the elements of another criminal offence.

(20.05.2004. likuma redakcijā ar grozījumiem, kas izdarīti ar 29.10.2015. likumu, kas stājas spēkā 03.12.2015.)

### 236.pants.

Storage, carrying, transportation and forwarding of firearms, essential components of firearms, firearm ammunition, high-energy pneumatic weapons, explosives and explosive devices in violation of regulatory enactments

(1) For the storage, carrying, transportation or forwarding of a firearm, essential components of a firearm, firearm ammunition, high-energy pneumatic weapon, explosives or explosive device in violation of regulatory enactments, if as a result thereof any of the aforementioned items has been lost or has been acquired by another person, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to two years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2) For the same act, if it has caused serious consequences, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(20.05.2004. likuma redakcijā ar grozījumiem, kas izdarīti ar 13.12.2012., 29.10.2015. un 17.12.2020. likumu, kas stājas spēkā 01.01.2022.)

### 237.pants. Violation of the conditions or procedures for the use or application of firearms and high-energy pneumatic weapons

and violation of the procedures for the use of explosives and explosive devices

(1) For violation of the conditions or procedures for the use or application of a firearm or high-energy pneumatic weapon, or violation of the procedures for the use of explosives and explosive devices, if committed by a person who has been permitted to acquire, store or carry a firearm or high-energy pneumatic weapon or who has the right to use explosives and explosive devices, and if substantial harm has been caused thereby, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to three years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2) For the acts provided for in the first paragraph of this Section, if they have caused serious consequences, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(13.12.2012. likuma redakcijā ar grozījumiem, kas izdarīti ar 29.10.2015. un 17.12.2020. likumu, kas stājas spēkā 237.1 pants. Violation of the circulation rules for goods of strategic importance

(1) For violation of the circulation rules for goods of strategic importance, if substantial harm has been caused thereby, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to one year or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2) For violation of the prohibition on the circulation of equipment, devices or instruments and their components or software specifically created or adapted for operational activities to be carried out in a special manner or for obstructing them — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to two years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine, with deprivation of the right to engage in specific employment for a term of up to five years.

(13.12.2012. likuma redakcijā ar grozījumiem, kas izdarīti ar 12.11.2015., 26.04.2018. un 17.12.2020. likumu, kas stājas spēkā 01.01.2022.)

### 238.pants. Violation of production safety and technical discipline regulations

(1) For violation of production safety or technical discipline regulations in construction works or works related to blasting, or at enterprises (commercial companies) endangered by explosions, if it has caused serious consequences, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine, with deprivation of the right to engage in specific employment or the right to hold a specific position for a term of up to five years.

(2) For the criminal offence provided for in the first paragraph of this Section, if committed by a person who is responsible for compliance with production safety or technical discipline regulations, or for the acts provided for in the first paragraph of this Section, if they have caused the death of two or more persons, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to eight years, with deprivation of the right to engage in specific employment or the right to hold a specific position for a term of up to ten years.

(Ar grozījumiem, kas izdarīti ar 13.12.2012., 25.09.2014., 17.12.2020., 07.01.2021. un 17.12.2020. likumu, kas stājas spēkā 01.01.2022.)

### 239.pants. Violation of construction regulations

(1) For carrying out construction works during the time they have been suspended, in a third group building or in a multi-dwelling building, if the construction works have been suspended due to their performance without a construction permit or due to the commencement of construction works before fulfilment of the conditions of the construction permit, — shall be punished by short-term deprivation of liberty or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2) For violation of construction norms or regulations for structures, bridges, overpasses or other buildings, if as a result thereof a building or part thereof has collapsed, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to two years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine, with deprivation of the right to engage in specific employment or the right to hold a specific position for a term of up to five years.

(3) For the acts provided for in the first or second paragraph of this Section, if they have caused serious consequences, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine, with deprivation of the right to engage in specific employment or the right to hold a specific position for a term of up to seven years.

(4) For the acts provided for in the first or second paragraph of this Section, if they have caused the death of two or more persons, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to eight years, with deprivation of the right to engage in specific employment or the right to hold a specific position for a term of up to ten years.

(25.09.2014. likuma redakcijā ar grozījumiem, kas izdarīti ar 07.01.2021. un 17.12.2020. likumu, kas stājas spēkā 239.1 pants. Operation of a dangerous building

(1) For violation of the prohibition on the operation of a dangerous building, if committed by a person who is responsible for the operation of the building, and if it has caused serious consequences, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2) For the acts provided for in the first paragraph of this Section, if they have caused the death of two or more persons, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to eight years.

(11.06.2020. likuma redakcijā ar grozījumiem, kas izdarīti ar 06.07.2021. likumu, kas stājas spēkā 05.08.2021. Grozījums pirmajā daļā par vārdu aizstāšanu "vai ar piespiedu darbu" ar vārdiem "vai ar probācijas uzraudzību, vai ar sabiedrisko darbu" stājas spēkā 01.01.2022. Sk. pārejas noteikumu 26. punktu)

### 240.pants. Violation of Fire Safety Regulations

(1) (Deleted by the law of 13.12.2012.)

(2) For intentional violation of fire safety regulations, if committed by a person who is responsible for compliance with these regulations, and if it has caused substantial harm — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term not exceeding three years or by short-term deprivation of liberty or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(3) For intentional violation of fire safety regulations, if committed by a person who is responsible for compliance with these regulations, and if it has caused serious consequences, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term not exceeding five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine, with deprivation of the right to engage in specific employment or the right to hold a specific position for a term not exceeding five years.

(4) For intentional violation of fire safety regulations, if committed by a person who is responsible for compliance with these regulations, and if it has caused the death of two or more persons, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term not exceeding eight years, with deprivation of the right to engage in specific employment or the right to hold a specific position for a term not exceeding ten years.

(With amendments made by the laws of 13.12.2012., 25.09.2014. and 17.12.2020., which enter into force on 01.01.2022.)

### 241.pants. Unauthorised Access to an Automated Data Processing System

(1) For unauthorised access to the resources of an automated data processing system, if it is connected with overcoming the protection means of the system or if it is committed without appropriate authorisation or by using rights granted to another person and if it has caused substantial harm, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term not exceeding two years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2) For the criminal offence specified in the first paragraph of this Section, if it has been committed for mercenary purposes, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term not exceeding four years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property.

(3) For the acts specified in the first paragraph of this Section, if they have been committed by an organised group or if they have caused serious consequences, or if they are directed against an automated data processing system that processes information related to the political, economic, military, social or other security of the State, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term not exceeding five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property. (In the wording of the law of 25.09.2014. with amendments made by the laws of 06.07.2021. and 17.12.2020., which enter into force on

### 242.pants. Unauthorised Acquisition of Computer Software

(Deleted by the law of 28.04.2005., which enters into force on 01.06.2005.)

### 243.pants. Interference with the Operations of an Automated Data Processing System and Unlawful Actions with Information Contained in this

System

(1) For unauthorised modification, damage, destruction, deterioration or concealment of information contained in an automated data processing system or for the introduction of deliberately false information into an automated data processing system, if it has caused substantial harm, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term not exceeding three years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2) For deliberate interference with the operations of an automated data processing system by introducing, transferring, damaging, deleting, deteriorating, modifying or concealing information, if it damages or destroys the protection system and causes substantial harm, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term not exceeding three years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(3) For the criminal offence specified in the first or second paragraph of this Section, if it has been committed for mercenary purposes, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term not exceeding five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property.

(4) (Deleted by the law of 13.12.2012.)

(5) For the acts specified in the first or second paragraph of this Section, if they have caused serious consequences or if they are directed against an automated data processing system that processes information related to the political, economic, military, social or other security of the State, or for the criminal offence specified in the first or second paragraph of this Section, if it has been committed by an organised group, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term not exceeding seven years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property, and with probation supervision for a term not exceeding three years or without it.

(In the wording of the law of 28.04.2005. with amendments made by the laws of 13.12.2007., 08.07.2011., 13.12.2012., 25.09.2014., 08.06.2017., 17.12.2020. and 27.10.2022., which enters into force on 25.11.2022.)

### 244.pants. Unlawful Actions with Devices Affecting the Resources of an Automated Data Processing System

(1) For unauthorised production, adaptation for use, sale, distribution, acquisition, transfer or storage of a tool (device, computer program, computer password, access code or similar data) that is intended for affecting the resources of an automated data processing system or with the help of which it is possible to access an automated data processing system or a part thereof for the purpose of committing a criminal offence, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term not exceeding two years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2) For the same acts, if they have been committed by an organised group or if they have caused serious consequences, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term not exceeding five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(In the wording of the law of 28.04.2005. with amendments made by the laws of 13.12.2012., 25.09.2014., 06.07.2021. and 17.12.2020., which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

244.1 pants. Acquisition, Production, Modification, Storage and Distribution of Data, Software and Equipment for Unlawful Actions with Electronic Communications Network Terminal Equipment

For modification of data necessary for identification of electronic communications network terminal equipment in an electronic communications network or for acquisition, storage or distribution of data intended for such purpose, as well as for acquisition, production, storage or distribution of software or equipment intended for such purpose without the consent of the manufacturer or a person authorised by it, if such actions have been committed for mercenary purposes or if they have been committed by a group of persons pursuant to prior agreement, or if they have caused substantial harm, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term not exceeding two years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(In the wording of the law of 21.05.2009. with amendments made by the laws of 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which enters into force on

### 245.pants. Violation of Information System Security Regulations

For violation of information storage and processing regulations or other information computer system security regulations developed in accordance with the information regime or its protection, committed by a person who is responsible for compliance with these regulations, if it has been the reason for theft, destruction or damage to information or if it has caused other substantial harm, — shall be punished by short-term deprivation of liberty or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(With amendments made by the laws of 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

### 246.pants. Unauthorized Sending of Highly Flammable Substances and Objects, as well as Corrosive Substances

(1) For unauthorized sending of highly flammable substances or objects, as well as corrosive substances, if it has caused serious consequences, — shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of up to five years or short-term imprisonment, or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.

(2) For the same acts, if they have caused the death of two or more persons, — shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of up to eight years.

(With amendments made by the laws of 13.12.2012., 25.09.2014. and 17.12.2020., which come into force on 01.01.2022.)

### 247.pants. Unauthorized Transportation of Highly Flammable Substances and Objects on an Aircraft

(1) For unauthorized transportation of highly flammable substances or objects on an aircraft — shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of up to two years or short-term imprisonment, or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.

(2) For the acts provided for in the first paragraph of this Article, if they have caused serious consequences, — shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of up to five years or short-term imprisonment, or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.

(3) For the acts provided for in the first paragraph of this Article, if they have caused the death of two or more persons, — shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of up to eight years.

(With amendments made by the laws of 12.02.2004., 13.12.2012., 25.09.2014. and 17.12.2020., which come into force on

### 248.pants.

Unauthorized Manufacture, Acquisition, Storage, Sale and Sending of Poisonous and Potent Substances

(1) For the manufacture, acquisition, storage or sale of poisonous or potent substances that are not narcotic or psychotropic substances without appropriate authorization, as well as for violation of regulations regarding the manufacture, storage, dispensing, accounting, transportation or sending of such substances, if substantial harm has been caused thereby, — shall be punished by short-term imprisonment or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.

(2) For the criminal offence provided for in the first paragraph of this Article, if it has been committed by a group of persons pursuant to prior agreement, — shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of up to one year or short-term imprisonment, or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.

(3) For unauthorized manufacture, acquisition or sale of the substances provided for in the first paragraph of this Article, if it has caused serious consequences, — shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of up to five years or short-term imprisonment, or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.

(4) For unauthorized manufacture, acquisition or sale of the substances provided for in the first paragraph of this Article, if it has caused the death of two or more persons, — shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of up to eight years and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years or without it.

(With amendments made by the laws of 12.02.2004., 13.12.2012., 25.09.2014. and 17.12.2020., which come into force on 248.1 pants. Unauthorized Manufacture, Acquisition, Storage, Transportation and Sending of New Psychoactive Substances for the Purpose of Sale and Sale

(1) For the manufacture, acquisition, storage, transportation or sending for the purpose of sale or sale of a new psychoactive substance or a product containing it, the circulation of which is prohibited or restricted, — shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of up to two years or short-term imprisonment, or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years.

(2) For the same acts, if they have caused serious consequences, — shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of up to five years or probation supervision, or community service. (In the wording of the law of 03.04.2014. with amendments made by the laws of 15.05.2014., 25.09.2014. and 17.12.2020., which come into force on 01.01.2022.)

248.2 pants. Unauthorized Manufacture, Acquisition, Storage, Transportation, Sending of New Psychoactive Substances and Unauthorized Use of New Psychoactive Substances

(1) For unauthorized acquisition, storage, transportation or sending of a new psychoactive substance or a product containing it, the circulation of which is prohibited or restricted, without the intention to sell them or unauthorized use of a new psychoactive substance or a product containing it, the circulation of which is prohibited or restricted, if it has been committed by a person who has been warned about criminal liability for unauthorized acquisition, storage, transportation, sending and use of a new psychoactive substance or a product containing it, the circulation of which is prohibited or restricted, — shall be punished by short-term imprisonment or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.

(2) For unauthorized manufacture of a new psychoactive substance or a product containing it, the circulation of which is prohibited or restricted, without the intention to sell them — shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of up to three years or short-term imprisonment, or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.

(In the wording of the law of 25.09.2014. with amendments made by the law of 17.12.2020., which come into force on 01.01.2022.)

### 249.pants.

Violation of Regulations on Production, Acquisition, Storage, Accounting, Dispensing, Transportation and Sending of Narcotic and Psychotropic Substances

(1) For violation of regulations on production, acquisition, storage, accounting, dispensing, transportation or sending of narcotic or psychotropic substances, if substantial harm has been caused thereby, — shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of up to one year or short-term imprisonment, or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine, with deprivation of the right to engage in specific employment for a term of up to three years.

(2) For the criminal offence provided for in the first paragraph of this Article, if it has been committed by a group of persons pursuant to prior agreement, — shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of up to four years or short-term imprisonment, or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine, with deprivation of the right to engage in specific employment for a term of up to three years.

(With amendments made by the laws of 12.02.2004., 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which come into force on 01.01.2022.)

### 250.pants. Unauthorized Dispensing of Narcotic and Psychotropic Substances

(1) For prescribing a prescription without medical necessity or illegally writing another document for obtaining a narcotic or psychotropic substance, as well as for dispensing a narcotic or psychotropic substance without a prescription or other document or knowing that the prescription or other document is fictitious or illegally written, if such acts are committed for mercenary purposes or other personal interest, — shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of up to three years or by short-term imprisonment, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine, with deprivation of the right to engage in certain activities for a term of up to five years.

(2) For prescribing a prescription without medical necessity or illegally writing another document for obtaining a narcotic or psychotropic substance, as well as for dispensing a narcotic or psychotropic substance without a prescription or other document or knowing that the prescription or other document is fictitious or illegally written, if committed with narcotic or psychotropic substances in large amounts or has caused serious consequences, — shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of up to five years or by short-term imprisonment, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine, with deprivation of the right to engage in certain activities for a term of up to five years.

(3) For the criminal offence provided for in the first or second part of this Section, if committed by an organized group or if it has caused the death of two or more persons, — shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of up to ten years, with deprivation of the right to engage in certain activities for a term of up to five years and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years or without it.

(With amendments made by the laws of 25.04.2002., 13.12.2012., 25.09.2014., 07.01.2021. and 17.12.2020., which comes into force 01.01.2022.)

### 251.pants. Incitement to Use Narcotic, Psychotropic and New Psychoactive Substances

(1) For incitement to use narcotic or psychotropic substances or new psychoactive substances or products containing them, the circulation of which is prohibited or restricted, or providing premises for the use of these substances — shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of up to three years or by short-term imprisonment, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2) For the same acts, if committed against a minor, mentally ill person or person undergoing addiction treatment or who is in material or other dependence on the guilty person, or if substances that enhance their effect have been added to the narcotic or psychotropic substances, — shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of up to five years or by short-term imprisonment, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(3) For incitement to use narcotic or psychotropic substances or new psychoactive substances or products containing them, the circulation of which is prohibited or restricted, if the use of these substances has caused serious consequences, — shall be punished by imprisonment for a term from two to ten years and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years or without it.

(With amendments made by the laws of 25.04.2002., 13.12.2012., 15.05.2014. and 17.12.2020., which comes into force

### 252.pants. Administration of Narcotic, Psychotropic and New Psychoactive Substances Against a Person's Will

(1) For administering narcotic or psychotropic substances or new psychoactive substances or products containing them, the circulation of which is prohibited or restricted, to another person or adding them to a product intended for use by another person against that person's will or without their knowledge — shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of up to five years or by short-term imprisonment, or by probation supervision, or by community service.

(2) For the same acts, if substances that enhance their effect have been added to the narcotic or psychotropic substances, — shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of up to ten years and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years or without it.

(3) For the acts provided for in the first or second part of this Section, if committed against a minor or using violence or threatening to use violence or if they have caused serious consequences, — shall be punished by imprisonment for a term from three to twelve years and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years.

(With amendments made by the laws of 25.04.2002., 13.12.2012., 15.05.2014. and 17.12.2020., which comes into force

### 253.pants.

Unauthorized Manufacturing, Acquisition, Storage, Transportation and Forwarding of Narcotic and Psychotropic Substances

(1) For unauthorized manufacturing, acquisition, storage, transportation or forwarding of narcotic or psychotropic substances without intent to distribute them — shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of up to three years or by short-term imprisonment, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2) For the same acts, if committed by a group of persons pursuant to a prior agreement or if committed with narcotic or psychotropic substances in large amounts, — shall be punished by imprisonment for a term from three to ten years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property, and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years.

(17.10.2002. law edition with amendments made by the laws of 08.07.2011., 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which comes into force 01.01.2022.)

253.1 pants. Unauthorized Manufacturing, Acquisition, Storage, Transportation and Forwarding of Narcotic and Psychotropic Substances for the Purpose of Distribution and Unauthorized Distribution

(1) For unauthorized manufacturing, acquisition, storage, transportation or forwarding of narcotic or psychotropic substances for the purpose of distribution or for unauthorized distribution of narcotic or psychotropic substances — shall be punished by imprisonment for a term from two to eight years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property, and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years.

(2) For the same acts, if committed by a group of persons pursuant to a prior agreement, — shall be punished by imprisonment for a term from three to ten years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property, and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years.

(3) For the same acts, if committed by an organized group or if committed with narcotic or psychotropic substances in large amounts, or if they have caused serious consequences, as well as for distribution of narcotic or psychotropic substances to a minor, in educational institutions or on their territory, in restaurants, cafes, bars, public entertainment or celebration venues — shall be punished by imprisonment for a term from five to fifteen years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property, and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years.

(17.10.2002. law edition with amendments made by the laws of 08.07.2011., 13.12.2012. and 07.01.2021., which comes into force 02.02.2021.)

253.2 pants. Unauthorized Acquisition, Storage and Distribution of Narcotic and Psychotropic Substances in Small Amounts and Unauthorized Use of Narcotic and Psychotropic Substances

(1) For unauthorized acquisition or storage of narcotic or psychotropic substances in small amounts without intent to distribute them or unauthorized use of narcotic or psychotropic substances, if committed by a person who has been warned about criminal liability for unauthorized acquisition, storage and use of narcotic and psychotropic substances, — shall be punished by short-term imprisonment or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2) For unauthorized manufacturing, acquisition, storage, transportation or forwarding of narcotic or psychotropic substances in small amounts for the purpose of distribution or for unauthorized distribution of narcotic or psychotropic substances in small amounts — shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of up to three years or by short-term imprisonment, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(13.12.2012. law edition with amendments made by the law of 17.12.2020., which comes into force 01.01.2022.)

### 254.pants.

Release of persons from criminal liability for the acquisition, storage, transportation and forwarding of narcotic and psychotropic substances

A person who has voluntarily surrendered narcotic, psychotropic, new psychoactive substances or products containing them, the circulation of which is prohibited or restricted, or has voluntarily notified of their acquisition, storage, transportation or forwarding, shall be released from criminal liability for the use, acquisition, storage, transportation or forwarding of these substances.

(In the wording of the law of 17.10.2002. with amendments made by the law of 25.09.2014., which came into force on 29.10.2014.)

### 255.pants. Manufacturing, acquisition, storage, transportation, forwarding and distribution of equipment and substances (precursors)

intended for the unauthorised manufacture of narcotic and psychotropic substances

(1) For the manufacturing, acquisition, storage, transportation or forwarding of equipment, devices, articles, materials or substances (precursors exceeding a small amount) intended for the unauthorised manufacture of narcotic or psychotropic substances, the applicable sentence is deprivation of liberty for a term of up to three years, or short-term deprivation of liberty, or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.

(2) For the same acts, if they are committed with the intention to distribute such equipment, devices, articles, materials or substances (precursors), or for the distribution of equipment, devices, articles, materials or substances (precursors) intended for the unauthorised manufacture of narcotic or psychotropic substances — the applicable sentence is deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years, or short-term deprivation of liberty, or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.

(3) For the acts provided for in paragraph one or two of this Section, if they have been committed by an organised group, — the applicable sentence is deprivation of liberty for a term of three to ten years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property. (With amendments made by the laws of 25.04.2002., 21.06.2007., 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which came into force

### 256.pants. Unauthorised sowing and cultivation of plants containing narcotic and psychotropic substances

(1) For the unauthorised sowing or cultivation of plants containing narcotic or psychotropic substances — the applicable sentence is deprivation of liberty for a term of up to two years, or short-term deprivation of liberty, or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.

(2) For the unauthorised sowing or cultivation of plants containing narcotic or psychotropic substances on large areas, or if it has been committed by an organised group, — the applicable sentence is deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years, or short-term deprivation of liberty, or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property. (With amendments made by the laws of 25.04.2002., 12.02.2004., 13.12.2012., 07.01.2021. and 17.12.2020., which came into force on 01.01.2022.)

## XXI nodaļa

Criminal offences against traffic safety

### 257.pants. Violation of railway, water and air transport traffic safety and operation regulations

(1) For the violation of railway, water or air transport traffic safety or operation regulations or for the deliberate putting into operation of a technically defective railway, water or air vehicle, committed by a transport employee, if it has significantly disrupted transport operations, — the applicable sentence is deprivation of liberty for a term of up to three years, or short-term deprivation of liberty, or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.

(2) For the same offence, if it has caused serious consequences, — the applicable sentence is deprivation of liberty for a term of up to eight years.

(With amendments made by the laws of 12.02.2004., 21.10.2010., 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which came into force

### 258.pants. Damage to traffic roads and railway, water and air vehicles

(1) For the intentional destruction, damage or other intentional actions of traffic roads, road structures or facilities, railway, water or air vehicles, transport telecommunication networks or signalling means, electronic or communication equipment, as a result of which they have become unfit for operation, as well as if the said actions have caused disruptions to transport operations, — the applicable sentence is deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years, or short-term deprivation of liberty, or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.

(2) For the criminal offence provided for in paragraph one of this Section, if it has caused serious consequences, — the applicable sentence is deprivation of liberty for a term of up to ten years.

(With amendments made by the laws of 21.10.2010., 13.12.2012., 29.10.2015. and 17.12.2020., which came into force

### 259.pants. Arbitrary stopping of a train

(1) (Deleted by the law of 13.12.2012.)

(2) For the arbitrary stopping of a train without necessity with an emergency brake, by disconnecting the brake air line or otherwise, if it has caused a disaster, damage to rolling stock or other serious consequences, — the applicable sentence is deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years, or short-term deprivation of liberty, or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.

(3) For the same acts, if they have caused the death of two or more persons, — the applicable sentence is deprivation of liberty for a term of up to eight years.

(With amendments made by the laws of 13.12.2012., 25.09.2014. and 17.12.2020., which came into force on 01.01.2022.)

### 260.pants. Violation of road traffic regulations and vehicle operation regulations

(1) (Deleted by the law of 11.06.2020.)

(11) For violation of road traffic regulations or vehicle operation regulations, if it has been committed by a person operating a vehicle, and if as a result the victim has suffered moderate bodily harm, — the applicable sentence is deprivation of liberty for a term of up to three years, or short-term deprivation of liberty, or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.

(2) For violation of road traffic regulations or vehicle operation regulations, if it has been committed by a person operating a vehicle, and if as a result the victim has suffered serious bodily harm or it has caused the death of a person, — the applicable sentence is deprivation of liberty for a term of up to eight years, with revocation of driving licence for a term of up to five years.

(3) For violation of road traffic regulations or vehicle operation regulations, if it has been committed by a person operating a vehicle, and if it has caused the death of two or more persons, — the applicable sentence is deprivation of liberty for a term of three to twelve years, with revocation of driving licence for a term of up to seven years.

(With amendments made by the laws of 27.05.2004., 12.02.2004., 06.10.2005., 21.10.2010., 13.12.2012., 15.05.2014., 29.10.2015., 11.06.2020. and 17.12.2020., which came into force on 01.01.2022.)

### 261.pants. Concept of vehicle

Vehicles referred to in Sections 175, 176, 260, 262-265 and 284 of this Law shall mean all types of automobiles, tractors and other self-propelled machines, trams, trolleybuses, motorcycles and other mechanical vehicles that move with their own energy source, except vehicles with an internal combustion engine whose working volume is less than 50 cubic centimetres or an electric motor whose maximum power does not exceed four kilowatts and the maximum speed provided for in the design does not exceed 45 kilometres per hour.

(With amendments made by the laws of 13.12.2012., 06.06.2019. and 27.10.2022., which came into force on 25.11.2022.)

### 262.pants.

Driving a vehicle under the influence of alcohol, narcotic, psychotropic, toxic or other intoxicating substances

(1) For driving a vehicle without the appropriate category of driving licence (the driving licence has not been obtained in the prescribed manner or has been revoked) and if the driver is under the influence of alcohol, narcotic, psychotropic, toxic or other intoxicating substances, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to one year or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine, with the revocation of driving licence for five years.

(11) For driving a vehicle or teaching to drive a vehicle, if the alcohol concentration in the blood established by examination of exhaled air or blood exceeds 1.5 per mille, or for driving a vehicle or teaching to drive a vehicle while under the influence of narcotic, psychotropic, toxic or other intoxicating substances, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to one year or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine, with the revocation of driving licence for five years.

(2) For a violation of traffic regulations or vehicle operation rules, if committed by a person driving a vehicle under the influence of alcohol, narcotic, psychotropic, toxic or other intoxicating substances, and if as a result thereof the victim has sustained slight bodily injury, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to four years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine, with the revocation of driving licence for five years.

(3) For a violation of traffic regulations or vehicle operation rules, if committed by a person driving a vehicle under the influence of alcohol, narcotic, psychotropic, toxic or other intoxicating substances, and if as a result thereof the victim has sustained medium serious bodily injury, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine, with the revocation of driving licence for a term from five to seven years.

(4) For a violation of traffic regulations or vehicle operation rules, if committed by a person driving a vehicle under the influence of alcohol, narcotic, psychotropic, toxic or other intoxicating substances, and if as a result thereof the victim has sustained serious bodily injury or it has caused the death of a person, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term from three to ten years, with the revocation of driving licence for a term from five to ten years.

(5) For a violation of traffic regulations or vehicle operation rules, if committed by a person driving a vehicle under the influence of alcohol, narcotic, psychotropic, toxic or other intoxicating substances, and if it has caused the death of two or more persons, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term from three to fifteen years, with the revocation of driving licence for a term from five to ten years.

(As worded by the law of 29.10.2015. with amendments made by the laws of 22.06.2017., 17.12.2020. and 27.10.2022., which entered into force on 25.11.2022.)

262.1 pants. Refusal to undergo testing for the influence of alcohol, narcotic, psychotropic, toxic and other intoxicating substances and leaving the scene of a road traffic accident

(1) For refusal to undergo medical examination to determine the concentration of alcohol in the blood or testing for the influence of narcotic, psychotropic, toxic or other intoxicating substances, if committed by a vehicle driver, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to one year or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine, with the revocation of driving licence for five years.

(2) For refusal to undergo medical examination to determine the concentration of alcohol in the blood or testing for the influence of narcotic, psychotropic, toxic or other intoxicating substances or for leaving the scene of a road traffic accident after a road traffic accident, in violation of the prescribed procedure, if committed by a vehicle driver who caused a road traffic accident, as a result of which the victim sustained slight, medium or serious bodily injury or as a result of which the death of a person occurred, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to four years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service or by a fine, with the revocation of driving licence for a term from five to seven years.

(As worded by the law of 29.10.2015. with amendments made by the laws of 11.06.2020., 17.12.2020. and 27.10.2022., which entered into force on 25.11.2022.)

### 263.pants. Putting technically defective vehicles into operation

For deliberate putting of technically defective vehicles into operation or violation of other operation rules guaranteeing traffic safety, committed by a person who is responsible for the technical condition or operation of vehicles, if as a result thereof the victim has sustained medium serious or serious bodily injury or it has caused the death of a person, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine, with the revocation of the right to engage in specific employment for a term of up to five years.

(With amendments made by the laws of 12.02.2004., 13.12.2012., 29.10.2015., 11.06.2020. and 17.12.2020., which entered into force on 01.01.2022.)

### 264.pants

Permitting persons who are under the influence of alcohol, narcotic, psychotropic, toxic or other intoxicating substances to drive vehicles

For permitting a person who is under the influence of alcohol, narcotic, psychotropic, toxic or other intoxicating substances to drive a vehicle, if as a result thereof the victim sustained slight, medium serious or serious bodily injury or it has caused the death of a person, a person who is responsible for the technical condition or operation of vehicles, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine, with the revocation of the right to engage in specific employment for a term of up to five years.

(With amendments made by the laws of 27.05.2004., 13.12.2012., 29.10.2015. and 17.12.2020., which entered into force on

### 265. pants. Unlawful manufacturing, sale, issuance, falsification, destruction, theft of a vehicle registration document, unit and registration number plate

and use of a forgery

(1) For unlawful manufacturing, sale, issuance, falsification, destruction of a vehicle registration document, unit or registration number plate or use of a forged registration number plate shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to one year or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2) For theft of a vehicle registration document or registration number plate — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to two years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(3) For the acts provided for in paragraph one or two of this Article, if they have been committed by a group of persons according to prior agreement, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to three years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(With amendments made by the laws of 12.02.2004., 13.12.2012., 17.12.2020. and 27.10.2022., which entered into force on

### 266.pants. Violation of traffic regulations

(1) For violation of traffic order or safety protection regulations, if it has caused serious consequences, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term up to five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2) For the same acts, if they have caused the death of two or more persons, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term up to eight years.

(As amended by the laws of 12.02.2004., 13.12.2012., 25.09.2014. and 17.12.2020., which enters into force

### 267.pants. Failure to report vessel name in case of vessel collision

For failure to report to another vessel that has collided with his vessel the name and port of registration of his vessel, as well as the place of departure or destination, although it was possible to provide this information, the captain — shall be punished by short-term deprivation of liberty or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(As amended by the laws of 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which enters into force 01.01.2022.)

### 268.pants. Seizure of air and water transport

(1) For seizure of an air or water transport, except small-sized vehicles, on land, in waters or in flight — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term up to seven years and by probation supervision for a term up to three years or without it.

(2) For the same acts, if committed by a group of persons upon prior agreement or if committed using violence or threatening with violence, or if they have caused an accident or other serious consequences, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term from two to twelve years and by probation supervision for a term up to three years or without it.

(3) For the acts provided for in the first or second paragraph of this Section, if they have caused the death of two or more persons, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term from three to fifteen years and by probation supervision for a term up to three years.

(As amended by the laws of 13.12.2012. and 25.09.2014., which enters into force 29.10.2014. See transitional provision Paragraph 16)

## XXII nodaļa

Criminal Offences Against the Order of Administration

### 269.pants. Attack on a representative of authority and other public official

(1) For attack on a representative of authority or other public official in connection with their lawful official activity, as well as for attack on a person in connection with their participation in preventing or stopping a criminal or other unlawful act — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term up to five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2) For the same acts, if as a result of the attack serious bodily injuries have been inflicted or other serious consequences have been caused or if the attack has been committed by an organised group, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term from two to ten years and by probation supervision for a term up to three years or without it.

(As amended by the laws of 25.04.2002., 13.12.2007., 08.07.2011., 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which enters into force 01.01.2022.)

### 270.pants. Resistance to a representative of authority and other public official

(1) For resistance to a representative of authority or other public official, if they are performing their assigned official duties, or for resistance to a person, if they are participating in preventing or stopping a criminal or other unlawful act, or for forcing these persons to perform manifestly unlawful actions, if the resistance or forcing has been committed using violence or threatening with violence, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term up to one year or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2) For the same acts, if committed by a group of persons, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term up to three years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(As amended by the laws of 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which enters into force 01.01.2022.)

### 271.pants. Offence to the honour and dignity of a representative of authority and other public official

(Excluded by the law of 22.01.2004., which enters into force 01.02.2004.)

### 272.pants. Providing false information to a state institution

For knowingly providing false information to a state institution, including to a parliamentary investigation committee, if committed by a person who, according to law, has a duty to provide information to a state institution or parliamentary investigation committee, or for refusing to give an explanation, opinion or interpretation to a parliamentary investigation committee — shall be punished by short-term deprivation of liberty or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(In the wording of the law of 13.12.2012. as amended by the law of 17.12.2020., which enters into force 01.01.2022.)

272.1 pants. Forcing to give false explanation, opinion or interpretation to a parliamentary investigation committee

(1) For bribing a person or other unlawful influence for the purpose of making a person give false explanation, opinion or interpretation or refuse to give explanation, opinion or interpretation to a parliamentary investigation committee, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term up to three years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2) For the same acts, if they are connected with violence or threat of violence, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term up to five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(3) For the acts provided for in the first paragraph of this Section, if they are connected with torture, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term up to eight years.

(In the wording of the law of 18.12.2003. as amended by the laws of 19.11.2009., 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which enters into force 01.01.2022.)

### 273.pants. Arbitrary appropriation of the title and authority of a public official

For arbitrary appropriation of the title or authority of a public official for the purpose of committing a criminal offence — shall be punished by short-term deprivation of liberty or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(As amended by the laws of 12.02.2004., 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which enters into force 01.01.2022.)

### 274.pants. Theft and destruction of document, seal and stamp

(1) For theft, concealment, intentional destruction or damage of a document that grants rights or releases from obligations, seal or stamp, as well as for use or disposal of a stolen document, seal or stamp — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term up to one year or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2) For the same acts, if committed for mercenary purpose or if they have caused substantial harm, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term up to three years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(As amended by the laws of 12.02.2004., 13.12.2012., 17.12.2020. and 07.04.2022., which enters into force

### 275.pants. Forgery of Documents, Seals and Stamps and Realization and Use of Forged Documents, Seals and Stamps

(1) For forgery of a document that grants rights or releases from obligations, a seal or a stamp, as well as for realization or use of a forged document, seal or stamp — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term not exceeding one year or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2) For the same acts, if committed with mercenary intent or if committed by a group of persons pursuant to prior agreement, or if they have caused substantial harm — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term not exceeding four years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(With amendments made by the Laws of 12.02.2004., 13.12.2012., 06.06.2019., 17.12.2020. and 07.04.2022., which enters into force on 04.05.2022.)

275.1 pants. Obtaining an Identity Document Using Another Person's Data

For obtaining an identity document using another person's data — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term not exceeding one year or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(In the wording of the Law of 13.12.2012. with amendments made by the Law of 17.12.2020., which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

275.2 pants. Obtaining and Possession of a Forged Interoperable Certificate

For obtaining or possessing a forged interoperable vaccination, testing or recovery certificate — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term not exceeding one year or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(In the wording of the Law of 11.11.2021. with amendments made by the Law of 07.04.2022., which enters into force on 04.05.2022.)

275.3 pants. Release from Criminal Liability of the Acquirer, Possessor and User of a Forged Interoperable Certificate

A person who has acquired, possessed or used a forged interoperable vaccination, testing or recovery certificate may be released from criminal liability if, after committing the criminal acts, such person voluntarily reports the occurrence and actively facilitates the detection and investigation of the criminal offence.

(In the wording of the Law of 11.11.2021., which enters into force on 14.11.2021.)

### 276.pants. Unlawful Opening and Destruction of Postal Shipment

(1) For unlawful opening or destruction of a postal shipment — shall be punished by short-term deprivation of liberty or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2) For the same acts, if committed by an employee of postal, railway, water or air transport — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term not exceeding one year or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(With amendments made by the Laws of 12.02.2004., 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

### 277. pants. Unauthorized Actions with Archive Documents

For damaging, concealing or unlawfully destroying, alienating, storing, moving, sending or exporting from the Republic of Latvia or importing into the Republic of Latvia an archive document, a document with archival value or a copy substituting such documents under the protection of the Republic of Latvia or another state, if substantial harm has been caused thereby — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term not exceeding three years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(In the wording of the Law of 13.12.2012. with amendments made by the Laws of 07.01.2021., 17.12.2020. and 07.04.2022., which enters into force on 04.05.2022.)

### 278.pants. Forgery of Postal Payment Marks

For forgery of postage stamps or other postal payment marks or international postal reply coupons, as well as for use of forged or used postage stamps or other postal payment marks or international postal reply coupons for postal shipments or sale for the same purpose — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term not exceeding one year or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(With amendments made by the Laws of 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

### 279.pants. Arbitrariness

(1) For performing an act arbitrarily, bypassing the procedure established in a regulatory enactment, if the lawfulness of such act is contested by a state or local government institution or another person (arbitrariness) and if substantial harm has been caused as a result of such act — shall be punished by short-term deprivation of liberty or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2) For the criminal offence provided for in Paragraph one of this Section, if committed by a group of persons pursuant to prior agreement — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term not exceeding three years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(3) For arbitrariness, if it is connected with violence or threat of violence or if it has caused serious consequences — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term not exceeding five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(With amendments made by the Laws of 13.12.2012., 29.10.2015. and 17.12.2020., which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

### 280.pants. Violation of Employment Regulations

(1) For violation of employment restrictions or regulations provided for by law, if committed by an employer and if substantial harm has been caused thereby — shall be punished by short-term deprivation of liberty or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2) For employing a person who is not entitled to stay in the Republic of Latvia, if committed by an employer and if a minor is employed or if more than five persons are employed, or if a person is employed in particularly exploitative working conditions, or if a victim of trafficking in human beings is knowingly employed — shall be punished by short-term deprivation of liberty or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(In the wording of the Law of 16.06.2011. with amendments made by the Laws of 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which enters into force on

### 281.pants. Concealment of Person's Identity

(1) For concealing one's identity while staying in the Republic of Latvia without a proper identity document or using another person's or forged identity document — shall be punished by short-term deprivation of liberty or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2) For concealing one's identity, if committed with the intent to evade criminal liability or administrative liability or to commit a criminal offence or with the intent to help another person evade criminal liability or administrative liability — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term not exceeding one year or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(With amendments made by the Laws of 12.02.2004., 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

281.1 pants. Impersonation of Another Person

(1) (Deleted by the Law of 13.12.2012.)

(2) For impersonating another person when taking an examination of Latvian language proficiency or other knowledge specified in the Citizenship Law in order to create an opportunity for such person to acquire Latvian citizenship through naturalization, if committed with mercenary intent — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term not exceeding one year or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(In the wording of the Law of 21.06.2007. with amendments made by the Laws of 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which enters into force on

### 282.pants. Evasion of conscription for mandatory military service

(Deleted by the law of 14.12.2006, which entered into force on 01.01.2007.)

282.1 pants. Evasion of mobilization

(1) For intentional evasion of mobilization, if committed by a person subject to mobilization for civil protection measures, — shall be punished by short-term deprivation of liberty or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.

(2) For the same acts, if committed by a reserve soldier, reservist or National Guard member, — shall be punished by short-term deprivation of liberty or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine. (In the wording of the law of 17.10.2002. with amendments made by the laws of 13.12.2012., 17.12.2020. and 27.10.2022., which entered into force on 25.11.2022.)

282.2 pants. Obstruction of mobilization activities and failure to comply with mobilization requirements

(1) For intentional obstruction of the execution of an order of a competent state institution during mobilization or for intentional obstruction of the execution of a municipal order when carrying out civil protection measures, or for intentional failure to comply with other mobilization activities or mobilization requirements — shall be punished by short-term deprivation of liberty or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.

(2) For the same acts, if they have caused substantial harm, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to one year or short-term deprivation of liberty, or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.
