(In the wording of the law of 17.10.2002. with amendments made by the laws of 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which entered into force on 282.3 pants. Evasion of alternative service

(Deleted by the law of 14.12.2006, which entered into force on 01.01.2007.)

### 283.pants. Violation of state border regime

(Deleted by the law of 11.12.2003, which entered into force on 02.01.2004.)

### 284.pants. Illegal crossing of state border

(1) For intentional illegal crossing of the external state border — shall be punished by short-term deprivation of liberty or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.

(11) For intentional illegal crossing of the state border, if committed by a person for whom a prohibition on departure from the Republic of Latvia has been imposed to prevent a threat to national security, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to one year or short-term deprivation of liberty, or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.

(2) For the acts provided for in the first paragraph, if committed by a group of persons or using a vehicle, or disregarding the imposed prohibition on entry into the Republic of Latvia, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to two years or short-term deprivation of liberty, or probation supervision, or community service, or a fine.

(In the wording of the law of 13.12.2012. with amendments made by the laws of 08.06.2017., 26.04.2018., 17.12.2020. and 14.12.2023., which entered into force on 30.12.2023.)

### 285.pants. Illegal Movement of Persons Across the State Border

(1) For illegal movement of a person across the state border, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to three years or by probation supervision, or by a fine.

(2) For the same acts, if committed by a state official using their official position, or by a group of persons pursuant to a prior agreement, or for illegal movement of several persons across the state border in one instance — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to six years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property, and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years or without it.

(3) For the same acts, if committed by an organised group or if they have caused serious consequences, or for illegal movement of a large number of persons, that is, more than five persons in one instance, across the state border — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of two to eight years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property, and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years or without it.

(31) For the same acts, if committed during the period of operation of the enhanced border protection system or during a state of emergency declared due to a threat to the inviolability of the state border, regardless of the place of commission of the offence in the territory of Latvia — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of two to ten years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property, and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years or without it.

(4) For the same acts, if they have caused the death of two or more persons, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of three to fifteen years and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years.

(Ar grozījumiem, kas izdarīti ar 25.04.2002., 11.12.2003., 13.12.2007., 08.07.2011., 13.12.2012., 25.09.2014., 10.03.2016., 17.12.2020. un 05.10.2023. likumu, kas stājas spēkā 20.10.2023. Sk. pārejas noteikumu 25.2 punktu) 285.1 pants. Nodrošināšana ar iespēju nelikumīgi uzturēties Latvijas Republikā

(1) Par apzinātu personas nodrošināšanu ar iespēju nelikumīgi uzturēties Latvijas Republikā, ja to izdarījusi personu grupa vai valsts amatpersona, izmantojot savu dienesta stāvokli, — soda ar brīvības atņemšanu uz laiku līdz trim gadiem vai ar probācijas uzraudzību, vai ar naudas sodu.

(2) Par apzinātu personas nodrošināšanu ar iespēju nelikumīgi uzturēties Latvijas Republikā, ja tas izdarīts mantkārīgā nolūkā vai ja šāda iespēja nodrošināta divām vai vairākām personām, — soda ar brīvības atņemšanu uz laiku līdz sešiem gadiem, konfiscējot mantu vai bez mantas konfiskācijas, un ar probācijas uzraudzību uz laiku līdz trim gadiem vai bez tās.

(3) Par apzinātu personas nodrošināšanu ar iespēju nelikumīgi uzturēties Latvijas Republikā, ja to izdarījusi organizēta grupa vai ja ar to radītas smagas sekas, vai par liela personu skaita, tas ir, vienā gadījumā vairāk nekā piecu personu, nodrošināšanu ar iespēju nelikumīgi uzturēties Latvijas Republikā — soda ar brīvības atņemšanu uz laiku no diviem līdz astoņiem gadiem, konfiscējot mantu vai bez mantas konfiskācijas, un ar probācijas uzraudzību uz laiku līdz trim gadiem vai bez tās.

(13.12.2012. likuma redakcijā ar grozījumiem, kas izdarīti ar 10.03.2016., 17.12.2020. un 05.10.2023. likumu, kas 285.2 pants. Ļaunprātīga nodrošināšana ar iespēju likumīgi iegūt tiesības uzturēties Latvijas Republikā, citā Eiropas Savienības dalībvalstī, Eiropas Ekonomikas zonas valstī vai Šveices Konfederācijā

(1) Par ļaunprātīgu personas nodrošināšanu ar iespēju likumīgi iegūt tiesības uzturēties Latvijas Republikā, citā Eiropas Savienības dalībvalstī, Eiropas Ekonomikas zonas valstī vai Šveices Konfederācijā — soda ar brīvības atņemšanu uz laiku līdz trim gadiem vai ar probācijas uzraudzību, vai ar naudas sodu.

(2) Par ļaunprātīgu personas nodrošināšanu ar iespēju likumīgi iegūt tiesības uzturēties Latvijas Republikā, citā Eiropas Savienības dalībvalstī, Eiropas Ekonomikas zonas valstī vai Šveices Konfederācijā, ja tas izdarīts mantkārīgā nolūkā vai ja šāda iespēja nodrošināta divām vai vairākām personām, vai ja to izdarījusi personu grupa, — soda ar brīvības atņemšanu uz laiku līdz sešiem gadiem, konfiscējot mantu vai bez mantas konfiskācijas, un ar probācijas uzraudzību uz laiku līdz trim gadiem vai bez tās.

(3) Par ļaunprātīgu personas nodrošināšanu ar iespēju likumīgi iegūt tiesības uzturēties Latvijas Republikā, citā Eiropas Savienības dalībvalstī, Eiropas Ekonomikas zonas valstī vai Šveices Konfederācijā, ja to izdarījusi organizēta grupa vai ja ar to radītas smagas sekas, vai par liela personu skaita, tas ir, vienā gadījumā vairāk nekā piecu personu, nodrošināšanu ar iespēju likumīgi iegūt tiesības uzturēties Latvijas Republikā, citā Eiropas Savienības dalībvalstī, Eiropas Ekonomikas zonas valstī vai Šveices Konfederācijā — soda ar brīvības atņemšanu uz laiku no diviem līdz astoņiem gadiem, konfiscējot mantu vai bez mantas konfiskācijas, un ar probācijas uzraudzību uz laiku līdz trim gadiem vai bez tās.

(13.12.2012. likuma redakcijā ar grozījumiem, kas izdarīti ar 17.12.2020. un 05.10.2023. likumu, kas stājas spēkā

### 286.pants. Unauthorised Raising of the Flag of the State of Latvia on a Vessel

Par Latvijas valsts karoga pacelšanu uz kuģa, ja uz to nav tiesību, — soda ar īslaicīgu brīvības atņemšanu vai ar probācijas uzraudzību, vai ar sabiedrisko darbu, vai ar naudas sodu.

(Ar grozījumiem, kas izdarīti ar 12.02.2004., 13.12.2012. un 17.12.2020. likumu, kas stājas spēkā 01.01.2022.)

### 287.pants. Unauthorised Use of the Red Cross, Red Crescent and Blue and White Shield Signs

Par Sarkanā Krusta, Sarkanā Pusmēness vai Zili Baltā Vairoga zīmju, kā arī Sarkanā Krusta vai Sarkanā Pusmēness nosaukuma neatļautu lietošanu — soda ar īslaicīgu brīvības atņemšanu vai ar probācijas uzraudzību, vai ar sabiedrisko darbu, vai ar naudas sodu. (18.12.2003. likuma redakcijā ar grozījumiem, kas izdarīti ar 13.12.2012. un 17.12.2020. likumu, kas stājas spēkā

### 288.pants. Damage to Telecommunication Equipment, Radio and Television Transmitters and Postal Technological Equipment

For intentional destruction or damage to telecommunication equipment, radio or television transmitter or postal technological equipment, if it causes substantial harm, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to four years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(As amended by the Law of 13.12.2012., with amendments made by the Law of 17.12.2020., which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

288.1 pants. Violation of the Equipment Installation and Use Procedures Provided for in Regulatory Enactments for Broadcasting and Distribution of Radio and Television Programs

(1) (Deleted by the Law of 13.12.2012.)

(2) For violation of the installation or use procedures for radio wave emitting equipment provided for in regulatory enactments for broadcasting or distribution of radio or television programs, as well as cable network systems for distribution of television or sound broadcasting signals, if it has caused substantial harm, — shall be punished by short-term deprivation of liberty or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine. (As amended by the Law of 31.10.2002., with amendments made by the Laws of 13.12.2012., 17.12.2020. and 07.04.2022., which enters into force on 04.05.2022.) financing

(1) For illegal financing of a political organization (party) or alliance of political organizations (parties) on a large scale — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to two years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2) For the acts provided for in paragraph one of this Article, if they have been committed by a group of persons pursuant to a prior agreement, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to four years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(As amended by the Law of 08.09.2011., with amendments made by the Laws of 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which enters into force 288.3 pants. Intermediation in Illegal Financing of a Political Organization (Party) or Alliance of Political Organizations (Parties)

For intermediation in illegal financing of a political organization (party) or alliance of political organizations (parties) on a large scale — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to two years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(As amended by the Law of 08.09.2011., with amendments made by the Laws of 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which enters into force acceptance and extortion of funding

(1) For acceptance of illegal funding of a political organization (party) or alliance of political organizations (parties) on a large scale — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to three years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2) For the acts provided for in paragraph one of this Article, if they have been committed by a group of persons pursuant to a prior agreement or if they are related to demanding funding for a political organization (party) or alliance of political organizations (parties) on a large scale, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to four years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(3) For extortion of funding of a political organization (party) or alliance of political organizations (parties) — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(As amended by the Law of 08.09.2011., with amendments made by the Laws of 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which enters into force 288.5 pants. Release from Criminal Liability of a Person Who Has Illegally Financed Political Organizations (Parties) or Alliances of Political Organizations (Parties)

(1) A person who has illegally financed a political organization (party) or alliance of political organizations (parties) may be released from criminal liability if the illegal financing of the political organization (party) or alliance of political organizations (parties) is related to extortion or if this person, after the illegal financing of the political organization (party) or alliance of political organizations (parties), voluntarily reports what has occurred and actively facilitates the detection and investigation of the criminal offense.

(2) Extortion shall be understood as demanding financing of a political organization (party) or alliance of political organizations (parties) which is related to threats to harm the legitimate interests of the person.

(3) A supporter and intermediary of illegal financing of a political organization (party) or alliance of political organizations (parties) may be released from criminal liability if he or she, after the illegal financing of the political organization (party) or alliance of political organizations (parties), voluntarily reports what has occurred and actively facilitates the detection and investigation of the criminal offense.

(As amended by the Law of 08.09.2011., with amendments made by the Law of 13.12.2012., which enters into force on 01.04.2013.)

## XXIII nodaļa

Criminal Offenses Against Jurisdiction

### 289.pants. Falsification of Evidence

(1) For deliberate creation of false evidence or deliberate concealment of existing evidence, if it has been committed by a judge, prosecutor or investigator, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to one year or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2) For the same acts, if they have been committed with mercenary intent or in cases concerning serious or particularly serious crimes, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to three years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(With amendments made by the Laws of 12.02.2004., 21.05.2009., 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2022., which enters into force

### 290.pants. Bringing to Criminal Liability a Person Knowing That the Person Is Not Guilty

(1) For bringing to criminal liability a person knowing that the person is not guilty, if it has been committed by a prosecutor, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to three years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2) For the same acts, if they have been committed with mercenary intent or if they are related to an accusation of committing a serious or particularly serious crime or to falsification of evidence, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(With amendments made by the Laws of 21.05.2009., 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

### 291.pants. Making of an Unlawful Judgment or Decision

(1) For deliberately making an unlawful judgment or decision, if it has been committed by a judge, prosecutor or investigator, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to three years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2) For the same acts, if they have been committed with mercenary intent or if they are related to an accusation of committing a serious or particularly serious crime or to falsification of evidence, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(With amendments made by the Laws of 21.05.2009., 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

### 292.pants. Deliberately Unlawful Detention

For deliberately unlawful detention, if it has been committed by a judge, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to one year or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(With amendments made by the Laws of 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

### 293.pants. Deliberately Unlawful Detention and Forced Bringing

For deliberately unlawful detention or forced bringing, if it has been committed for mercenary purposes or out of revenge, or for other personal interest and if it has been committed by a judge, prosecutor or employee of a pre-trial investigation institution, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term up to one year or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(With amendments made by the laws of 12.02.2004., 21.05.2009., 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which come into force

### 294.pants. Forcing to Give Testimony

(1) For forcing to give testimony during interrogation, if it is connected with violence, threat of violence, mockery of the person being interrogated or committed in another way and if it has been committed by an official conducting pre-trial criminal proceedings, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term up to five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2) For forcing to give testimony during interrogation, if it is connected with torture and if it has been committed by an official conducting pre-trial criminal proceedings, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term up to ten years.

(With amendments made by the laws of 21.05.2009., 19.11.2009., 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which come into force 294.1 pants. Interference in Pre-trial Criminal Proceedings

(1) For any influencing of an official conducting pre-trial criminal proceedings with the purpose of obstructing the initiation or conduct of pre-trial criminal proceedings or achieving the adoption of an unlawful decision, — shall be punished by short-term deprivation of liberty or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2) For the same acts, if they have been committed by a state official, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term up to one year or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term up to one year or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine, with deprivation of the right to hold a specific position for a term up to five years.

(3) (Deleted by the law of 13.12.2012.)

(In the wording of the law of 25.04.2002. with amendments made by the laws of 12.02.2004., 13.12.2007., 21.05.2009., 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which come into force 01.01.2022.)

### 295.pants. Interference in Trial of Cases

(1) For any influencing of a judge with the purpose of obstructing the lawful trial of a case or achieving the adoption and pronouncement of an unlawful judgment or decision — shall be punished by short-term deprivation of liberty or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2) For the same acts, if they have been committed by a state official, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term up to one year or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine, with deprivation of the right to hold a specific position for a term up to five years.

(3) (Deleted by the law of 13.12.2012.)

(With amendments made by the laws of 25.04.2002., 12.02.2004., 13.12.2007., 21.05.2009., 13.12.2012., 11.06.2020. and 17.12.2020., which come into force 01.01.2022.)

### 296.pants. Non-execution of Court Decision and Prosecutor's Order on Punishment

(1) For non-execution or delay of execution of a court decision or prosecutor's order on punishment, if it has been committed by a person who according to law or assigned task was obliged to execute it, — shall be punished by short-term deprivation of liberty or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2) For the same acts, if they have been committed by a state official who had an obligation to execute the court decision or prosecutor's order on punishment, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty up to one year or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(With amendments made by the laws of 13.12.2012., 29.10.2015. and 17.12.2020., which come into force 01.01.2022.)

### 297.pants. False Impersonation of the Accused, Victim and Witness

A person who in pre-trial criminal proceedings or in court has falsely impersonated the accused, victim or witness, — shall be punished by short-term deprivation of liberty or by probation supervision, or by community service or by a fine.

(With amendments made by the laws of 21.05.2009., 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which come into force 01.01.2022.)

### 298.pants. Deliberately False Report

(1) For a deliberately false report about the commission of a criminal offense with the purpose of initiating criminal proceedings against a specific person — shall be punished by short-term deprivation of liberty or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2) For the same acts, if they are connected with accusation of commission of a grave or especially grave crime or with creation of false evidence of accusation or if they have been committed for mercenary purposes, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term up to two years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(With amendments made by the laws of 12.02.2004., 21.05.2009., 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which come into force

### 299.pants. Deliberately False Notification

For a deliberately false notification, declaration or submission to a notary or bailiff, if the submission of the notification, declaration or submission is prescribed by law, — shall be punished by short-term deprivation of liberty or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine. (With amendments made by the laws of 17.10.2002., 12.02.2004., 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which come into force

### 300.pants.

Giving Deliberately False Testimony, Opinion, Translation, Explanation and Statement

(1) For giving deliberately false testimony, opinion, translation, explanation or statement in administrative violation proceedings in an institution, in pre-trial criminal proceedings, in court, to a notary or bailiff, if it has been committed by a person who has been warned about criminal liability for giving deliberately false testimony, opinion, translation, explanation or statement, — shall be punished by short-term deprivation of liberty or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2) For the same acts, if they have been committed during the conduct of pre-trial criminal proceedings or trial in court of cases concerning a grave or especially grave crime, or if they have had grave consequences, or if they have been committed for mercenary purposes, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term up to three years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(With amendments made by the laws of 17.10.2002., 27.05.2004., 12.02.2004., 21.05.2009., 21.10.2010., 13.12.2012., 15.05.2014., 06.06.2019. and 17.12.2020., which come into force 01.01.2022.)

300.1 pants. Submission of Forged Material or Written Evidence

For submission of forged material or written evidence to an investigation institution, prosecutor's office or court — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term up to one year or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service or by a fine.

(In the wording of the law of 25.09.2014. with amendments made by the law of 17.12.2020., which come into force 01.01.2022.)


This excerpt sits under: Izdevējs: Saeima Publicēts: > XXIII nodaļa

### 301.pants. Coercion to Give False Testimony, Explanation, Opinion and Interpretation

(1) For bribery or other unlawful influencing of a witness, victim, person against whom criminal proceedings have been initiated, detainee, suspect, accused, applicant, expert or interpreter with the intent to cause them to give false testimony or a sworn false explanation to a court in an administrative matter, or an opinion, or to perform a false interpretation, or to refuse to give testimony or an opinion or to perform an interpretation — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to three years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2) For the same acts, if they are related to violence or threat of violence, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(3) For the acts provided for in the first paragraph of this Article, if they are related to torture, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to ten years.

(With amendments made by the laws of 27.05.2004., 12.02.2004., 21.05.2009., 19.11.2009., 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which enter into force on 01.01.2022.)

### 302.pants. Refusal to Give Testimony, Opinion and to Perform Interpretation

(1) For unjustified refusal of a witness, victim or other person warned about refusal to testify to give testimony in administrative offense proceedings in an institution, pre-trial investigation institution, prosecutor's office or court — shall be punished by short-term deprivation of liberty or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2) For unjustified refusal of an expert or interpreter to fulfill their assigned duties in administrative offense proceedings in an institution, pre-trial investigation institution, prosecutor's office or court — shall be punished by short-term deprivation of liberty or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine. (With amendments made by the laws of 21.05.2009., 21.10.2010., 13.12.2012., 11.06.2020. and 17.12.2020., which enter into force on 01.01.2022.)

### 303.pants. Persons Who Are Not Liable for Refusal to Give Testimony

The following persons are not liable for refusal to give testimony: the fiancé, spouse, parents, children, brothers and sisters, grandparents and grandchildren of the person called to administrative liability, person against whom criminal proceedings have been initiated, detainee, suspect, accused, as well as the person with whom the natural person who has committed an administrative offense or criminal offense lives together and with whom they have a common (undivided) household.

(In the wording of the law of 11.06.2020., which enters into force on 06.07.2020.)

### 304.pants. Disclosure of Information Obtained in Pre-trial Criminal Proceedings

For disclosure of information obtained in pre-trial criminal proceedings without the permission of the investigator or prosecutor until the completion of these proceedings, if committed by a person who has been warned about non-disclosure of the relevant information, — shall be punished by short-term deprivation of liberty or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine. (In the wording of the law of 21.05.2009. with amendments made by the laws of 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which enter into force

### 305.pants. Violation of Special Protection Rules for Persons

(1) For non-compliance with the legally established special protection procedure for persons, as well as for disclosure of identity data or location of a protected person, committed by a person who, in connection with the performance of official duties or other circumstances, has had knowledge of information about a specially protected person and who has been warned about non-disclosure of this information, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to one year or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2) For the same acts, if committed with mercenary intent, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to two years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(3) For deliberate disclosure of the organization, methods, tactics, means of special protection measures or information about persons involved in the implementation of protection measures, committed by a protected person, if as a result of these acts death of a person has occurred or other serious consequences have been caused, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(4) For the acts provided for in the first or second paragraph of this Article, if as a result death of a person has occurred or other serious consequences have been caused, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to seven years.

(In the wording of the law of 05.05.2005. with amendments made by the laws of 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which enter into force

### 306.pants. Non-production of Evidence

For deliberate non-production of objects, documents or other materials that may have evidentiary value in a criminal case, if requested to be produced by a pre-trial investigation institution, prosecutor's office or court from a person who is not a detainee, person against whom criminal proceedings have been initiated, suspect, accused, — shall be punished by short-term deprivation of liberty or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(With amendments made by the laws of 21.05.2009., 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which enter into force on 01.01.2022.)

### 307.pants. Unlawful Actions with Criminal Case Materials

For theft, deliberate destruction, damage or falsification of criminal case materials — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to three years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(With amendments made by the laws of 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which enter into force on 01.01.2022.)

307.1 pants. Violation of the Prohibition on Reproduction of Criminal Case Materials and Dissemination of Their Content

For violation of the prohibition to reproduce criminal case materials in any way or the prohibition to transfer to the disposal of third parties or otherwise disseminate non-publishable information arising from criminal case materials, if committed by a person who has been warned about criminal liability for violation of the mentioned prohibitions, — shall be punished by short-term deprivation of liberty or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(In the wording of the law of 11.10.2018. with amendments made by the law of 17.12.2020., which enter into force on 01.01.2022.)

### 308. pants. Unlawful Actions with Pledged, Seized and Confiscated Property

(1) For squandering, damage, alienation, concealment or destruction of pledged, seized or confiscated in administrative offense proceedings property, as well as for its exchange — shall be punished by short-term deprivation of liberty or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2) For the same acts, if committed by a person to whom this property has been entrusted, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to one year or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(With amendments made by the laws of 12.02.2004., 13.12.2012., 08.06.2017., 11.06.2020. and 17.12.2020., which enter into force on 01.01.2022.)

### 309.pants. Illegal Transfer of Substances and Objects to Persons and Receipt from Persons Who Are Placed in Short-term Detention and Imprisonment Facilities

(1) For the unauthorized transfer of correspondence, money, food products or other objects or substances to persons who are placed in short-term detention or imprisonment facilities, or receipt from such persons, if committed by an employee of these institutions, — shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of up to one year or by short-term imprisonment, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine, with deprivation of the right to hold a specific position for a term of up to five years.

(2) For the transfer of narcotic, psychotropic substances, explosives, weapons or ammunition to persons who are placed in short-term detention or imprisonment facilities, or receipt from such persons — shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of up to six years and by probation supervision for a term of up to three years or without it.

(3) For the acts provided for in the second paragraph of this Section, if committed by an employee of a short-term detention or imprisonment facility, — shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of up to eight years, with deprivation of the right to hold a specific position for a term from two to five years, and by probation supervision for a term of up to three years or without it.

(13.12.2012. likuma redakcijā ar grozījumiem, kas izdarīti ar 17.12.2020. likumu, kas stājas spēkā 01.01.2022.)

### 310.pants. Escape from a Short-term Detention and Imprisonment Facility

(1) For escape from a short-term detention or imprisonment facility — shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of up to one year or by short-term imprisonment, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2) For the same acts, if they are connected with violence or threat of violence against guards or another short-term detention or imprisonment facility official or if committed by a group of persons, — shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of up to five years or by short-term imprisonment, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine and by probation supervision for a term of up to three years or without it.

(Ar grozījumiem, kas izdarīti ar 21.05.2009., 13.12.2012. un 17.12.2020. likumu, kas stājas spēkā 01.01.2022.)

### 311.pants. Attack in an Imprisonment Facility

For the creation of an organized group of persons who are in an imprisonment facility, or participation in such a group, for the purpose of committing an attack or using other violence against an official of the imprisonment facility, a prisoner or any other person who is in the imprisonment facility, as well as for such an attack or violence — shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of up to eight years and by probation supervision for a term of up to three years. (Ar grozījumiem, kas izdarīti ar 13.12.2007., 21.05.2009., 08.07.2011. un 13.12.2012. likumu, kas stājas spēkā 01.04.2013. Sk. pārejas noteikumu 11.punktu)

### 312. pants. Evasion from Execution of a Court Decision

(1) For evasion from execution of a court decision imposing an obligation to perform certain actions, except a decision on collection of money or return of property, after application of a procedural sanction or penalty for non-execution of the decision — shall be punished by short-term imprisonment or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2) For evasion from serving a sentence of deprivation of liberty or restriction of rights — shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of up to one year or by short-term imprisonment, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(06.06.2019. likuma redakcijā ar grozījumiem, kas izdarīti ar 17.12.2020. likumu, kas stājas spēkā 01.01.2022.)

### 313.pants. Concealment Not Promised in Advance

(1) For concealment not promised in advance of a criminal, as well as instruments and means of committing a crime, traces of a crime, if the crime being concealed is serious, — shall be punished by short-term imprisonment or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2) For the same acts, if the crime being concealed is particularly serious, — shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of up to two years or by short-term imprisonment, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(Ar grozījumiem, kas izdarīti ar 12.02.2004., 13.12.2012., 06.06.2019. un 17.12.2020. likumu, kas stājas spēkā

### 314. pants. Acquisition, Storage and Disposal of Property Obtained by Criminal Means

(1) For the acquisition, storage or disposal of property, the value of which does not exceed a substantial amount, being aware that it was obtained by criminal means, — shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of up to one year or by short-term imprisonment, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2) (Izslēgta ar 22.06.2017. likumu)

(3) (Izslēgta ar 22.06.2017. likumu)

(Ar grozījumiem, kas izdarīti ar 25.04.2002., 12.02.2004., 13.12.2007., 08.07.2011., 13.12.2012., 22.06.2017. un 17.12.2020. likumu, kas stājas spēkā 01.01.2022.)

### 315.pants. Failure to Report a Crime

For failure to report, if it is known that a serious or particularly serious crime is being prepared or committed, — shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of up to two years or by short-term imprisonment, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(Ar grozījumiem, kas izdarīti ar 13.12.2012., 12.11.2015. un 17.12.2020. likumu, kas stājas spēkā 01.01.2022.)

## XXIV nodaļa

Criminal Offences in the Service of State Institutions

### 316.pants. Concept of State Official

(1) State officials shall be considered representatives of state power, as well as any person who permanently or temporarily performs state or local government service duties, including in a state or local government capital company, and who has the right to make decisions that are binding on other persons, or who has the right to perform supervisory, control, investigation or punishment functions or manage public person's or its capital company's property or financial resources.

(2) The President of the State, members of the Saeima, the Prime Minister, members of the Cabinet of Ministers, as well as officials of state institutions elected, appointed or confirmed by the Saeima and the Cabinet of Ministers, heads of local governments, their deputies and executive directors shall be considered state officials holding a responsible position.

(3) State officials shall also be considered officials of international organizations, international parliamentary assemblies and international courts and persons delegated by these institutions, as well as any person holding a legislative, executive or judicial office in a foreign country or any of its administrative units, regardless of whether this person is appointed or elected to the position, as well as any person performing a public function in the interests of a foreign country, including its administrative unit, state agency or state enterprise.

(4) A foreign country within the meaning of this Section is any territory outside the Republic of Latvia.

(Ar grozījumiem, kas izdarīti ar 25.04.2002., 11.12.2003., 13.12.2012., 15.05.2014. un 10.03.2016. likumu, kas stājas spēkā 07.04.2016.)

### 317.pants. Exceeding Official Authority

(1) For intentional actions committed by a state official that manifestly exceed the boundaries of rights and powers granted to the state official by law or an assigned task, if such actions have caused substantial harm, — shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of up to three years, or by short-term imprisonment, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2) For the actions specified in the first paragraph of this Section, if they are connected with violence or a threat of violence, or if they are committed with mercenary intent, — shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of up to five years, or by short-term imprisonment, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine, with deprivation of the right to hold a certain position for a term of up to five years.

(3) For the actions specified in the first paragraph of this Section, if they are connected with torture or if they have caused serious consequences, — shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of up to ten years, with deprivation of the right to hold a certain position for a term of up to five years.

(With amendments made by the laws of 12.02.2004., 19.11.2009., 13.12.2012., 06.06.2019., 06.07.2021. and 17.12.2020., which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

### 318.pants. Abuse of Official Position

(1) For intentional actions committed by a state official, abusing their official position, if such actions have caused substantial harm, — shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of up to three years, or by short-term imprisonment, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2) For the actions specified in the first paragraph of this Section, if they are committed with mercenary intent, — shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of up to four years, or by short-term imprisonment, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine, with deprivation of the right to hold a certain position for a term of up to five years.

(3) For intentional actions committed by a state official, abusing their official position, if such actions have caused serious consequences, — shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of up to five years, or by short-term imprisonment, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine, with deprivation of the right to hold a certain position for a term of up to five years.

(4) For intentional actions committed by a state official, abusing their official position, if such actions have caused the death of two or more persons, — shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of up to eight years, with deprivation of the right to hold a certain position for a term of up to five years.

(With amendments made by the laws of 12.02.2004., 13.12.2012., 25.09.2014., 06.06.2019., 06.07.2021. and 17.12.2020., which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

### 319.pants. Inaction of a State Official

(1) For the failure by a state official to fulfil their duties, that is, where the state official intentionally or through negligence fails to perform actions which they must perform according to law or an assigned task, and if substantial harm has been caused thereby, — shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of up to three years, or by short-term imprisonment, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2) For the actions specified in the first paragraph of this Section, if they are committed with mercenary intent, — shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of up to four years, or by short-term imprisonment, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine, with deprivation of the right to hold a certain position for a term of up to five years.

(3) For the failure by a state official to fulfil their duties, if serious consequences have been caused thereby, — shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of up to five years, or by short-term imprisonment, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine, with deprivation of the right to hold a certain position for a term of up to five years.

(4) For the failure by a state official to fulfil their duties, if the death of two or more persons has occurred as a result thereof, — shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of up to eight years, with deprivation of the right to hold a certain position for a term of up to five years.

(With amendments made by the laws of 12.02.2004., 13.12.2012., 25.09.2014., 29.10.2015., 06.06.2019., 06.07.2021. and 17.12.2020., which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

### 320.pants. Acceptance of a Bribe

(1) For the demanding, extorting or accepting of a bribe, that is, material values, benefits of a material or other nature, committed by a state official personally or through an intermediary for any lawful or unlawful action already performed or inaction permitted, using their official position, regardless of whether the demanded, extorted, accepted or offered bribe is intended for this state official or any other person, — shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of up to five years, or by short-term imprisonment, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine, with deprivation of the right to a certain occupation or the right to hold a certain position for a term of up to two years.

(2) For the accepting of a bribe or an offer thereof, or for the demanding or extorting of a bribe, committed by a state official personally or through an intermediary before the performance or non-performance of any lawful or unlawful action, using their official position, regardless of whether the demanded, extorted, accepted or offered bribe is intended for this state official or any other person, — shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of up to eight years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property, and with deprivation of the right to a certain occupation or the right to hold a certain position for a term of up to five years.

(3) For the actions specified in the first or second paragraph of this Section, if they are committed on a large scale or if they are committed by a group of persons pursuant to prior agreement, or if the bribe has been accepted following its demanding, — shall be punished by imprisonment for a term from two to ten years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property, and with deprivation of the right to a certain occupation or the right to hold a certain position for a term of up to five years.

(4) For the actions specified in the first or second paragraph of this Section, if they are committed by an organised group or by a state official who holds a responsible position, or if the bribe has been accepted following its extortion, — shall be punished by imprisonment for a term from three to eleven years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property, with deprivation of the right to a certain occupation or the right to hold a certain position for a term of up to five years, and with probation supervision for a term of up to three years.

(In the wording of the law of 19.11.2009. with amendments made by the laws of 08.07.2011., 13.12.2012., 08.06.2017. and 17.12.2020., which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

### 321.pants. Misappropriation of a Bribe

(1) For misappropriation of a bribe received by a person for transfer to a public official or received by pretending to be a public official, — is punishable by deprivation of liberty for a period of up to three years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property.

(2) For misappropriation of a bribe received by a public official for transfer to another public official or received by pretending to be another public official, — is punishable by deprivation of liberty for a period of up to four years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property.

(3) For the criminal offence provided for in paragraph one or two of this Section, if committed by a group of persons pursuant to prior agreement or if committed on a large scale, — is punishable by deprivation of liberty for a period of up to five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property.

(4) For the criminal offence provided for in paragraph one or two of this Section, if committed by an organised group, or for the criminal offence provided for in paragraph two of this Section, if committed by a public official holding a position of responsibility, — is punishable by deprivation of liberty for a period of up to seven years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property, and with probation supervision for a period of up to three years or without it.

(With amendments made by the laws of 12.02.2004., 13.12.2012., 08.06.2017. and 17.12.2020., which enter into force

### 322.pants. Intermediation in Bribery

(Deleted by the law of 07.04.2022., which entered into force on 04.05.2022.)

### 323.pants. Bribery

(1) For giving, offering or promising a bribe, that is, material values, benefits of a property or other nature, following its solicitation, personally or through an intermediary, to a public official, so that by using their official position they perform or refrain from performing an action, regardless of whether the bribe given, offered or promised is intended for that public official or any other person, — is punishable by deprivation of liberty for a period of up to five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2) For the same acts, if committed on a large scale or if committed by a public official, or if committed by a group of persons pursuant to prior agreement, — is punishable by deprivation of liberty for a period of up to eight years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property and with deprivation of the right to engage in specific employment or the right to hold a specific position for a period of up to five years.

(3) For the acts provided for in paragraph one of this Section, if committed by an organised group,— is punishable by deprivation of liberty for a period from two to ten years, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property, with deprivation of the right to engage in specific employment or the right to hold a specific position for a period of up to five years, and with probation supervision for a period of up to three years.

(In the wording of the law of 19.11.2009. with amendments made by the laws of 08.07.2011., 13.12.2012., 10.03.2016., 06.06.2019. and 17.12.2020., which entered into force on 01.01.2022.)

### 324.pants. Release of Bribe-Giver and Bribery Intermediary from Criminal Liability

(1) A person who has given a bribe may be released from criminal liability if the bribe was extorted from that person or if they voluntarily report the incident after giving the bribe and actively facilitate the detection and investigation of the criminal offence. A person who has promised or offered a bribe may be released from criminal liability if they voluntarily report the incident and actively facilitate the detection and investigation of the criminal offence.

(2) Extortion of a bribe means its solicitation for performing a lawful act, as well as solicitation associated with threats to harm the person's lawful interests.

(3) An intermediary or facilitator in bribery may be released from criminal liability if they voluntarily report the incident after commission of the criminal acts and actively facilitate the detection and investigation of the criminal offence.

(With amendments made by the laws of 25.04.2002., 13.12.2012. and 10.03.2016., which entered into force on 07.04.2016.)

### 325.pants. Violation of Restrictions Established for Public Officials

### 325.pants. Violation of Restrictions Established for Public Officials

(1) For violation of restrictions or prohibitions established by law for a public official, if it has caused substantial harm, — is punishable by deprivation of liberty for a period of up to three years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2) For the criminal offence provided for in paragraph one of this Section, if committed by a public official holding a position of responsibility, — is punishable by deprivation of liberty for a period of up to five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property and with deprivation of the right to engage in specific employment or the right to hold a specific position for a period of up to five years.

(With amendments made by the laws of 17.10.2002.,12.02.2004., 19.11.2009., 13.12.2012., 17.12.2020. and 07.04.2022., which entered into force on 04.05.2022.)


This excerpt sits under: Izdevējs: Saeima Publicēts: > XXIV nodaļa

### 326.Section. Unauthorized Participation in Property Transactions

(1) For facilitating or participating in a property transaction, if committed by a state official with mercenary intent or due to other personal interest, for whom this is prohibited by law in connection with their official position, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to one year or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2) For the same acts, if committed by a state official who holds a responsible position, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to three years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine, with confiscation of property or without confiscation of property and with deprivation of the right to engage in certain activities or the right to hold a certain position for a term of up to five years.

(As amended by the laws of 12.02.2004., 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

326.1 Section. Trading in Influence

(1) For offering or giving material values, benefits of a property or other nature, personally or through an intermediary, to any person, so that they, using their official, professional or social position, would unlawfully influence the actions of a state official or would induce another person to unlawfully influence the actions of a state official, regardless of whether the material values, benefits of a property or other nature are intended for that person or any other person, if there are no signs of the crime provided for in Section 323 of this Law, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to three years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2) For accepting material values, benefits of a property or other nature, or their offer, or for demanding material values, benefits of a property or other nature for oneself or any other person, so that they, using their official, professional or social position, would unlawfully influence the actions of a state official or would induce another person to unlawfully influence the actions of a state official or decision-making, if there are no signs of the crime provided for in Sections 198 and 320 of this Law, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine, with deprivation of the right to engage in certain activities or the right to hold a certain position for a term of up to five years.

(As worded by the law of 19.11.2009., as amended by the laws of 13.12.2012., 06.06.2019. and 17.12.2020., which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

326.2 Section. Unlawful Demanding and Accepting of Benefits

(1) For unlawful acceptance of material values, benefits of a property or other nature, or their offer, which has been committed personally or through an intermediary by an employee of a state or local government institution who is not a state official, or an authorized person of the same kind of a state institution, for performing or not performing any action, using their powers, regardless of whether the accepted material values, benefits of a property or other nature are intended for that person or any other person, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to three years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2) For the same acts, if they are committed on a large scale or if they have been committed by a group of persons pursuant to a prior agreement, or if they are related to demanding or extorting material values, benefits of a property or other nature, shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine, with deprivation of the right to engage in certain activities or the right to hold a certain position for a term of up to five years.

(As worded by the law of 19.11.2009., as amended by the laws of 13.12.2012., 06.06.2019. and 17.12.2020., which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

326.3 Section. Unlawful Giving of Benefits

(1) For offering or giving material values, benefits of a property or other nature, personally or through an intermediary, to an employee of a state or local government institution who is not a state official, or to an authorized person of the same kind of a state institution, so that they would perform any unlawful action, using their powers, regardless of whether the material values, benefits of a property or other nature are intended for that or any other person, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to one year or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision or by community service, or by a fine.

(2) For the same acts, if they are committed on a large scale, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to two years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(As worded by the law of 13.12.2012., as amended by the laws of 06.06.2019., 11.11.2021. and 17.12.2020., which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

A person who has unlawfully given a benefit may be released from criminal liability if the benefit was demanded from or extorted from that person, or if they voluntarily report what has occurred after the commission of the criminal acts and actively facilitate the detection and investigation of the criminal offense. A person who has offered a benefit may be released from criminal liability if they voluntarily report what has occurred and actively facilitate the detection and investigation of the criminal offense.

(As worded by the law of 13.12.2012., which enters into force on 01.04.2013.)

### 327.Section. Official Forgery

(1) For forging a document or for issuing or using a forged document, being aware that the document is forged, if committed by a state official, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to two years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2) For the same acts, if they are committed with mercenary intent or if they have been committed by a group of persons pursuant to a prior agreement, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to four years or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine, with deprivation of the right to hold a certain position for a term of up to five years.

(As worded by the law of 15.05.2014., as amended by the law of 17.12.2020., which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

### 328.Section. False Official Report

For deliberately providing false information to an institution or state official who has the right to request this information, as well as for concealing a document or information or deliberate non-disclosure, if committed by a state official who has the duty to provide this information, and if substantial harm has been caused thereby, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to one year or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(As amended by the laws of 12.02.2004., 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

### 329.Section. Disclosure of Non-Disclosable Information

For disclosure of such non-disclosable information that is not confidential, secret or top secret information, if committed by a state official who has been warned about non-disclosure of the information or who is responsible under law for keeping the information, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to one year or by short-term deprivation of liberty, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(As worded by the law of 26.05.2005., as amended by the laws of 13.12.2012., 10.03.2016., 17.12.2020. and 20.04.2023., which enters into force on 01.05.2023.)

### 330.pants. Disclosure of Non-Disclosable Information After Leaving Office

For disclosure of such non-disclosable information that is not confidential, secret or top secret information, if a person has done so after leaving the service of a state official during the period specified for them in a warning about non-disclosure of information, — shall be punished with probation supervision or with community service or with a fine.

(With amendments made by the laws of 13.12.2012., 17.12.2020. and 20.04.2023., which enter into force on 01.05.2023.)

## XXV nodaļa

Criminal Offenses in Military Service

### 331.pants. Concept of Criminal Offense and Its Subjects in Military Service

Criminal offenses in military service shall be recognized as the criminal offenses mentioned in this law directed against the established procedure for performance of military service, which have been committed by soldiers and persons to whom special provisions provided for in laws apply.

(In the wording of the law of 17.10.2002., which enters into force on 20.11.2002.)

### 332.pants. Unauthorized Absence

For unauthorized absence, that is, leaving a unit or place of service of the National Armed Forces, as well as failure to arrive without justifiable reason at the specified time at the place of service, if the unauthorized absence has lasted more than one day but not longer than three days and if it has been committed during a state of war or emergency, in combat conditions, during an emergency situation declared in the event of public disorder, terrorism or armed conflict, — shall be punished with deprivation of liberty for a term of up to two years or with short-term deprivation of liberty, or with probation supervision, or with community service, or with a fine.

(In the wording of the law of 14.12.2006. with amendments made by the laws of 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which enters into force

### 333.pants. Desertion

For desertion, that is, leaving the place of service or failure to arrive at the place of service with the intent to evade active service, if it has been committed during a state of war or emergency, in combat conditions, during an emergency situation declared in the event of public disorder, terrorism or armed conflict, — shall be punished with deprivation of liberty for a term of up to four years or with short-term deprivation of liberty, or with probation supervision, or with community service, or with a fine.

(In the wording of the law of 14.12.2006. with amendments made by the laws of 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which enters into force

### 334.pants. Evasion of Active Service

(1) For evasion of the performance of active service duties by inflicting some bodily injury on oneself (self-mutilation) or simulating illness, falsifying documents or otherwise deceiving, as well as for refusal to perform service duties — shall be punished with short-term deprivation of liberty or with probation supervision, or with community service, or with a fine.

(2) For the same offense, if it has been committed during wartime or in combat conditions, — shall be punished with deprivation of liberty for a term of up to four years or with short-term deprivation of liberty, or with probation supervision, or with community service, or with a fine.

(With amendments made by the laws of 14.12.2006., 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

### 335.pants. Insubordination

(1) For insubordination, that is, for open refusal to execute a superior's order, as well as for other intentional failure to execute an order, if it has caused substantial harm, — shall be punished with deprivation of liberty for a term of up to one year or with short-term deprivation of liberty, or with probation supervision, or with community service, or with a fine.

(2) For insubordination, as well as for other intentional failure to execute an order, if it has been committed by a group of persons or if it has caused serious consequences, — shall be punished with deprivation of liberty for a term of up to three years or with short-term deprivation of liberty, or with probation supervision, or with community service, or with a fine.

(3) For insubordination, as well as for other intentional failure to execute an order during wartime or in combat conditions — shall be punished with deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or with short-term deprivation of liberty, or with probation supervision, or with community service, or with a fine.

(In the wording of the law of 13.12.2012. with amendments made by the law of 17.12.2020., which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

### 336.pants. Failure to Execute an Order

For failure to execute a superior's order due to negligence, if it has caused serious consequences, — shall be punished with deprivation of liberty for a term of up to two years or with short-term deprivation of liberty, or with probation supervision, or with community service, or with a fine.

(With amendments made by the laws of 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

### 337.pants. Resistance to a Superior and Compelling Him to Violate Service Duties

(1) For resistance to a superior, as well as to another person in connection with the performance of military service duties imposed on them or for compelling him to violate these duties, if it has been committed with violence or if it has caused serious consequences, — shall be punished with deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or with short-term deprivation of liberty, or with probation supervision, or with community service, or with a fine.

(2) (Deleted by the law of 13.12.2012.)

(With amendments made by the laws of 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

### 338.pants. Violence Against a Subordinate

(1) For violence against a subordinate, if as a result thereof physical suffering has been inflicted on him, — shall be punished with short-term deprivation of liberty or with probation supervision, or with community service, or with a fine.

(2) For intentional infliction of light bodily injury on a subordinate — shall be punished with deprivation of liberty for a term of up to one year or with short-term deprivation of liberty, or with probation supervision, or with community service, or with a fine.

(3) For intentional infliction of moderate bodily injury on a subordinate, as well as for other acts having the character of torture, — shall be punished with deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or with short-term deprivation of liberty, or with probation supervision, or with community service, or with a fine.

(4) For intentional infliction of serious bodily injury on a subordinate — shall be punished with deprivation of liberty for a term from three to twelve years.

(With amendments made by the laws of 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

### 339.pants. Offense to the Honor of a Soldier

(Deleted by the law of 13.12.2012., which enters into force on 01.04.2013.)

### 340.pants. Beating and Torture of a Soldier

(1) For intentional striking or beating, as well as for other intentional violence committed by a soldier against a soldier, if at least one of them is performing military service duties, — shall be punished with short-term deprivation of liberty or with probation supervision, or with community service, or with a fine.

(2) For the same acts, if they are connected with intentional infliction of light bodily injury, — shall be punished with deprivation of liberty for a term of up to one year or with short-term deprivation of liberty, or with probation supervision, or with community service, or with a fine.

(3) For acts provided for in the first paragraph of this Article, if they are connected with intentional infliction of moderate bodily injury or if they have the character of torture, — shall be punished with deprivation of liberty for a term of up to five years or with short-term deprivation of liberty, or with probation supervision, or with community service, or with a fine.

(4) For acts provided for in the first paragraph of this Article, if they are connected with intentional infliction of serious bodily injury, shall be punished with deprivation of liberty for a term from three to twelve years.

(With amendments made by the laws of 17.10.2002., 13.12.2012. and 17.12.2020., which enters into force on 01.01.2022.)

### 341.pants. Abuse of Power and Exceeding Official Authority

(1) For abuse of power or official position or exceeding official authority committed by a superior, if this offense is committed for mercenary purposes or if substantial harm is caused, — shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of up to five years or by short-term imprisonment, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2) For abuse of power or official position or exceeding official authority committed by a superior, if this offense has caused serious consequences, — shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of up to seven years.

(As amended by the laws of 13.12.2012 and 17.12.2020, which enter into force on 01.01.2022.)

### 342.pants. Official Negligence

(1) For failure to perform official duties, as well as for negligent performance thereof, if as a result substantial harm is caused, shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of up to three years or by short-term imprisonment, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2) For failure to perform official duties, as well as for negligent performance thereof, if it has caused serious consequences, — shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of up to five years or by short-term imprisonment, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(As amended by the laws of 13.12.2012 and 17.12.2020, which enter into force on 01.01.2022.)

### 343.pants. Squandering and Loss of Official Property

(1) (Deleted by the law of 13.12.2012)

(2) For the sale of weapons, ammunition, means of transportation, military service equipment entrusted for official use or for the loss of these items by violating the rules for their storage, — shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of up to three years or by short-term imprisonment, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(As amended by the laws of 17.10.2002, 13.12.2012 and 17.12.2020, which enter into force on 01.01.2022.)

### 344.pants. Intentional Destruction and Damage of Military Service Property

(1) For intentional destruction or damage of weapons, ammunition, means of transportation, military service equipment — shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of up to three years or by short-term imprisonment, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2) For the same acts, if they have caused substantial harm, — shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of up to five years or by short-term imprisonment, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(As amended by the laws of 13.12.2012 and 17.12.2020, which enter into force on 01.01.2022.)

### 345.pants. Destruction and Damage of Military Service Property by Negligence

For destruction or damage of weapons, ammunition, means of transportation or military service equipment by negligence, if it has caused substantial harm, — shall be punished by short-term imprisonment or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(As amended by the laws of 13.12.2012 and 17.12.2020, which enter into force on 01.01.2022.)

### 346.pants.

Violation of Rules for Storage, Use, Accounting and Transportation of Weapons, Ammunition, Explosives, Radioactive and Dangerous Substances

(1) For violation of the rules for storage, use, accounting or transportation of weapons, ammunition, explosives, radioactive substances, as well as other dangerous substances, items or materials, if it has caused substantial harm, — shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of up to two years or by short-term imprisonment, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2) For the same offense, if it has caused serious consequences, — shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of up to five years or by short-term imprisonment, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(As amended by the laws of 13.12.2012 and 17.12.2020, which enter into force on 01.01.2022.)

### 347.pants. Violation of Rules for Operating and Exploiting Combat Vehicles

For violation of the rules for operating or exploiting a combat vehicle, if it has caused the death of a person or other serious consequences, — shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of up to seven years.

(As amended by the law of 13.12.2012, which enters into force on 01.04.2013.)

### 348.pants. Violation of Rules for Operating Vessels

For violation of the rules for operating a vessel, if it has caused the destruction of the vessel or other serious consequences, — shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of up to ten years.

(As amended by the law of 13.12.2012, which enters into force on 01.04.2013.)

### 349.pants. Violation of Rules for Flights and Their Preparation

For violation of the rules for a flight or its preparation, if it has caused a catastrophe or other serious consequences, — shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of up to ten years.

(As amended by the law of 13.12.2012, which enters into force on 01.04.2013.)

### 350.pants. Violation of Guard (Security) Regulations

(1) For violation of guard (security) regulations or orders or instructions issued for the application of these regulations, committed while on guard or at posts, guarding ammunition, weapons, fuel, equipment warehouses or other facilities of important significance, — shall be punished by short-term imprisonment or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(2) For the same offense, if it has caused serious consequences, — shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of up to three years or by short-term imprisonment, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(As amended by the laws of 13.12.2012 and 17.12.2020, which enter into force on 01.01.2022.)

### 351.pants. Violation of Internal Service Regulations, Instructions or Orders

(1) (Deleted by the law of 13.12.2012)

(2) For violation of internal service regulations, instructions or orders, if committed by a serviceman assigned to 24-hour duty (except guard), and if it has caused serious consequences, — shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of up to five years or by short-term imprisonment, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(As amended by the laws of 17.10.2002, 13.12.2012 and 17.12.2020, which enter into force on 01.01.2022.)

### 352.pants. Disclosure of Military Information

For disclosure of such non-disclosable military information that is not confidential, secret or top secret information, — shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of up to one year or by short-term imprisonment, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(As amended by the laws of 13.12.2012, 10.03.2016, 17.12.2020 and 20.04.2023, which enters into force

### 353.pants. Surrender and Abandonment of Means of Warfare to the Enemy

For surrender to the enemy of the entrusted military forces, as well as for abandonment to the enemy of fortifications, combat equipment or other means of warfare, when not justified by combat circumstances, commanders — shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of three to ten years.

(As amended by the law of 13.12.2012, which enters into force on 01.04.2013.)

### 354.pants. Unauthorized Abandonment of the Battlefield and Refusal to Use Weapons

For unauthorized abandonment of the battlefield during combat or refusal to use weapons in combat — shall be punished by imprisonment for a term of up to five years or by short-term imprisonment, or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine.

(As amended by the laws of 13.12.2012 and 17.12.2020, which enter into force on 01.01.2022.)

### 355.pants. Criminal Conduct of a Serviceman in Captivity

(1) For violence against other prisoners of war or cruel treatment of them, if committed by a prisoner of war who holds a senior position, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to seven years.

(2) For intentional action of a serviceman in captivity directed against the Republic of Latvia, the purpose of which is to support the enemy or assist the enemy, — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a term of up to ten years.

(With amendments made by the laws of 17.10.2002. and 13.12.2012., which enter into force on 01.04.2013.)

### 356.pants. Illegal Wearing and Malicious Use of the Red Cross, Red Crescent and Blue White Shield Symbols

For illegal wearing of the Red Cross, Red Crescent or Blue White Shield symbols in areas of military operations, if committed by a person who has no right thereto, as well as for malicious use of the Blue White Shield symbols or of the Red Cross or Red Crescent flag or symbols, or of the designated colouring of medical evacuation vehicles during wartime — shall be punished by deprivation of liberty for a short term or by probation supervision, or by community service, or by a fine. (As amended by the Law of 18 December 2003, as amended by the Laws of 13 December 2012 and 17 December 2020, which comes into force Transitional Provisions

1. The time and procedure for entry into force of this Law shall be determined by a special law.

2. The Cabinet of Ministers shall, by 1 August 1998, develop and submit to the Saeima a draft law "On the Time and Procedure for Entry into Force of the Criminal Law".

3. The Cabinet of Ministers shall, by 1 August 1998, develop and adopt regulations:

- 1) on access to computer programs and information system security;
- 2) on the restriction of prostitution.
  - 4. Sections 282.1 and 282.2 of this Law shall enter into force on 1 December 2003.

(As amended by the Law of 17 October 2002, which entered into force on 20 November 2002.)

5. Section 195.2 of this Law shall enter into force on 1 July 2006.

(As amended by the Law of 8 December 2005, which entered into force on 5 January 2006.)

6. The amendment to paragraph four of Section 61 of this Law regarding the mandatory obligation to participate in probation programmes shall enter into force on 1 January 2013. With respect to persons who have been punished under Sections 159, 160, 161, 162, 162.1, 164,
