165 and 166 of this Law, the amendment to paragraph four of Section 61 of this Law regarding the mandatory obligation to participate in probation programmes shall enter into force on 1 October 2011.

(As amended by the Law of 30 October 2008, as amended by the Laws of 16 June 2009 and 8 July 2011, which enters into force

7. Sub-paragraphs 2.1 and 3 of paragraph four of Section 58.1 of this Law shall not be applied until 31 December 2012.

(As amended by the Law of 16 June 2009, which entered into force on 1 July 2009.)

8. The amendment to paragraph two of Section 65 of this Law regarding reduction of the term of deprivation of liberty and non-application of this type of punishment applies to minors who have committed a criminal offence after the entry into force of this amendment.

(As amended by the Law of 16 June 2009, which entered into force on 1 July 2009.)

9. The regulation provided for in paragraphs 1 and 2 of the transitional provisions of the Law "Amendments to the Criminal Law" adopted on 21 October 2010, in the part concerning criminal proceedings pending before courts, prosecutor's offices and pre-trial investigation institutions for criminal offences qualified under Section 149, shall be applicable only with respect to those criminal proceedings in which the criminal offence is qualified under paragraph one of Section 149 of the Criminal Law. In criminal cases pending before courts, prosecutor's offices and pre-trial investigation institutions for criminal offences committed before 31 December 2010 and qualified under paragraphs two, three and four of Section 149 of the Criminal Law, the qualification of the offence shall remain unchanged and punishment shall be applied to persons in accordance with the law that was in force until 31 December 2010.

(As amended by the Law of 2 December 2010, which entered into force on 1 January 2011.)

10. Amendments to sub-paragraph 5 of paragraph two of Section 36, Section 45, sub-paragraph 3 of paragraph one of Section 52 and paragraph five of Section 55 of this Law regarding the elimination of the additional punishment — police supervision — shall enter into force on 1 January 2015.

(As amended by the Law of 8 July 2011, which entered into force on 1 October 2011.)

11. Amendments to paragraph three of Section 98, paragraph four of Section 99, Sections 116, 117, 118, 125, paragraph three of Section 148, paragraph three of Section 152, paragraph three of Section 153, paragraph three of Section 154, paragraph three of Section 154.1, paragraph three of Section 165.1, Section 174, paragraph four of Section 175, Section 176, paragraph three of Section 177, paragraph three of Section 177.1, paragraph two of Section 183, Section 184, paragraph two of Section 187, paragraph three of Section 190.1, paragraph two of Section 192, paragraph four of Section 193, paragraph three of Section 193.1, paragraph three of Section 195, paragraph three of Section 206, paragraph three of Section 218, paragraph three of Section 220.1, paragraph three of Section 221, paragraph three of Section 221.1, Sections 224, 225, paragraph two of Section 231, paragraph three of Section 233, paragraph three of Section 243, Sections 253, 253.1, paragraph two of Section 269, paragraph three of Section 285, Section 311, paragraph three of Section 314, paragraph four of Section 320, paragraph three of Section 323 of this Law regarding the replacement of the additional punishment — police supervision — with the additional punishment — probation supervision — shall enter into force on 1 January 2015.

(As amended by the Law of 14 March 2013, which entered into force on 1 April 2013.)

12. Additional punishment — police supervision — shall continue to be applied to persons who have committed the criminal offence provided for in Sections 159, 160, 164, 165 and 166 of this Law before 30 September 2011, and to persons who have committed another criminal offence provided for in a section of the special part of this Law before 31 December 2014.

(As amended by the Law of 8 July 2011, which entered into force on 1 October 2011.)

12.1 As of 1 January 2022, for persons referred to in paragraph 12 of these transitional provisions who have not yet been convicted, the additional punishment — probation supervision — shall be applied instead of the additional punishment — police supervision —, calculating two days of police supervision as one day of probation supervision.

(As amended by the Law of 17 December 2020, which entered into force on 1 January 2022.)

13. Additional punishment — police supervision — shall continue to be served by persons to whom it has been imposed, and the following conditions for execution of police supervision shall apply to them:

- 1) if a person has been conditionally released before term from serving punishment, execution of the additional punishment — police supervision — shall commence from the moment when the person's supervision following conditional early release has ended;
- 2) the court, upon the submission of a police institution, may reduce the term of police supervision or cancel police supervision;
- 3) if a convicted person has committed a new crime during the serving of the additional punishment, the court shall replace the unserved part of the additional punishment with deprivation of liberty and determine the final punishment in accordance with the provisions of Section 51 of this Law, calculating two days of police supervision as one day of deprivation of liberty;
- 4) if a person to whom police supervision has been imposed by court judgment maliciously violates the provisions thereof, the court, upon the submission of a police institution, may replace the unserved part of the additional punishment, calculating two days of police supervision as one day of deprivation of liberty. Violation of police supervision provisions is malicious if a person has been administratively punished twice within a year for such violation.

(As amended by the Law of 8 July 2011, as amended by the Law of 16 October 2014, which entered into force on 1 February 2015.)

14. Additional punishment — probation supervision — shall begin to be applied as of 1 October 2011 to persons who have committed the criminal offence provided for in Sections 159, 160, 161, 162, 162.1, 164, 165 and 166 of this Law after 30 September 2011, and as of 1 January 2015 — to persons who have committed another criminal offence provided for in a section of the special part of this Law after 31 December 2014.

(As amended by the Law of 29 October 2015, which entered into force on 3 December 2015.)

14.1 When determining punishment for several criminal offences in accordance with Section 50 of the Criminal Law or under several judgments in accordance with Section 51 of the Criminal Law and finding that both police supervision and probation supervision have been imposed on a person, police supervision shall be incorporated into probation supervision regardless of the term imposed for police supervision. If probation supervision has been imposed in accordance with paragraph 12.1 of these transitional provisions, then it shall be incorporated into probation supervision that has been imposed in accordance with paragraph 14 of these transitional provisions.

(As amended by the Law of 10 March 2016, as amended by the Law of 17 December 2020, which entered into force on 1 January 2022.)

15. Persons whose death penalty has been replaced with deprivation of liberty shall be subject to those provisions of this Law that apply to persons who have been sentenced to life imprisonment.

(As amended by the Law of 1 December 2011, which entered into force on 1 January 2012.)

16. Amendments to paragraph two of Section 78, Sections 80, 80.1, 82, 86, 87, 88, 88.2, 88.3, 89.1 and 107, paragraph two of Section 124, paragraph two of Section 126, paragraph two of Section 152, paragraphs one and two of Section 153, paragraphs one and two of Section 154, paragraph two of Section 154.1, paragraph two of Section 165.1, paragraph three of Section 179, paragraph one of Section 183, paragraph two of Section 185, paragraph three of Section 190, paragraph three of Section 191, Section 230, paragraphs two and three of Section 251, paragraph three of Section 252, Section 268, paragraphs two and three of Section 309 and paragraph two of Section 310 of this Law regarding additional punishment — probation supervision — shall enter into force on 1 January 2015.

(As amended by the Law of 13 December 2012, which entered into force on 1 April 2013.)

17. The legal regulation regarding full or partial cancellation of an imposed obligation, execution of the punishment imposed in the judgment or extension of the probation period for a conditionally convicted person, which was in force until 31 January 2015, shall be applicable with respect to persons who were conditionally convicted until 31 January 2015.

(As amended by the Law of 16 October 2014, which entered into force on 1 February 2015.)

18. The legal regulation regarding execution of the unserved part of punishment for a person conditionally released before term, which was in force until 31 January 2015, shall be applicable with respect to persons who were conditionally released from serving punishment before term until 31 January 2015.

(As amended by the Law of 16 October 2014, which entered into force on 1 February 2015.)

19. Paragraphs 2.1, 3.1, 4.1 and 4.2 of Section 61, Section 61.1 and paragraph 3.1 of Section 65 of this Law shall enter into force on 1 July 2015.

(As amended by the Law of 15 January 2015, which entered into force on 1 February 2015.)

20. Amendments to the title of Section 84, the disposition and sanction of paragraph one of this Law shall enter into force simultaneously with the Law on International and National Sanctions of the Republic of Latvia.

(As amended by the Law of 28 January 2016, which entered into force on 1 March 2016. The said amendments are incorporated into the version of the law on

21. The provisions of Chapter VIII2 of this Law, with respect to those criminal offences committed before 31 July 2017,

31 July, shall be applicable insofar as these provisions are not less favourable than those provisions of the Criminal Procedure Law which regulated the handling of criminally acquired property at the time of the commission of the respective criminal offence.

(As amended by the Law of 22 June 2017, which entered into force on 1 August 2017.)

22. Persons who have committed the criminal offences provided for in Sections 88, 88.1, 88.2 or 88.3 of this Law before the date when the amendment regarding the elimination of these sections entered into force shall be prosecuted and punished in accordance with those provisions of this Law that were in force at the time of the commission of the respective offence.

(As amended by the Law of 26 April 2018, which entered into force on 23 May 2018.)

23. The amendment to the disposition of paragraph one of Section 300 of this Law shall enter into force simultaneously with the Law on Administrative Liability.

(As amended by the Law of 6 June 2019, which entered into force on 3 July 2019.)

24. The new name "community service" for the type of punishment — forced labour — may be used when making decisions on the application of punishment also for such criminal offences that were committed before 31 December 2021.

(As amended by the Law of 17 December 2020, which entered into force on 1 January 2022.)

25. Principal punishment — probation supervision — shall begin to be applied as of 1 January 2022 to adults who have committed the criminal offence provided for in Sections 126, 130, 130.1, 174, 248.2, 253.2 and 340 of this Law after 31 December 2021, and to minors who have committed any criminal offence provided for in the special part of this Law after 31 December 2021.

(As amended by the Law of 17 December 2020, which entered into force on 1 January 2022.)

25.1 Principal punishment — probation supervision — shall begin to be applied as of 15 July 2023 to adults who have committed the criminal offence provided for in Sections 132, 132.1 and 168.1 of this Law after 14 July 2023.

(As amended by the Law of 15 June 2023, which entered into force on 15 July 2023.)

25.2 Principal punishment — probation supervision — shall begin to be applied as of 20 October 2023 to adults who have committed the criminal offence provided for in Sections 285, 285.1 and 285.2 of this Law after 19 October 2023.

(As amended by the Law of 5 October 2023, which entered into force on 20 October 2023.)

26. Amendments to paragraph one of Section 41, the sanction of paragraph two of Section 139, the sanction of paragraphs one and two of Section 139.1, the sanction of paragraph 2.1 of Section 141, the sanction of paragraph 2.1 of Section 195.2, the sanction of Section 221.6 and the sanction of paragraph one of Section 239.1 of this Law regarding principal punishment — community service and probation supervision — shall enter into force on 1 January 2022.

(As amended by the Law of 6 July 2021, which entered into force on 5 August 2021.)

27. Until 31 December 2021, the type of punishment — forced labour — shall be applicable in place of the type of punishment — community service — provided for in Section 218.1 of this Law.

(As amended by the Law of 6 July 2021, which entered into force on 5 August 2021.)

28. Persons who have committed the criminal offence provided for in Section 322 of this Law before the date when the amendment regarding the elimination of this section entered into force shall be prosecuted and punished in accordance with those provisions of this Law that were in force at the time of the commission of the respective offence.

(As amended by the Law of 7 April 2022, which entered into force on 4 May 2022.)

29. Paragraph two of Section 66.2 of this Law shall not be applied from 1 July 2022 until 31 December 2024.

(As amended by the Law of 16 June 2022, which entered into force on 23 June 2022.)

Informative Reference to European Union Directives

(As amended by the Law of 21 October 2010, as amended by the Laws of 16 June 2011, 13 December 2012, 15 May 2014, 25 September 2014, 16 October 2014, 28 January 2016, 22 June 2017, 26 April 2018, 6 June 2019 and 6 July 2021, which enters into force The Law incorporates legal provisions arising from:

- 1) Council Directive 79/409/EEC of 2 April 1979 on the conservation of wild birds;
- 2) Council Directive 91/308/EEC of 10 June 1991 on prevention of the use of the financial system for the purpose of money laundering;
- 3) Council Directive 91/477/EEC of 18 June 1991 on control of the acquisition and possession of weapons;
- 4) Council Directive 92/43/EEC of 21 May 1992 on the conservation of natural habitats and of wild fauna and flora;
- 5) Council Directive 93/15/EEC of 5 April 1993 on the harmonization of the provisions relating to the placing on the market and supervision of explosives for civil uses;
- 6) Directive 95/46/EC of the European Parliament and of the Council of 24 October 1995 on the protection of individuals with regard to the processing of personal data and on the free movement of such data;
- 7) Council Directive 2000/43/EC of 29 June 2000 implementing the principle of equal treatment between persons irrespective of racial or ethnic origin;
- 8) Directive 2000/59/EC of the European Parliament and of the Council of 27 November 2000 on port reception facilities for ship-generated waste and cargo residues;
- 9) Directive 2001/97/EC of the European Parliament and of the Council of 4 December 2001 amending Council Directive 91/308/EEC on prevention of the use of the financial system for the purpose of money laundering;
- 10) Directive 2005/35/EC of the European Parliament and of the Council of 7 September 2005 on ship-source pollution and on the introduction of penalties for infringements;
- 11) Directive 2006/24/EC of the European Parliament and of the Council of 15 March 2006 on the retention of data generated or processed in connection with the provision of publicly available electronic communications services or of public communications networks and amending Directive 2002/58/EC;
- 12) Directive 2008/99/EC of the European Parliament and of the Council of 19 November 2008 on the protection of the environment through criminal law;
- 13) Directive 2009/123/EC of the European Parliament and of the Council of 21 October 2009 amending Directive 2005/35/EC on ship-source pollution and on the introduction of penalties for infringements;
- 14) Directive 2009/52/EC of the European Parliament and of the Council of 18 June 2009 providing for minimum standards on sanctions and measures against employers of illegally staying third-country nationals;
- 15) Directive 2011/36/EU of the European Parliament and of the Council of 5 April 2011 on preventing and combating trafficking in human beings and protecting its victims, and replacing Council Framework Decision 2002/629/JHA;
- 16) Directive 2011/93/EU of the European Parliament and of the Council of 13 December 2011 on combating the sexual abuse and sexual exploitation of children and child pornography, and replacing Council Framework Decision 2004/68/JHA;
- 17) Directive 2013/40/EU of the European Parliament and of the Council of 12 August 2013 on attacks against information systems and replacing Council Framework Decision 2005/222/JHA;
- 18) Directive 2004/38/EC of the European Parliament and of the Council of 29 April 2004 on the right of citizens of the Union and their family members to move and reside freely within the territory of the Member States amending Regulation (EEC) No 1612/68 and repealing Directives 64/221/EEC, 68/360/EEC, 72/194/EEC, 73/148/EEC, 75/34/EEC, 75/35/EEC, 90/364/EEC, 90/365/EEC and 93/96/EEC;
- 19) Directive 2014/57/EU of the European Parliament and of the Council of 16 April 2014 on criminal sanctions for market abuse (market abuse directive);
- 20) Directive 2014/62/EU of the European Parliament and of the Council of 15 May 2014 on the protection of the euro and other currencies against counterfeiting by criminal law, and replacing Council Framework Decision 2000/383/JHA;
- 21) Directive 2014/42/EU of the European Parliament and of the Council of 3 April 2014 on the freezing and confiscation of instrumentalities and proceeds of crime in the European Union;
- 22) Directive (EU) 2017/541 of the European Parliament and of the Council of 15 March 2017 on combating terrorism and replacing Council Framework Decision 2002/475/JHA and amending Council Decision 2005/671/JHA;
- 23) Directive (EU) 2017/1371 of the European Parliament and of the Council of 5 July 2017 on the fight against fraud to the Union's financial interests by means of criminal law;
- 24) Directive (EU) 2018/1673 of the European Parliament and of the Council of 23 October 2018 on combating money laundering by criminal law;
- 25) Directive (EU) 2019/713 of the European Parliament and of the Council of 17 April 2019 on combating fraud and counterfeiting of non-cash means of payment and replacing Council Framework Decision 2001/413/JHA.

The Law was adopted by the Saeima on 17 June 1998. President of the State G.Ulmanis
